### [1] [Auditing Preferences for Brands and Cultures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18300)
*Jasmine Rienecker,Katarina Mpofu,Naman Goel,Siddhartha Datta,Jun Zhao,Oscar Danielsson,Fredrik Thorsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ChoiceEval是一个用于审计大语言模型在品牌和文化偏好方面偏见的可重复框架，通过生成多样化的用户画像和查询，将自由形式输出转换为可比较的选择集和量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的AI系统越来越多地影响数十亿人的选择、购买和信息获取，迫切需要量化LLM驱动的市场中介的系统性风险，包括对市场公平性、竞争和信息多样性的影响。

Method: ChoiceEval框架通过两个核心技术解决：(1)生成基于心理画像（如预算意识型、健康导向型、便利型）的多样化评估查询；(2)将自由形式输出转换为标准化的top-k选择集和量化偏好指标。框架涵盖10个商业和文化主题，超过2000个问题。

Result: 应用ChoiceEval对Gemini、GPT和DeepSeek的审计显示：美国开发的模型（Gemini和GPT）对美国实体表现出明显偏好，而中国开发的DeepSeek则表现出更平衡但仍可检测的地理偏好。这些模式在不同用户画像中持续存在，表明是系统性而非偶然效应。

Conclusion: ChoiceEval为研究人员、平台和监管机构提供了一个可扩展的审计管道，将模型行为与现实世界的经济结果联系起来，揭示了LLM在品牌和文化偏好方面存在系统性偏见，需要进一步研究和监管干预。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) based AI systems increasingly mediate what billions of people see, choose and buy. This creates an urgent need to quantify the systemic risks of LLM-driven market intermediation, including its implications for market fairness, competition, and the diversity of information exposure.
  This paper introduces ChoiceEval, a reproducible framework for auditing preferences for brands and cultures in large language models (LLMs) under realistic usage conditions. ChoiceEval addresses two core technical challenges: (i) generating realistic, persona-diverse evaluation queries and (ii) converting free-form outputs into comparable choice sets and quantitative preference metrics. For a given topic (e.g. running shoes, hotel chains, travel destinations), the framework segments users into psychographic profiles (e.g., budget-conscious, wellness-focused, convenience), and then derives diverse prompts that reflect real-world advice-seeking and decision-making behaviour. LLM responses are converted into normalised top-k choice sets. Preference and geographic bias are then quantified using comparable metrics across topics and personas. Thus, ChoiceEval provides a scalable audit pipeline for researchers, platforms, and regulators, linking model behaviour to real-world economic outcomes.
  Applied to Gemini, GPT, and DeepSeek across 10 topics spanning commerce and culture and more than 2,000 questions, ChoiceEval reveals consistent preferences: U.S.-developed models Gemini and GPT show marked favouritism toward American entities, while China-developed DeepSeek exhibits more balanced yet still detectable geographic preferences. These patterns persist across user personas, suggesting systematic rather than incidental effects.

</details>


### [2] [PeriphAR: Fast and Accurate Real-World Object Selection with Peripheral Augmented Reality Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18350)
*Yutong Ren,Arnav Reddy,Michael Nebeling*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PeriphAR是一种利用外围视觉在单目AR显示器上为基于注视的选择提供反馈的可视化技术，通过增强颜色对比度来改善外围视觉中的目标选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XR中的注视选择需要视觉确认，而宽视场显示器使用的世界锁定中央覆盖层不适合始终开启的AR眼镜。需要一种利用外围视觉的反馈技术来改善注视选择体验。

Method: 1. 首次用户研究：分离文本、颜色和形状属性，比较外围选择线索；2. 开发两种增强外围视觉中颜色的策略；3. 第二次用户研究：评估颜色增强策略的主观偏好；4. 概念验证：在端到端系统中实现PeriphAR，使用真实世界物体检测测试性能。

Result: 1. 外围视觉对颜色比对形状更敏感，但在低对比度下敏感性迅速下降；2. 最大化目标与颜色最相似相邻对象对比度的策略主观上更受偏好；3. PeriphAR系统在实际物体检测中表现出可行性。

Conclusion: PeriphAR通过利用外围视觉和增强颜色对比度，为AR眼镜上的注视选择提供了一种有效的可视化技术，改善了选择性能并保持了前注意处理能力。

Abstract: Gaze-based selection in XR requires visual confirmation due to eye-tracking limitations and target ambiguity in 3D contexts. Current designs for wide-FOV displays use world-locked, central overlays, which are not conducive to always-on AR glasses. This paper introduces PeriphAR (per-ree-far), a visualization technique that leverages peripheral vision for feedback during gaze-based selection on a monocular AR display. In a first user study, we isolated text, color, and shape properties of target objects to compare peripheral selection cues. Peripheral vision was more sensitive to color than shape, but this sensitivity rapidly declined at lower contrast. To preserve preattentive processing of color, we developed two strategies to enhance color in users' peripheral vision. In a second user study, our strategy that maximized contrast of the target to the neighboring object with the most similar color was subjectively preferred. As proof of concept, we implemented PeriphAR in an end-to-end system to test performance with real-world object detection.

</details>


### [3] [Deconstructing Open-World Game Mission Design Formula: A Thematic Analysis Using an Action-Block Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18398)
*Kaijie Xu,Yiwei Zhang,Brian Yang,Clark Verbrugge*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出MAQV六维框架和动作块语法，通过LLM解析2200个AAA游戏任务，开发交互式仪表板分析任务设计模式，验证工具有效性并识别设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界游戏任务常依赖重复公式，但设计师缺乏系统方法来分析大规模任务组合的节奏、变化和体验平衡，需要数据驱动的分析工具支持。

Method: 引入任务动作质量向量（MAQV）六维框架和动作块语法，使用LLM辅助解析将社区攻略转换为结构化动作序列，基于20款AAA游戏的约2200个任务进行分析，开发交互式仪表板。

Result: 验证了管道的保真度和工具的可用性，通过主题分析识别出重复的设计权衡、节奏语法，以及不同任务类型和系列演变中的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 提供了可重复的分析工作流程、数据驱动的可视化工具和反思性见解，支持大规模下更平衡、多样化的任务设计。

Abstract: Open-world missions often rely on repeated formulas, yet designers lack systematic ways to examine pacing, variation, and experiential balance across large portfolios. We introduce the Mission Action Quality Vector (MAQV), a six-dimensional framework-covering combat, exploration, narrative, emotion, problem-solving, and uniqueness-paired with an action block grammar representing missions as gameplay sequences. Using about 2200 missions from 20 AAA titles, we apply LLM-assisted parsing to convert community walkthroughs into structured action sequences and score them with MAQV. An interactive dashboard enables designers to reveal underlying mission formulas. In a mixed-methods study with experienced players and designers, we validate the pipeline's fidelity and the tool's usability, and use thematic analysis to identify recurring design trade-offs, pacing grammars, and systematic differences by quest type and franchise evolution. Our work offers a reproducible analytical workflow, a data-driven visualization tool, and reflective insights to support more balanced, varied mission design at scale.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Ray-Casting: Evaluating Controller, Free-Hand, and Virtual-Touch Modalities for Immersive Text Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18435)
*Md. Tanvir Hossain,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Akif Islam,Md. Omar Faruqe,Mahboob Qaosar,A. F. M. Mahbubur Rahman,Sanjoy Kumar Chakravarty,M. Khademul Islam Molla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究比较了VR中六种物理输入系统和三种交互方式，发现控制器驱动的手势组合在生产力方面表现最佳，但虚拟触摸打字在主观体验上评分最高，揭示了VR文本输入中效率与自然交互之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的文本输入是VR成为生产力平台的主要瓶颈，需要实证比较不同输入系统的性能，为未来VR界面设计提供数据驱动的指导。

Method: 通过21名参与者实证比较六种物理输入系统和三种交互方式（控制器驱动、自由手、虚拟触摸），评估离散点击打字和连续手势打字（滑动），同时包含语音转文本作为非物理参考模态。

Result: 控制器驱动的手势组合（CD TGC）在生产力方面表现最佳，速度比最慢系统快2.25倍，比当前行业标准快30%，错误率降低高达68%。虚拟触摸打字在主观体验评分最高，系统可用性量表（SUS）得分比最低系统高80%。自由手交互受限于跟踪稳定性和物理疲劳，语音输入存在隐私、编辑控制和沉浸感等实际问题。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VR文本输入中吞吐量与自然交互之间的张力：控制器手势在性能上最优，但虚拟触摸在用户体验上更受欢迎。这些发现为未来VR界面设计提供了数据驱动的指导，需要在效率和可用性之间找到平衡。

Abstract: Efficient text entry remains a primary bottleneck preventing Virtual Reality (VR) from evolving into a viable productivity platform. To address this, we conducted an empirical comparison of six physical input systems across three interaction styles Controller Driven, Free Hand, and Virtual Touch evaluating both discrete tap typing and continuous gesture typing (swiping), alongside a speech to text (Voice) condition as a non physical reference modality. Results from 21 participants show that the Controller Driven Tap Gesture Combo (CD TGC) delivers the best productivity performance, achieving speeds 2.25 times higher than the slowest system and 30% faster than the current industry standard, while reducing error rates by up to 68%. A clear trade off emerged between performance and perceived usability: although controller based gesture input led on speed and accuracy, participants rated Virtual Touch Tap Typing highest in subjective experience, scoring 80% higher on the System Usability Scale (SUS) than the lowest rated alternative. We further observe that Free Hand interaction remains limited by tracking stability and physical fatigue, whereas Voice input introduces practical constraints related to privacy, editing control, and immersive engagement. Together, these findings characterize the tension between throughput and natural interaction in immersive text entry and provide data driven guidance for future VR interface design.

</details>


### [5] [Align-to-Scale: Mode Switching Technique for Unimanual 3D Object Manipulation with Gaze-Hand-Object Alignment in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18535)
*Min-yung Kim,Jinwook Kim,Ken Pfeuffer,Sang Ho Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在XR环境中实现单手缩放交互的新方法，通过视线与手部空间对齐作为模式切换，解决了现有Gaze+Pinch交互模型中缩放通常需要双手操作的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着XR技术日益普及，支持单手交互变得至关重要。当前主流的Gaze+Pinch交互模型虽然部分支持单手操作（选择、移动、旋转物体），但缩放通常需要双手操作，限制了交互的灵活性和移动性。

Method: 利用视线与手部空间对齐作为模式切换机制，实现单手捏合缩放功能。设计并评估了多种针对单手缩放的技术，并在复合平移-缩放任务中测试其可用性。

Result: 所有提出的方法都能有效实现单手缩放，但每种方法都有其独特的优势和权衡。研究为支持未来3D界面的单手交互提供了设计指南。

Conclusion: 该工作使XR中的眼手3D交互更加移动化、灵活化和可访问化，为未来3D界面设计提供了支持单手交互的设计指导原则。

Abstract: As extended reality (XR) technologies rapidly become as ubiquitous as today's mobile devices, supporting one-handed interaction becomes essential for XR. However, the prevalent Gaze + Pinch interaction model partially supports unimanual interaction, where users select, move, and rotate objects with one hand, but scaling typically requires both hands. In this work, we leverage the spatial alignment between gaze and hand as a mode switch to enable single-handed pinch-to-scale. We design and evaluate several techniques geared for one-handed scaling and assess their usability in a compound translate-scale task. Our findings show that all proposed methods effectively enable one-handed scaling, but each method offers distinct advantages and trade-offs. To this end, we derive design guidelines to support futuristic 3D interfaces with unimanual interaction. Our work helps make eye-hand 3D interaction in XR more mobile, flexible, and accessible.

</details>


### [6] [Cognitive Amplification vs Cognitive Delegation in Human-AI Systems: A Metric Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18677)
*Eduardo Di Santi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一个区分人机协作中认知放大与认知委托的概念框架，定义了量化指标来评估人机系统的协同性能与认知可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能越来越多地嵌入人类决策过程，需要区分AI是真正增强人类推理能力，还是导致人类过度依赖AI而丧失自身认知能力。当前缺乏系统性的框架来评估人机协作的认知可持续性。

Method: 提出了一个概念和数学框架，定义了四个操作指标：认知放大指数(CAI*)、依赖比率(D)、人类依赖指数(HRI)和人类认知漂移率(HCDR)。这些指标构成了一个低维度量空间，用于评估人机系统的协同性能和认知可持续性。

Result: 框架揭示了人机系统设计中的核心张力：最大化短期混合能力不一定能保持长期人类认知能力。提出了认知可持续性约束的设计原则。

Conclusion: 人机系统应在认知可持续性约束下设计，确保混合性能的提升不以人类专业知识的退化为代价。需要平衡短期性能与长期认知能力的保持。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in human decision-making, where it can either enhance human reasoning or induce excessive cognitive dependence. This paper introduces a conceptual and mathematical framework for distinguishing cognitive amplification, in which AI improves hybrid human-AI performance while preserving human expertise, from cognitive delegation, in which reasoning is progressively outsourced to AI systems.
  To characterize these regimes, we define a set of operational metrics: the Cognitive Amplification Index (CAI*), the Dependency Ratio (D), the Human Reliance Index (HRI), and the Human Cognitive Drift Rate (HCDR). Together, these quantities provide a low-dimensional metric space for evaluating not only whether human-AI systems achieve genuine synergistic performance, but also whether such performance is cognitively sustainable for the human component over time.
  The framework highlights a central design tension in human-AI systems: maximizing short-term hybrid capability does not necessarily preserve long-term human cognitive competence. We therefore argue that human-AI systems should be designed under a cognitive sustainability constraint, such that gains in hybrid performance do not come at the cost of degradation in human expertise.

</details>


### [7] [Dual-Model Prediction of Affective Engagement and Vocal Attractiveness from Speaker Expressiveness in Video Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18758)
*Hung-Yue Suen,Kuo-En Hung,Fan-Hsun Tseng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于说话者侧情感表达的机器学习方法，预测异步视频学习中观众的情感参与度和声音吸引力，无需观众侧输入信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足对可扩展、保护隐私的情感计算应用的需求，探索仅使用说话者侧情感表达能否有效预测观众反馈

Method: 开发两个回归模型：1) 情感参与度预测模型，整合面部动态、眼动特征、韵律和认知语义；2) 声音吸引力预测模型，仅基于说话者侧声学特征。使用MOOC大规模语料库训练

Result: 在说话者独立测试集上，两个模型均表现出优异预测性能：情感参与度R²=0.85，声音吸引力R²=0.88，证实说话者侧情感能有效代表聚合观众反馈

Conclusion: 说话者侧多模态特征（包括声学特征）能够前瞻性预测观众反馈，无需依赖观众侧输入信息，为可扩展、隐私保护的情感AI应用提供了实证支持

Abstract: This paper outlines a machine learning-enabled speaker-centric Emotion AI approach capable of predicting audience-affective engagement and vocal attractiveness in asynchronous video-based learning, relying solely on speaker-side affective expressions. Inspired by the demand for scalable, privacy-preserving affective computing applications, this speaker-centric Emotion AI approach incorporates two distinct regression models that leverage a massive corpus developed within Massive Open Online Courses (MOOCs) to enable affectively engaging experiences. The regression model predicting affective engagement is developed by assimilating emotional expressions emanating from facial dynamics, oculomotor features, prosody, and cognitive semantics, while incorporating a second regression model to predict vocal attractiveness based exclusively on speaker-side acoustic features. Notably, on speaker-independent test sets, both regression models yielded impressive predictive performance (R2 = 0.85 for affective engagement and R2 = 0.88 for vocal attractiveness), confirming that speaker-side affect can functionally represent aggregated audience feedback. This paper provides a speaker-centric Emotion AI approach substantiated by an empirical study discovering that speaker-side multimodal features, including acoustics, can prospectively forecast audience feedback without necessarily employing audience-side input information.

</details>


### [8] [Through the Looking-Glass: AI-Mediated Video Communication Reduces Interpersonal Trust and Confidence in Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18868)
*Nelson Navajas Fernández,Jeffrey T. Hancock,Maurice Jakesch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI视频中介工具（美颜、背景替换、虚拟化身）不影响谎言检测准确性，但降低信任感和判断信心


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI中介工具（视频美颜、背景替换、虚拟化身）如何影响人际信任、谎言检测能力和判断信心，验证AI是否破坏人们区分真假的能力

Method: 两个预注册在线实验（N=2000），参与者观看说话者真实或欺骗性陈述的视频，分为三种不同AI中介水平条件，测量信任度、谎言检测准确性和判断信心

Result: AI中介视频中感知信任和判断信心下降，特别是在部分参与者使用虚拟化身而其他人不使用的场景中；但实际判断准确性不变，参与者不会更怀疑使用AI工具者说谎

Conclusion: AI中介不会破坏人们区分真假的能力，反驳了基于线索的谎言检测理论；在信任和信心重要的情境中，需要可信赖的AI中介工具

Abstract: AI-based tools that mediate, enhance or generate parts of video communication may interfere with how people evaluate trustworthiness and credibility. In two preregistered online experiments (N = 2,000), we examined whether AI-mediated video retouching, background replacement and avatars affect interpersonal trust, people's ability to detect lies and confidence in their judgments. Participants watched short videos of speakers making truthful or deceptive statements across three conditions with varying levels of AI mediation. We observed that perceived trust and confidence in judgments declined in AI-mediated videos, particularly in settings in which some participants used avatars while others did not. However, participants' actual judgment accuracy remained unchanged, and they were no more inclined to suspect those using AI tools of lying. Our findings provide evidence against concerns that AI mediation undermines people's ability to distinguish truth from lies, and against cue-based accounts of lie detection more generally. They highlight the importance of trustworthy AI mediation tools in contexts where not only truth, but also trust and confidence matter.

</details>


### [9] [From Accuracy to Readiness: Metrics and Benchmarks for Human-AI Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18895)
*Min Hun Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出评估人-AI决策的团队准备度框架，通过交互轨迹而非模型属性或自我报告信任度来衡量校准、错误恢复和治理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统作为人类决策的协作者被部署，但评估实践主要关注模型准确性，而非人-AI团队是否准备好安全有效地协作。实证证据表明许多失败源于校准不当的依赖，包括AI错误时的过度使用和AI有帮助时的使用不足

Method: 提出以团队准备度为中心的人-AI决策评估框架，引入包含结果、依赖行为、安全信号和时间学习四个部分的评估指标分类法，并将这些指标连接到人-AI入职和协作的"理解-控制-改进"(U-C-I)生命周期

Result: 通过交互轨迹而非模型属性或自我报告信任度来操作化评估，使框架能够进行部署相关的校准、错误恢复和治理能力评估

Conclusion: 该框架旨在支持更可比较的基准测试和人-AI准备度的累积研究，推动更安全、更负责任的人-AI协作

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are deployed as collaborators in human decision-making. Yet, evaluation practices focus primarily on model accuracy rather than whether human-AI teams are prepared to collaborate safely and effectively. Empirical evidence shows that many failures arise from miscalibrated reliance, including overuse when AI is wrong and underuse when it is helpful.
  This paper proposes a measurement framework for evaluating human-AI decision-making centered on team readiness. We introduce a four part taxonomy of evaluation metrics spanning outcomes, reliance behavior, safety signals, and learning over time, and connect these metrics to the Understand-Control-Improve (U-C-I) lifecycle of human-AI onboarding and collaboration.
  By operationalizing evaluation through interaction traces rather than model properties or self-reported trust, our framework enables deployment-relevant assessment of calibration, error recovery, and governance. We aim to support more comparable benchmarks and cumulative research on human-AI readiness, advancing safer and more accountable human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [10] [What We Talk About When We Talk About Frameworks in HCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18950)
*Shitao Fang,Koji Yatani,Kasper Hornbæk*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对CHI会议2015-2024年间615篇包含"框架"术语的论文进行系统综述，分析HCI领域中框架的实际使用情况、功能和学术实践


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI领域中框架作为理论贡献的重要形式，广泛用于支持构思、设计和评估，但对其实际使用方式、功能作用以及塑造它们的学术实践了解甚少

Method: 对CHI会议2015-2024年间的615篇论文进行系统综述，这些论文都突出使用了"框架"术语；将论文分为六种参与类型，通过功能类型学分析新提出框架的角色、形式和基本组成部分，考察它们如何构建、验证和表述以便重用

Result: 研究结果显示：1）提出新框架的热情超过了对现有框架进行迭代的意愿；2）框架功能存在模糊性；3）系统性验证稀缺

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，呼吁在HCI框架的开发和使用中采取更严谨、反思性和累积性的实践方法

Abstract: In HCI, frameworks function as a type of theoretical contribution, often supporting ideation, design, and evaluation. Yet, little is known about how they are actually used, what functions they serve, and which scholarly practices that shape them. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic review of 615 papers from a decade of CHI proceedings (2015-2024) that prominently featured the term framework. We classified these papers into six engagement types. We then examined the role, form, and essential components of newly proposed frameworks through a functional typology, analyzing how they are constructed, validated, and articulated for reuse. Our results show that enthusiasm for proposing new frameworks exceeds the willingness to iterate on existing ones. They also highlight the ambiguity in the function of frameworks and the scarcity of systematic validation. Based on these insights, we call for more rigorous, reflective, and cumulative practices in the development and use of frameworks in HCI.

</details>


### [11] [Sketch2Topo: Using Hand-Drawn Inputs for Diffusion-Based Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18960)
*Shuyue Feng,Cedric Caremel,Yoshihiro Kawahara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Sketch2Topo：一种基于扩散模型的拓扑优化工具，通过手绘草图输入实现几何定制和物理约束指定，支持掩码输入实现局部优化，平衡结构性能与美学属性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化方法计算成本高、时间消耗大；现有基于生成式AI的方法存在定制性有限、用户认知负荷高的问题；同时难以平衡结构性能与美学属性

Method: 开发Sketch2Topo工具，基于扩散模型增强图像到图像生成和图像编辑能力；支持手绘草图输入定制几何形状和指定物理约束；支持掩码输入实现局部区域优化

Result: 总结了工具的工作流程和细节；进行了简要的定量评估；探索了应用场景并讨论了手绘输入如何提高可用性，同时平衡功能性与美学

Conclusion: Sketch2Topo通过手绘输入提高了拓扑优化的定制性和可用性，有效平衡了结构性能与美学属性，解决了传统方法和现有AI方法的局限性

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) is employed in engineering to optimize structural performance while maximizing material efficiency. However, traditional TO methods incur significant computational and time costs. Although research has leveraged generative AI to predict TO outcomes and validated feasibility and accuracy, existing approaches still suffer from limited customizability and impose a high cognitive load on users. Furthermore, balancing structural performance with aesthetic attributes remains a persistent challenge. We developed Sketch2Topo, which augments a diffusion-based TO model with image-to-image generation and image editing capabilities. With Sketch2Topo, users can use sketching to customize geometries and specify physical constraints. The tool also supports mask input, enabling users to perform TO on selected regions only, thereby supporting higher levels of customization. We summarize the workflow and details of the tool and conduct a brief quantitative evaluation. Finally, we explore application scenarios and discuss how hand-drawn input improves usability while balancing functionality and aesthetics.

</details>


### [12] [SVLAT: Scientific Visualization Literacy Assessment Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19000)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Kaiyuan Tang,Kuangshi Ai,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个科学可视化素养评估测试(SVLAT)，用于测量公众的科学可视化素养，包含49个项目，基于18个科学可视化，涵盖8种可视化技术和11种任务类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学可视化已成为探索、理解和交流复杂科学现象的重要手段，但该领域缺乏经过验证的评估工具来衡量人们如何阅读、理解和解释科学可视化。

Method: 采用分阶段的心理测量学流程：定义构念和蓝图、项目生成、五位科学可视化专家进行内容效度比评审(CVR均值=0.79)、30人试点测试、485人大规模测试。使用经典测试理论和项目反应理论进行项目分析和精炼。

Result: SVLAT在测试样本中表现出高信度(McDonald's omega_t=0.82，Cronbach's alpha=0.81)。评估材料可在https://osf.io/hr3nw/获取。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一个经过心理测量学验证的科学可视化素养评估测试，填补了该领域的工具空白，为评估公众科学可视化素养提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Scientific visualization (SciVis) has become an essential means for exploring, understanding, and communicating complex scientific phenomena. However, the field still lacks a validated instrument assessing how well people read, understand, and interpret them. We present a scientific visualization literacy assessment test (SVLAT) that measures the general public's SciVis literacy. Covering a range of visualization forms and interpretation demands, SVLAT comprises 49 items grounded in 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations spanning eight visualization techniques and 11 tasks. Instrument development followed a staged, psychometrically grounded pipeline. We defined the construct and blueprint, followed by item generation, and expert review with five SciVis experts using the content validity ratio (mean CVR = 0.79). We subsequently administered a pilot test (30 participants) and a large-scale test tryout (485 participants) to evaluate the instrument's psychometric properties. For validation, we performed item analysis and refinement using both classical test theory (CTT) and item response theory (IRT) to examine item functioning and overall test quality. SVLAT demonstrates high reliability in the tryout sample (McDonald's omega_t = 0.82, Cronbach's alpha = 0.81). The assessment materials are available at https://osf.io/hr3nw/.

</details>


### [13] [LLMs Aren't Human: A Critical Perspective on LLM Personality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19030)
*Kim Zierahn,Cristina Cachero,Anna Korhonen,Nuria Oliver*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文批判性地评估了LLM人格测试的有效性，发现LLM对人格测试的响应不满足人格的六个定义特征，因此这些评估不能测量等同于人类人格的结构。作者提出了从拟人化特质归因转向功能性评估的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究经常将大五人格量表应用于评估LLM行为，将其类比为人类人格，但缺乏对潜在假设的质疑。需要批判性地评估LLM对人格测试的响应是否真正满足人格的定义特征。

Method: 通过系统性地评估LLM对人格测试的响应是否满足人格的六个定义特征，进行批判性分析。具体方法包括检验LLM行为是否具有稳定性、一致性、内在性等关键人格特征。

Result: 研究发现LLM对人格测试的响应不满足人格的任何一个定义特征，表明这类评估不能测量等同于人类人格的结构。LLM的行为缺乏人类人格所具有的稳定性、内在性和一致性。

Conclusion: 需要从拟人化特质归因转向功能性评估的研究议程，澄清人格测试在LLM中实际测量的是什么，并开发LLM特定的框架来表征稳定、内在的行为模式。

Abstract: A growing body of research examines personality traits in Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in human-agent collaboration. Prior work has frequently applied the Big Five inventory to assess LLM behavior analogous to human personality, without questioning the underlying assumptions. This paper critically evaluates whether LLM responses to personality tests satisfy six defining characteristics of personality. We find that none are fully met, indicating that such assessments do not measure a construct equivalent to human personality. We propose a research agenda for shifting from anthropomorphic trait attribution toward functional evaluations, clarifying what personality tests actually capture in LLMs and developing LLM-specific frameworks for characterizing stable, intrinsic behavior.

</details>


### [14] [Exploring the Role of Interaction Data to Empower End-User Decision-Making In UI Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19196)
*Sérgio Alves,Carlos Duarte,Kyle Montague,Tiago Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了一种反思性个性化方法，让用户通过交互数据识别个性化机会，发现用户能独立识别机会但更偏好系统提供可视化建议支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户驱动的个性化设置中，由于缺乏识别和评估有价值机会的支持，导致个性化功能使用不足。需要探索如何让用户通过反思自己的数字交互数据来发现有意义的个性化机会和收益。

Method: 采用访谈12名参与者，使用实验性小插曲作为设计探针，支持参与者反思不同形式的交互数据使用方式，以及偏好的系统支持水平。

Result: 研究发现：1）用户能够独立识别个性化机会，但更偏好系统通过可视化个性化建议提供支持；2）交互数据能够塑造用户对个性化的认知和态度，包括强化改变的感知价值、数据收集价值、帮助权衡收益与努力、增加系统建议的透明度。

Conclusion: 通过反思性参与交互数据的设计，可以提升最终用户对界面的控制权，为设计提升用户自主权的个性化软件提供了机会。

Abstract: User interface personalization enhances digital efficiency, usability, and accessibility. However, in user-driven setups, limited support for identifying and evaluating worthwhile opportunities often leads to underuse. We explore a reflexive personalization approach where individuals engage with their digital interaction data to identify meaningful personalization opportunities and benefits. We interviewed 12 participants, using experimental vignettes as design probes to support reflection on different forms of using interaction data to empower decision-making in personalization and the preferred level of system support. We found that people can independently identify personalization opportunities but prefer system support through visual personalization suggestions. Interaction data can shape how users perceive and approach personalization by reinforcing the perceived value of change and data collection, helping them weigh benefits against effort, and increasing the transparency of system suggestions. We discuss opportunities for designing personalization software that raises end-users' agency over interfaces through reflective engagement with their interaction data.

</details>

### [15] [Frayed RoPE and Long Inputs: A Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18017)
*Davis Wertheimer,Aozhong Zhang,Derrick Liu,Penghang Yin,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoPE-ID：通过高频旋转部分通道的简单修改，解决RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时的性能崩溃问题，保持注意力机制中的关键/查询聚类分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时会导致性能崩溃，现有分析认为这是通道旋转"超出分布"所致，但缺乏对额外旋转如何导致病理行为的深入理解。

Method: 通过实证和理论分析建立RoPE注意力行为的统一几何理解，发现注意力机制形成关键/查询潜在点云的紧密聚类，创建"汇令牌"避免不必要的令牌混合。提出RoPE-ID：对部分通道应用高频旋转，保持关键/查询聚类分离。

Result: 在1B和3B参数Transformer上，使用LongBench和RULER信息检索基准测试，证明RoPE-ID能有效处理超出训练长度的输入。

Conclusion: RoPE的性能崩溃源于破坏关键/查询聚类分离，抑制汇令牌功能；RoPE-ID通过简单修改实现分布内泛化，为长序列处理提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a widely adopted technique for encoding position in language models, which, while effective, causes performance breakdown when input length exceeds training length. Prior analyses assert (rightly) that long inputs cause channels to rotate ``out of distribution,'' but it is not clear how extra rotation relates to or causes pathological behavior. Through empirical and theoretical analysis we advance a unified geometric understanding of attention behavior with RoPE. We find that attention induces tight clustering of separated key and query latent point clouds, allowing for creation of sink tokens: placeholders that allow attention heads to avoid token mixing when not required. RoPE applied to longer inputs damages this key/query cluster separation, producing pathological behavior by inhibiting sink token functionality. From this geometric perspective, we propose RoPE-ID (In Distribution), a straightforward modification that allows attention layers to generalize to longer inputs out of the box: apply RoPE with high frequency to a subset of channels. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RoPE-ID for extended inputs using 1B and 3B parameter Transformers on the LongBench and RULER information retrieval benchmarks.

</details>


### [16] [Quotient Geometry and Persistence-Stable Metrics for Swarm Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18041)
*Mark M. Bailey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于多智能体群组和星座重构的几何表示方法，通过引入商形成空间和形成匹配度量，为无序点配置的比较和监控提供持久性稳定、对称不变的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群组和星座重构可以视为无序点配置在环境空间中的运动。需要开发能够比较和监控多智能体配置数据的几何表示方法，这些方法应具有持久稳定性（对噪声鲁棒）和对称不变性（对智能体重标记和环境对称性不变）。

Method: 引入商形成空间 $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$，其中 $M$ 是环境空间，$G$ 是环境对称群，$S_n$ 是置换群。定义形成匹配度量 $d_{M,G}$，通过优化环境对称 $g\in G$ 和重标记 $σ\in S_n$ 下的最坏情况分配误差得到。该度量是Gromov-Hausdorff距离的结构化、物理可解释松弛。结合Vietoris-Rips持久性的稳定性，得到持久性稳定签名。

Result: 证明了 $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$ 和 $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$。分析了 $(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$ 的度量几何：在 $M$ 紧致/完备且 $G$ 紧致的假设下，该空间紧致/完备且度量诱导商拓扑；若 $M$ 是测地空间，则商空间也是测地空间，并在碰撞和对称层上呈现分层奇点。研究了签名的表达能力，识别了对称失配和持久性压缩机制。在相位圆模型中证明了条件逆定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体配置的比较和监控提供了理论框架，通过持久性稳定、对称不变的几何签名，能够有效处理群组和星座重构问题。在 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 和 $\mathbb{T}^m$ 上的示例展示了该方法在卫星星座和编队设置中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Swarm and constellation reconfiguration can be viewed as motion of an unordered point configuration in an ambient space. Here, we provide persistence-stable, symmetry-invariant geometric representations for comparing and monitoring multi-agent configuration data. We introduce a quotient formation space $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ and a formation matching metric $d_{M,G}$ obtained by optimizing a worst-case assignment error over ambient symmetries $g\in G$ and relabelings $σ\in S_n$. This metric is a structured, physically interpretable relaxation of Gromov--Hausdorff distance: the induced inter-agent metric spaces satisfy $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$. Composing this bound with stability of Vietoris--Rips persistence yields $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$, providing persistence-stable signatures for reconfiguration monitoring. We analyze the metric geometry of $(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$: under compactness/completeness assumptions on $M$ and compact $G$ it is compact/complete and the metric induces the quotient topology; if $M$ is geodesic then the quotient is geodesic and exhibits stratified singularities along collision and symmetry strata, relating it to classical configuration spaces. We study expressivity of the signatures, identifying symmetry-mismatch and persistence-compression mechanisms for non-injectivity. Finally, in a phase-circle model we prove a conditional inverse theorem: under semicircle support and a gap-labeling margin, the $H_0$ signature is locally bi-Lipschitz to $d_{M,G}$ up to an explicit factor, yielding two-sided control. Examples on $\mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{T}^m$ illustrate satellite-constellation and formation settings.

</details>


### [17] [Engineering Verifiable Modularity in Transformers via Per-Layer Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18029)
*J. Clayton Kerce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种架构干预方法，通过分层监督训练使Transformer模型展现隐藏的模块化特性，从而实现对模型行为的可预测控制，而非仅停留在相关性解释层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型存在"九头蛇效应"——即使移除被识别为关键功能的注意力头，模型行为也几乎不变，因为分布式冗余会补偿损伤。这使得基于相关性的可解释性变得虚幻，无法实现因果预测和控制。

Method: 提出三种架构干预：1) 双流处理分离token和上下文表示；2) 分层监督为每个深度提供独立梯度信号；3) 门控注意力正则化以产生离散激活模式。通过分层监督训练，使模型展现模块化特性。

Result: 分层监督训练的模型产生比标准训练模型大5-23倍的消融效应，提供4倍的控制杠杆。消融效应标准差从0.63%增至6.32%，揭示了哪些预测依赖哪些电路。验证了功能重组：不同任务通过不同注意力头路由。

Conclusion: 架构干预特别是分层监督能够暴露Transformer的隐藏模块化，将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制。这为理解模型内部计算动态提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Transformers resist surgical control. Ablating an attention head identified as critical for capitalization produces minimal behavioral change because distributed redundancy compensates for damage. This Hydra effect renders interpretability illusory: we may identify components through correlation, but cannot predict or control their causal role. We demonstrate that architectural interventions can expose hidden modularity. Our approach combines dual-stream processing separating token and contextual representations, per-layer supervision providing independent gradient signal at each depth, and gated attention regularizing toward discrete activation patterns. When trained with per-layer supervision, models produce ablation effects 5 to 23 times larger than architecturally identical controls trained with standard objectives. This enables 4 times greater control leverage on targeted behaviors: scaling identified attention heads produces smooth, predictable changes in model output. The key finding is architectural. Without per-layer supervision, ablation damage concentrates near zero with low variance (Winograd standard deviation 0.63%). With per-layer supervision, effects spread widely (standard deviation 6.32%), revealing which predictions depend on which circuits. The larger variance is not measurement noise but the signature of unmasked modularity. We validate our approach through three components: engineered features that capture computational dynamics rather than vocabulary structure (validated by near-zero correlation with raw activation clustering), an architecture providing positive control for modularity, and causal experiments demonstrating functional reorganization where different tasks route through different attention heads. This es tablishes a methodology for transforming interpretability from passive observation to active control.

</details>


### [18] [HEP Statistical Inference for UAV Fault Detection: CLs, LRT, and SBI Applied to Blade Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18546)
*Khushiyant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将粒子物理统计方法应用于多旋翼无人机螺旋桨故障检测，包括似然比检验、CLs修正频率方法和序列神经后验估计，在频谱特征上实现二元检测、可控误报率和故障严重程度后验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将粒子物理学中成熟的统计方法（特别是用于信号检测和不确定性量化的方法）迁移到多旋翼无人机螺旋桨故障检测领域，以提供更可靠、可解释且具有统计保证的故障检测系统。

Method: 1. 似然比检验(LRT)用于二元故障检测；2. CLs修正频率方法控制误报率；3. 序列神经后验估计(SNPE)进行定量故障表征。系统基于与转子谐波物理相关的频谱特征工作，输出包括：二元检测结果、可控误报率、故障严重程度和电机位置的校准后验分布。

Result: 在UAV-FD六旋翼数据集上（18次真实飞行，5%和10%叶片损伤），留一飞行交叉验证的AUC为0.862±0.007（95% CI: 0.849-0.876），优于CUSUM(0.708)、自编码器(0.753)和LSTM自编码器(0.551)。在5%误报率下，系统检测到93%的显著损伤和81%的轻微损伤。在PADRE四旋翼平台上，仅重新拟合生成模型后AUC达到0.986。SNPE提供完整的故障严重程度后验分布（90%可信区间覆盖率92-100%，MAE 0.012）。逐次飞行序列检测实现100%故障检测和94%总体准确率。

Conclusion: 粒子物理统计方法成功迁移到多旋翼无人机故障检测领域，提供了具有统计保证的检测系统。该系统不仅提供二元检测，还能控制误报率并提供包含不确定性的定量故障表征，优于传统方法。该方法具有良好泛化能力，在不同平台间迁移时仅需重新拟合生成模型。

Abstract: This paper transfers three statistical methods from particle physics to multirotor propeller fault detection: the likelihood ratio test (LRT) for binary detection, the CLs modified frequentist method for false alarm rate control, and sequential neural posterior estimation (SNPE) for quantitative fault characterization. Operating on spectral features tied to rotor harmonic physics, the system returns three outputs: binary detection, controlled false alarm rates, and calibrated posteriors over fault severity and motor location. On UAV-FD, a hexarotor dataset of 18 real flights with 5% and 10% blade damage, leave-one-flight-out cross-validation gives AUC 0.862 +/- 0.007 (95% CI: 0.849--0.876), outperforming CUSUM (0.708 +/- 0.010), autoencoder (0.753 +/- 0.009), and LSTM autoencoder (0.551). At 5% false alarm rate the system detects 93% of significant and 81% of subtle blade damage. On PADRE, a quadrotor platform, AUC reaches 0.986 after refitting only the generative models. SNPE gives a full posterior over fault severity (90% credible interval coverage 92--100%, MAE 0.012), so the output includes uncertainty rather than just a point estimate or fault flag. Per-flight sequential detection achieves 100% fault detection with 94% overall accuracy.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Differentiating Between Failures and Domain Shifts in Industrial Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18032)
*Natalia Wojak-Strzelecka,Szymon Bobek,Grzegorz J. Nalepa,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合变更点检测、域适应和可解释AI的方法，用于区分数据流中的故障与正常域偏移，避免误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，数据变化并不总是代表系统异常状态，可能只是正常系统演化（如处理新产品导致的域偏移）。传统方法可能将这种正常域偏移误判为故障，因此需要区分故障与健康的数据分布变化以确保系统鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 使用改进的Page-Hinkley变更点检测器识别域偏移和可能的故障；2. 结合监督域适应算法进行快速在线异常检测；3. 集成可解释人工智能(XAI)组件帮助操作员最终区分域偏移与故障。

Result: 方法在钢铁工厂数据流上进行了实验验证，展示了区分故障与正常域偏移的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅能检测数据分布变化和异常，还能有效区分故障与正常域偏移，提高了异常检测系统的实用性和鲁棒性，通过XAI组件增强了人机协作决策能力。

Abstract: Anomaly and failure detection methods are crucial in identifying deviations from normal system operational conditions, which allows for actions to be taken in advance, usually preventing more serious damages. Long-lasting deviations indicate failures, while sudden, isolated changes in the data indicate anomalies. However, in many practical applications, changes in the data do not always represent abnormal system states. Such changes may be recognized incorrectly as failures, while being a normal evolution of the system, e.g. referring to characteristics of starting the processing of a new product, i.e. realizing a domain shift. Therefore, distinguishing between failures and such ''healthy'' changes in data distribution is critical to ensure the practical robustness of the system. In this paper, we propose a method that not only detects changes in the data distribution and anomalies but also allows us to distinguish between failures and normal domain shifts inherent to a given process. The proposed method consists of a modified Page-Hinkley changepoint detector for identification of the domain shift and possible failures and supervised domain-adaptation-based algorithms for fast, online anomaly detection. These two are coupled with an explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) component that aims at helping the human operator to finally differentiate between domain shifts and failures. The method is illustrated by an experiment on a data stream from the steel factory.

</details>


### [20] [Taming Epilepsy: Mean Field Control of Whole-Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18035)
*Ming Li,Ting Gao,Jingqiao Dua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GK-MFG框架，结合储层计算和APAC-Net，通过图正则化Koopman算子将EEG动态嵌入线性潜空间，实现癫痫发作的鲁棒抑制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作期间的高维神经动力学控制面临非线性特性和复杂脑连接的挑战，需要既能抑制发作又尊重大脑功能拓扑结构的方法

Method: 提出图正则化Koopman平均场博弈框架，结合储层计算近似Koopman算子，使用APAC-Net解决分布控制问题，通过PLV导出的图拉普拉斯约束嵌入EEG动态到线性潜空间

Result: 方法实现了鲁棒的癫痫发作抑制，同时尊重大脑的功能拓扑结构

Conclusion: GK-MFG框架通过将非线性EEG动态线性化并施加图正则化约束，为癫痫发作控制提供了一种有效且尊重大脑功能连接的方法

Abstract: Controlling the high-dimensional neural dynamics during epileptic seizures remains a significant challenge due to the nonlinear characteristics and complex connectivity of the brain. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, namely Graph-Regularized Koopman Mean-Field Game (GK-MFG), which integrates Reservoir Computing (RC) for Koopman operator approximation with Alternating Population and Agent Control Network (APAC-Net) for solving distributional control problems. By embedding Electroencephalogram (EEG) dynamics into a linear latent space and imposing graph Laplacian constraints derived from the Phase Locking Value (PLV), our method achieves robust seizure suppression while respecting the functional topological structure of the brain.

</details>


### [21] [MST-Direct: Matching via Sinkhorn Transport for Multivariate Geostatistical Simulation with Complex Non-Linear Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18036)
*Tchalies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MST-Direct算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布，同时保持空间相关结构，以解决传统方法无法保留复杂非线性依赖关系的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元地质统计模拟需要忠实再现地质变量间复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系。传统方法如高斯Copula和LU分解假设线性相关结构，通常无法保留这些复杂的联合分布模式。

Method: 提出MST-Direct（通过Sinkhorn传输的匹配）算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布，同时保持空间相关结构。该方法将所有变量同时处理为单个多维向量，实现在完整联合空间中的关系匹配，而不是依赖成对线性依赖关系。

Result: 该方法能够处理复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系，相比传统方法能更好地保留复杂联合分布模式。

Conclusion: MST-Direct为多元地质统计模拟提供了一种新方法，能够直接匹配多元分布并保持空间结构，克服了传统线性方法在处理复杂非线性依赖关系时的局限性。

Abstract: Multivariate geostatistical simulation requires the faithful reproduction of complex non-linear dependencies among geological variables, including bimodal distributions, step functions, and heteroscedastic relationships. Traditional methods such as the Gaussian Copula and LU Decomposition assume linear correlation structures and often fail to preserve these complex joint distribution patterns. We propose MST-Direct (Matching via Sinkhorn Transport), a novel algorithm based on Optimal Transport theory that uses the Sinkhorn algorithm to directly match multivariate distributions while preserving spatial correlation structures. The method processes all variables simultaneously as a single multidimensional vector, enabling relational matching across the full joint space rather than relying on pairwise linear dependencies.

</details>


### [22] [NANOZK: Layerwise Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Verifiable Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18046)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METHOD是一个零知识证明系统，用于验证LLM推理过程，确保用户获得的输出确实来自特定模型的计算，防止服务商替换廉价模型或返回缓存结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用户查询专有LLM API时，无法获得加密保证确认输出确实来自声称的模型。服务提供商可能替换廉价模型、应用激进量化或返回缓存响应，而用户支付高价却无法检测这些欺诈行为。

Method: 利用transformer推理自然分解为独立层计算的特点，开发层间证明框架，每层生成恒定大小证明。采用查找表近似非算术操作（softmax、GELU、LayerNorm）且无精度损失，并引入Fisher信息引导的验证机制处理无法证明所有层的情况。

Result: 在d=128的transformer模型上，METHOD生成5.5KB的恒定大小层证明（2.1KB注意力+3.5KB MLP），验证时间24ms。相比EZKL，证明大小减少70倍，证明时间快5.7倍，同时保持形式化可靠性保证（epsilon < 1e-37）。查找表近似完全保持模型困惑度。

Conclusion: METHOD通过层间分解和查找表近似实现了可扩展的LLM推理验证，解决了传统整体方法面临的可扩展性障碍，为LLM服务提供了加密可验证性，确保用户获得所支付的高质量模型输出。

Abstract: When users query proprietary LLM APIs, they receive outputs with no cryptographic assurance that the claimed model was actually used. Service providers could substitute cheaper models, apply aggressive quantization, or return cached responses - all undetectable by users paying premium prices for frontier capabilities. We present METHOD, a zero-knowledge proof system that makes LLM inference verifiable: users can cryptographically confirm that outputs correspond to the computation of a specific model.
  Our approach exploits the fact that transformer inference naturally decomposes into independent layer computations, enabling a layerwise proof framework where each layer generates a constant-size proof regardless of model width. This decomposition sidesteps the scalability barrier facing monolithic approaches and enables parallel proving. We develop lookup table approximations for non-arithmetic operations (softmax, GELU, LayerNorm) that introduce zero measurable accuracy loss, and introduce Fisher information-guided verification for scenarios where proving all layers is impractical.
  On transformer models up to d=128, METHOD generates constant-size layer proofs of 5.5KB (2.1KB attention + 3.5KB MLP) with 24 ms verification time. Compared to EZKL, METHOD achieves 70x smaller proofs and 5.7x faster proving time at d=128, while maintaining formal soundness guarantees (epsilon < 1e-37). Lookup approximations preserve model perplexity exactly, enabling verification without quality compromise.

</details>


### [23] [Variational Phasor Circuits for Phase-Native Brain-Computer Interface Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18078)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPC是一种在S¹单位圆流形上运行的确定性经典学习架构，使用可训练相移、局部幺正混合和环境复空间中的结构化干涉，为空间分布信号的二分类和多分类提供统一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受变分量子电路启发，旨在提供一种替代密集神经计算的实用且数学原理清晰的方案，利用单位圆相位干涉减少可训练参数数量，同时保持竞争力。

Method: 采用相位原生设计，用可训练相移替代密集实值权重矩阵，通过局部幺正混合和结构化干涉在复空间操作；单VPC块支持紧凑的基于相位的决策边界，堆叠VPC组合通过块间拉回归一化扩展到更深电路。

Result: 在合成脑机接口基准测试中，VPC能够解码困难的思维状态分类任务，达到竞争性准确率，且比标准欧几里得基线显著减少可训练参数。

Conclusion: 单位圆相位干涉是密集神经计算的实用且数学原理清晰的替代方案，VPC既可作为独立分类器，也可作为未来混合相位-量子系统的前端编码层。

Abstract: We present the \textbf{Variational Phasor Circuit (VPC)}, a deterministic classical learning architecture operating on the continuous $S^1$ unit circle manifold. Inspired by variational quantum circuits, VPC replaces dense real-valued weight matrices with trainable phase shifts, local unitary mixing, and structured interference in the ambient complex space. This phase-native design provides a unified method for both binary and multi-class classification of spatially distributed signals. A single VPC block supports compact phase-based decision boundaries, while stacked VPC compositions extend the model to deeper circuits through inter-block pull-back normalization. Using synthetic brain-computer interface benchmarks, we show that VPC can decode difficult mental-state classification tasks with competitive accuracy and substantially fewer trainable parameters than standard Euclidean baselines. These results position unit-circle phase interference as a practical and mathematically principled alternative to dense neural computation, and motivate VPC as both a standalone classifier and a front-end encoding layer for future hybrid phasor-quantum systems.

</details>


### [24] [SLEA-RL: Step-Level Experience Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Agentic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18079)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLEA-RL：一种在每一步决策时基于当前观察检索相关经验的强化学习框架，解决了多轮工具使用任务中静态经验检索不匹配的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验增强方法在多轮任务中仅基于初始任务描述一次性检索经验，并在整个回合中保持不变。然而在多轮设置中，观察在每一步都会变化，这种静态检索会随着回合进展变得越来越不匹配

Method: SLEA-RL包含三个核心组件：1）步骤级观察聚类，将结构等价的环境状态分组以实现高效的聚类索引检索；2）自演化经验库，通过基于分数的准入和速率限制提取来提炼成功策略和失败模式；3）带有步骤级信用分配的策略优化，用于多轮回合中的细粒度优势估计。经验库通过语义分析而非梯度更新与策略共同演化

Result: 在长视野多轮智能体基准测试中，SLEA-RL相比各种强化学习基线实现了更优越的性能

Conclusion: SLEA-RL通过步骤级经验检索和自演化经验库，有效解决了多轮任务中经验检索与当前观察不匹配的问题，显著提升了LLM智能体在多轮工具使用任务中的表现

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown strong results on multi-turn tool-use tasks, yet they operate in isolation during training, failing to leverage experiences accumulated across episodes. Existing experience-augmented methods address this by organizing trajectories into retrievable libraries, but they retrieve experiences only once based on the initial task description and hold them constant throughout the episode. In multi-turn settings where observations change at every step, this static retrieval becomes increasingly mismatched as episodes progress. We propose SLEA-RL (Step-Level Experience-Augmented Reinforcement Learning), a framework that retrieves relevant experiences at each decision step conditioned on the current observation. SLEA-RL operates through three components: (i) step-level observation clustering that groups structurally equivalent environmental states for efficient cluster-indexed retrieval; (ii) a self-evolving experience library that distills successful strategies and failure patterns through score-based admission and rate-limited extraction; and (iii) policy optimization with step-level credit assignment for fine-grained advantage estimation across multi-turn episodes. The experience library evolves alongside the policy through semantic analysis rather than gradient updates. Experiments on long-horizon multi-turn agent benchmarks demonstrate that SLEA-RL achieves superior performance compared to various reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [25] [Enhancing Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning with an Online Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18088)
*Hao Ma,Zhiqiang Pu,Yang Liu,Xiaolin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束方法解决强化学习微调中约束与优化目标的冲突，通过参考模型作为在线精炼器生成最小修正，实现根据输出质量自动调整约束强度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束在稳定强化学习微调的同时限制了模型发现更优解的能力，需要一种能根据模型能力动态调整的约束机制

Method: 使用参考模型作为在线精炼器，对微调模型的输出进行最小修正，保留正确内容的同时修正错误，然后通过监督微调损失训练模型产生精炼输出

Result: 在对话和代码生成任务上，动态约束方法在保持训练稳定性的同时，显著优于KL正则化和无约束基线，获得更高的任务奖励

Conclusion: 动态约束通过自适应调整约束强度，有效解决了约束与优化目标的冲突，为强化学习微调提供了更灵活的约束机制

Abstract: Constraints are essential for stabilizing reinforcement learning fine-tuning (RFT) and preventing degenerate outputs, yet they inherently conflict with the optimization objective because stronger constraints limit the ability of a fine-tuned model to discover better solutions. We propose \textit{dynamic constraints} that resolve this tension by adapting to the evolving capabilities of the fine-tuned model based on the insight that constraints should only intervene when degenerate outputs occur. We implement this by using a reference model as an \textit{online refiner} that takes the response from the fine-tuned model and generates a minimally corrected version which preserves correct content verbatim while fixing errors. A supervised fine-tuning loss then trains the fine-tuned model to produce the refined output. This mechanism yields a constraint that automatically strengthens or relaxes based on output quality. Experiments on dialogue and code generation show that dynamic constraints outperform both KL regularization and unconstrained baselines, achieving substantially higher task rewards while maintaining training stability.

</details>


### [26] [ARTEMIS: A Neuro Symbolic Framework for Economically Constrained Market Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18107)
*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARTEMIS是一种神经符号框架，通过结合拉普拉斯神经算子、神经随机微分方程和可微分符号瓶颈，在量化金融中实现无套利约束和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化金融中的深度学习模型通常缺乏可解释性，且未能纳入无套利约束等基本经济原理，这限制了其在金融领域的实际应用和可信度。

Method: 提出ARTEMIS框架：1) 连续时间拉普拉斯神经算子编码器；2) 通过物理信息损失正则化的神经随机微分方程；3) 可微分符号瓶颈提取可解释交易规则。通过两个新颖的正则化项强制经济合理性：Feynman-Kac PDE残差惩罚局部无套利违规，以及市场风险价格惩罚限制瞬时夏普比率。

Result: 在四个数据集上的评估显示：在DSLOB上达到64.96%的方向准确性，在Time-IMM上达到96.0%，均优于六个强基线模型。消融研究表明，移除PDE损失会使方向准确性从64.89%降至50.32%。

Conclusion: ARTEMIS通过提供可解释的、基于经济原理的预测，弥合了深度学习能力与量化金融所需透明度之间的差距，为金融领域的可解释AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models in quantitative finance often operate as black boxes, lacking interpretability and failing to incorporate fundamental economic principles such as no-arbitrage constraints. This paper introduces ARTEMIS (Arbitrage-free Representation Through Economic Models and Interpretable Symbolics), a novel neuro-symbolic framework combining a continuous-time Laplace Neural Operator encoder, a neural stochastic differential equation regularised by physics-informed losses, and a differentiable symbolic bottleneck that distils interpretable trading rules. The model enforces economic plausibility via two novel regularisation terms: a Feynman-Kac PDE residual penalising local no-arbitrage violations, and a market price of risk penalty bounding the instantaneous Sharpe ratio. We evaluate ARTEMIS against six strong baselines on four datasets: Jane Street, Optiver, Time-IMM, and DSLOB (a synthetic crash regime). Results demonstrate ARTEMIS achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy, outperforming all baselines on DSLOB (64.96%) and Time-IMM (96.0%). A comprehensive ablation study confirms each component's contribution: removing the PDE loss reduces directional accuracy from 64.89% to 50.32%. Underperformance on Optiver is attributed to its long sequence length and volatility-focused target. By providing interpretable, economically grounded predictions, ARTEMIS bridges the gap between deep learning's power and the transparency demanded in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [27] [Tula: Optimizing Time, Cost, and Generalization in Distributed Large-Batch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18112)
*Sahil Tyagi,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tula是一个自动优化卷积模型大批次训练时间、成本和收敛质量的在线服务，通过结合并行系统建模和统计性能预测来寻找最优批次大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练通过扩展节点或增加批次大小来提高每轮迭代处理的批次数量，但最大配置不一定能获得最佳性能。水平扩展引入额外通信开销，垂直扩展受计算成本和设备内存限制。简单增加批次大小会导致收益递减：训练时间和成本最初减少但最终趋于平稳，在时间/成本与批次大小的帕累托曲线上形成拐点。最优批次大小取决于底层模型、数据和可用计算资源。大批次还会因泛化差距导致模型质量下降。

Method: Tula结合并行系统建模与统计性能预测来识别最优批次大小。它是一个在线服务，自动优化卷积模型大批次训练的时间、成本和收敛质量。

Result: Tula在多个模型上预测训练时间和成本的误差在7.5-14%范围内，相比标准大批次训练，在各种视觉任务上实现了高达20倍的整体加速，并将测试准确率平均提高了9%，成功缓解了泛化差距并同时加速了训练。

Conclusion: Tula通过自动优化批次大小，有效解决了大批次训练中的时间/成本权衡和泛化差距问题，为卷积模型训练提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed training increases the number of batches processed per iteration either by scaling-out (adding more nodes) or scaling-up (increasing the batch-size). However, the largest configuration does not necessarily yield the best performance. Horizontal scaling introduces additional communication overhead, while vertical scaling is constrained by computation cost and device memory limits. Thus, simply increasing the batch-size leads to diminishing returns: training time and cost decrease initially but eventually plateaus, creating a knee-point in the time/cost versus batch-size pareto curve. The optimal batch-size therefore depends on the underlying model, data and available compute resources. Large batches also suffer from worse model quality due to the well-known generalization gap. In this paper, we present Tula, an online service that automatically optimizes time, cost, and convergence quality for large-batch training of convolutional models. It combines parallel-systems modeling with statistical performance prediction to identify the optimal batch-size. Tula predicts training time and cost within 7.5-14% error across multiple models, and achieves up to 20x overall speedup and improves test accuracy by 9% on average over standard large-batch training on various vision tasks, thus successfully mitigating the generalization gap and accelerating training at the same time.

</details>


### [28] [Signals of Success and Struggle: Early Prediction and Physiological Signatures of Human Performance across Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18798)
*Yufei Cao,Penny Sweetser,Ziyu Chen,Xuanying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用早期眼动和心电信号预测用户在游戏任务中的后期表现，融合模型达到0.86平衡准确率，揭示了高低表现者的生理机制差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻性预测用户表现对于及时识别任务困难用户至关重要。虽然眼动和心电信号广泛用于表征与表现相关的视觉行为和生理激活，但它们在早期预测和揭示表现差异的生理机制方面的潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 在自然复杂度展开的游戏环境中进行被试内实验，使用早期眼动和心电信号预测后期表现，并检查生理和自我报告的组间差异。开发了眼动-心电融合模型和仅眼动模型。

Result: 眼动-心电融合模型达到0.86的平衡准确率，仅眼动模型也显示出相当的预测能力。高表现者表现出目标导向的注视、调整的视觉采样、随着需求加剧更稳定的心电激活，以及更积极的情感体验。

Conclusion: 研究证明了从早期生理信号进行跨会话预测的可行性，为表现差异提供了可解释的见解，并为未来主动干预提供了基础。

Abstract: User performance is crucial in interactive systems, capturing how effectively users engage with task execution. Prospectively predicting performance enables the timely identification of users struggling with task demands. While ocular and cardiac signals are widely used to characterise performance-relevant visual behaviour and physiological activation, their potential for early prediction and for revealing the physiological mechanisms underlying performance differences remains underexplored. We conducted a within-subject experiment in a game environment with naturally unfolding complexity, using early ocular and cardiac signals to predict later performance and to examine physiological and self-reported group differences. Results show that the ocular-cardiac fusion model achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.86, and the ocular-only model shows comparable predictive power. High performers exhibited targeted gaze and adjusted visual sampling, and sustained more stable cardiac activation as demands intensified, with a more positive affective experience. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of cross-session prediction from early physiology, providing interpretable insights into performance variation and facilitating future proactive intervention.

</details>


### [29] [VC-Soup: Value-Consistency Guided Multi-Value Alignment for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18113)
*Hefei Xu,Le Wu,Yu Wang,Min Hou,Han Wu,Zhen Zhang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VC-soup：基于价值一致性的数据过滤与参数融合框架，通过筛选高一致性偏好对、训练平滑策略模型，并线性组合策略来实现多价值对齐，有效缓解价值冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在内容生成、交互和决策中的广泛应用，使其与人类价值观对齐成为可信AI的核心目标。多价值对齐面临两大挑战：1) 为每种价值组合单独训练模型成本过高；2) 价值冲突显著降低对齐性能。现有方法难以在不同人类价值观间实现有利权衡。

Method: 提出VC-soup框架：1) 设计基于余弦相似度的价值一致性度量，量化偏好对的跨价值一致性；2) 过滤各价值数据集中低一致性偏好对；3) 在剩余数据上训练获得平滑、价值一致的策略模型；4) 线性组合这些策略模型，并应用跨价值的帕累托过滤以获得平衡的多价值性能解决方案。

Result: 大量实验和理论分析表明，VC-soup能有效缓解价值冲突，在多项指标上持续优于现有的多价值对齐方法，实现了更好的跨价值权衡。

Conclusion: VC-soup从数据价值一致性的角度重新审视多价值对齐问题，通过数据过滤和参数融合框架，显著提升了多价值对齐的性能和效率，为解决大语言模型与复杂人类价值观对齐问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape content generation, interaction, and decision-making across the Web, aligning them with human values has become a central objective in trustworthy AI. This challenge becomes even more pronounced when aligning multiple, potentially conflicting human values. Although recent approaches, such as reward reweighting, prompt-based supervised fine-tuning, and model merging, attempt to tackle multi-value alignment, they still face two major limitations: (1) training separate models for each value combination is prohibitively expensive; (2) value conflicts substantially degrade alignment performance. These limitations make it difficult to achieve favorable trade-offs across diverse human values. To address these challenges, we revisit multi-value alignment from the perspective of value consistency in data and propose VC-soup, a data filtering and parameter merging framework grounded in value-consistent learning. We first design a value consistency metric based on the cosine similarity between the reward-gap vector of each preference pair and an all-ones vector, which quantifies its cross-value coherence. We then filter out low-consistency preference pairs in each value dataset and train on the remaining data to obtain smooth, value-consistent policy models that better preserve linear mode connectivity. Finally, we linearly combine these policies and apply Pareto filtering across values to obtain solutions with balanced multi-value performance. Extensive experiments and theoretical analysis demonstrate that VC-soup effectively mitigates conflicts and consistently outperforms existing multi-value alignment methods.

</details>


### [30] [LLM-Augmented Computational Phenotyping of Long Covid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18115)
*Jing Wang,Jie Shen,Amar Sra,Qiaomin Xie,Jeremy C Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM增强的计算表型框架"Grace Cycle"，通过迭代假设生成、证据提取和特征精炼，从纵向患者数据中发现临床有意义的亚组。在13,511名长新冠参与者中识别出三种不同临床表型：保护型、响应型和难治型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长新冠作为一种复杂持久的疾病，其临床亚表型仍不清楚。表型特征对于理解慢性疾病的异质性和指导个性化干预至关重要，但目前缺乏有效的方法从复杂的纵向数据中发现临床有意义的亚组。

Method: 提出"Grace Cycle"框架，这是一个LLM增强的计算表型方法，通过迭代整合三个关键步骤：假设生成、证据提取和特征精炼。该框架从纵向患者数据中系统性地发现临床亚表型，并采用统计验证确保结果的可靠性。

Result: 在13,511名长新冠参与者中识别出三种不同的临床表型：保护型、响应型和难治型。这些表型在峰值症状严重程度、基线疾病负担和纵向剂量-反应模式方面表现出显著分离，并在多个独立维度上获得强有力的统计支持。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何将大语言模型整合到基于原则、统计基础的表型筛选流程中，用于处理复杂的纵向数据。提出的框架是疾病无关的，为发现临床可解释的亚表型提供了一种通用方法。

Abstract: Phenotypic characterization is essential for understanding heterogeneity in chronic diseases and for guiding personalized interventions. Long COVID, a complex and persistent condition, yet its clinical subphenotypes remain poorly understood. In this work, we propose an LLM-augmented computational phenotyping framework ``Grace Cycle'' that iteratively integrates hypothesis generation, evidence extraction, and feature refinement to discover clinically meaningful subgroups from longitudinal patient data. The framework identifies three distinct clinical phenotypes, Protected, Responder, and Refractory, based on 13,511 Long Covid participants. These phenotypes exhibit pronounced separation in peak symptom severity, baseline disease burden, and longitudinal dose-response patterns, with strong statistical support across multiple independent dimensions.
  This study illustrates how large language models can be integrated into a principled, statistically grounded pipeline for phenotypic screening from complex longitudinal data. Note that the proposed framework is disease-agnostic and offers a general approach for discovering clinically interpretable subphenotypes.

</details>


### [31] [R2-Dreamer: Redundancy-Reduced World Models without Decoders or Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18202)
*Naoki Morihira,Amal Nahar,Kartik Bharadwaj,Yasuhiro Kato,Akinobu Hayashi,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: R2-Dreamer是一种基于模型的强化学习框架，通过自监督冗余减少目标作为内部正则化器，避免表示崩溃，无需数据增强，在视觉任务中实现高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的模型强化学习中，现有方法存在局限性：基于重建的方法在无关视觉细节上浪费容量，而无解码器方法依赖外部数据增强正则化器，限制了方法的通用性。需要一种既能学习鲁棒表示又不依赖外部正则化的方法。

Method: 提出R2-Dreamer框架，采用无解码器架构，核心是基于Barlow Twins启发的冗余减少自监督目标作为内部正则化器。该方法可轻松集成到现有框架中，防止表示崩溃而不依赖数据增强。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和Meta-World基准测试中，R2-Dreamer与DreamerV3和TD-MPC2等强基线竞争，训练速度比DreamerV3快1.59倍。在DMC-Subtle（包含微小任务相关对象）上获得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的内部正则化器能够实现通用、高性能的无解码器模型强化学习。R2-Dreamer展示了自监督冗余减少目标作为内部正则化的潜力，为视觉强化学习提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: A central challenge in image-based Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) is to learn representations that distill essential information from irrelevant visual details. While promising, reconstruction-based methods often waste capacity on large task-irrelevant regions. Decoder-free methods instead learn robust representations by leveraging Data Augmentation (DA), but reliance on such external regularizers limits versatility. We propose R2-Dreamer, a decoder-free MBRL framework with a self-supervised objective that serves as an internal regularizer, preventing representation collapse without resorting to DA. The core of our method is a redundancy-reduction objective inspired by Barlow Twins, which can be easily integrated into existing frameworks. On DeepMind Control Suite and Meta-World, R2-Dreamer is competitive with strong baselines such as DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 while training 1.59x faster than DreamerV3, and yields substantial gains on DMC-Subtle with tiny task-relevant objects. These results suggest that an effective internal regularizer can enable versatile, high-performance decoder-free MBRL. Code is available at https://github.com/NM512/r2dreamer.

</details>


### [32] [Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling Improves Rollout Accuracy in PDE Surrogate Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18237)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GITS是一种针对神经模拟器的数据采样方法，通过联合优化引导模型局部梯度和集合级时间覆盖，在相同预算下比均匀采样获得更好的rollout精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经模拟器训练通常使用均匀采样的数值模拟数据，但如何在相同预算下采样训练数据以最大化rollout精度仍是一个未形式化的基本问题。现有采样方法要么倾向于坍缩到局部高信息密度区域，要么保持多样性但模型特异性不足，性能往往不优于均匀采样。

Method: 提出梯度感知时间采样（GITS）方法，联合优化引导模型局部梯度和集合级时间覆盖，有效平衡模型特异性和动力学信息。该方法专门针对神经模拟器设计。

Result: 与多个采样基线相比，GITS选择的数据在多个PDE系统、模型骨干和采样比例下实现了更低的rollout误差。消融研究证明了两个优化目标的必要性和互补性。

Conclusion: GITS是一种有效的神经模拟器数据采样方法，能够平衡模型特异性和动力学信息。研究还分析了GITS的成功采样模式以及在某些典型PDE系统和模型骨干上失败的情况。

Abstract: Researchers train neural simulators on uniformly sampled numerical simulation data. But under the same budget, does systematically sampled data provide the most effective information? A fundamental yet unformalized problem is how to sample training data for neural simulators so as to maximize rollout accuracy. Existing data sampling methods either tend to collapse into locally high-information-density regions, or preserve diversity but remain insufficiently model-specific, often leading to performance that is no better than uniform sampling. To address this, we propose a data sampling method tailored to neural simulators, Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling (GITS). GITS jointly optimizes pilot-model local gradients and set-level temporal coverage, thereby effectively balancing model specificity and dynamical information. Compared with multiple sampling baselines, the data selected by GITS achieves lower rollout error across multiple PDE systems, model backbones and sample ratios. Furthermore, ablation studies demonstrate the necessity and complementarity of the two optimization objectives in GITS. In addition, we analyze the successful sampling patterns of GITS as well as the typical PDE systems and model backbones on which GITS fails.

</details>


### [33] [AGRI-Fidelity: Evaluating the Reliability of Listenable Explanations for Poultry Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18247)
*Sindhuja Madabushi,Arda Dogan,Jonathan Liu,Dian Chen,Dong S. Ha,Sook Shin,Sam H. Noh,Jin-Hee Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AGRI-Fidelity框架，用于评估家禽疾病检测中可听解释的可靠性，解决现有XAI指标忽略模型多重性和静态伪影的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI指标仅针对单一模型评估忠实性，忽略了模型多重性（即多个接近最优的分类器可能依赖不同或虚假的声学线索）。在嘈杂农场环境中，静态伪影（如通风噪声）可能产生忠实但不可靠的解释，而基于掩蔽的指标无法惩罚冗余捷径。

Method: 提出AGRI-Fidelity框架，结合跨模型共识和循环时间置换构建零分布，计算错误发现率（FDR），以抑制静态伪影同时保留时间局部化的生物声学标记。该方法无需空间真实标注。

Result: 在真实和受控数据集上，AGRI-Fidelity相比基于掩蔽的指标，能够有效为所有数据点提供可靠性感知的区分能力。

Conclusion: AGRI-Fidelity为家禽疾病检测中的可听解释提供了一个可靠性导向的评估框架，能够处理模型多重性和环境噪声带来的挑战，提高解释的可靠性评估。

Abstract: Existing XAI metrics measure faithfulness for a single model, ignoring model multiplicity where near-optimal classifiers rely on different or spurious acoustic cues. In noisy farm environments, stationary artifacts such as ventilation noise can produce explanations that are faithful yet unreliable, as masking-based metrics fail to penalize redundant shortcuts. We propose AGRI-Fidelity, a reliability-oriented evaluation framework for listenable explanations in poultry disease detection without spatial ground truth. The method combines cross-model consensus with cyclic temporal permutation to construct null distributions and compute a False Discovery Rate (FDR), suppressing stationary artifacts while preserving time-localized bioacoustic markers. Across real and controlled datasets, AGRI-Fidelity effectively provides reliability-aware discrimination for all data points versus masking-based metrics.

</details>


### [34] [MolRGen: A Training and Evaluation Setting for De Novo Molecular Generation with Reasonning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18256)
*Philippe Formont,Maxime Darrin,Ismail Ben Ayed,Pablo Piantanida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolRGen是一个用于训练和评估基于推理的大语言模型在从头分子生成任务上的大规模基准和数据集，填补了现有方法需要监督标签的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的LLMs在药物发现和分子设计中的应用大多需要监督标签（如已知性质修饰的分子对），而在从头分子生成任务中缺乏这样的监督数据，需要新的基准来训练和评估模型。

Method: 1) 提出用于从头分子生成和性质预测的评估和训练设置；2) 引入新颖的多样性感知top-k分数，同时捕捉生成分子的质量和多样性；3) 使用强化学习训练24B参数的LLM用于分子生成。

Result: 建立了MolRGen基准和数据集，展示了该设置可用于训练LLMs进行分子生成，并对训练得到的24B LLM进行了详细的性能分析和局限性评估。

Conclusion: MolRGen填补了从头分子生成任务中缺乏无监督训练基准的空白，为基于推理的LLMs在药物发现领域的应用提供了重要的评估框架和训练资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning-based large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated substantial improvements in complex problem-solving tasks. Motivated by these advances, several works have explored the application of reasoning LLMs to drug discovery and molecular design. However, most existing approaches either focus on evaluation or rely on training setups that require ground-truth labels, such as molecule pairs with known property modifications. Such supervision is unavailable in \textit{de novo} molecular generation, where the objective is to generate novel molecules that optimize a desirability score without prior knowledge of high-scoring candidates. To bridge this gap, we introduce MolRGen, a large-scale benchmark and dataset for training and evaluating reasoning-based LLMs on \textit{de novo} molecular generation. Our contributions are threefold. First, we propose a setting to evaluate and train models for \textit{de novo} molecular generation and property prediction. Second, we introduce a novel diversity-aware top-$k$ score that captures both the quality and diversity of generated molecules. Third, we show our setting can be used to train LLMs for molecular generation, training a 24B LLM with reinforcement learning, and we provide a detailed analysis of its performance and limitations.

</details>


### [35] [Discovering What You Can Control: Interventional Boundary Discovery for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18257)
*Jiaxin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IBD是一种无需学习模型的因果特征选择方法，通过干预测试识别智能体动作真正影响的观测维度，解决混杂干扰特征问题，在12个连续控制任务中表现接近Oracle性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在混杂干扰特征的情况下，仅凭观测统计无法可靠区分与动作相关的维度和动作真正影响的维度。传统特征选择方法可能选择混杂干扰特征而丢弃真正的因果维度，导致强化学习性能下降。

Method: 提出干预边界发现(IBD)方法，应用Pearl的do-operator对智能体自身动作进行干预，使用双样本测试生成可解释的二进制掩码来标识观测维度。该方法无需学习模型，可作为预处理步骤与任何下游RL算法组合使用。

Result: 在12个连续控制设置中（最多100个干扰维度）：1）观测特征选择会主动选择混杂干扰特征并丢弃真正的因果维度；2）当干扰特征数量超过相关特征约3:1时，全状态RL性能急剧下降；3）IBD在所有干扰水平下都接近Oracle性能，在SAC和TD3算法上都有性能提升。

Conclusion: IBD通过因果干预方法有效解决了混杂干扰特征问题，能够可靠识别智能体的因果影响范围，为强化学习提供了一种鲁棒的特征选择预处理方法。

Abstract: Selecting relevant state dimensions in the presence of confounded distractors is a causal identification problem: observational statistics alone cannot reliably distinguish dimensions that correlate with actions from those that actions cause. We formalize this as discovering the agent's Causal Sphere of Influence and propose Interventional Boundary Discovery IBD, which applies Pearl's do-operator to the agent's own actions and uses two-sample testing to produce an interpretable binary mask over observation dimensions. IBD requires no learned models and composes with any downstream RL algorithm as a preprocessing step. Across 12 continuous control settings with up to 100 distractor dimensions, we find that: (1) observational feature selection can actively select confounded distractors while discarding true causal dimensions; (2) full-state RL degrades sharply once distractors outnumber relevant features by roughly 3:1 in our benchmarks; and (3)IBD closely tracks oracle performance across all distractor levels tested, with gains transferring across SAC and TD3.

</details>


### [36] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Logit Space Efficiently Enhances Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18258)
*Haocheng Luo,Zehang Deng,Thanh-Toan Do,Mehrtash Harandi,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出logits-SAM方法解决DPO训练中的squeezing effect问题，通过扰动输出层logits来抑制高曲率方向上的残差膨胀，提升DPO效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO虽然简单稳定，但存在squeezing effect问题，即训练过程中偏好响应的概率会意外下降。需要理解并缓解这一现象以改进DPO算法

Method: 建立logit空间的坐标动态理论框架，分析发现负梯度更新导致高曲率方向残差快速膨胀，而SAM的曲率正则化能抑制此行为。提出logits-SAM变体，仅扰动输出层，计算开销极小

Result: 在Pythia-2.8B、Mistral-7B和Gemma-2B-IT模型上，跨多个数据集和基准测试，logits-SAM一致提升DPO效果，并能无缝集成到其他DPO变体中

Conclusion: logits-SAM通过曲率正则化有效缓解DPO的squeezing effect问题，计算高效且兼容性强，为改进基于偏好的语言模型对齐提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a popular algorithm for aligning pretrained large language models with human preferences, owing to its simplicity and training stability. However, DPO suffers from the recently identified squeezing effect (also known as likelihood displacement), where the probability of preferred responses decreases unintentionally during training. To understand and mitigate this phenomenon, we develop a theoretical framework that models the coordinate-wise dynamics in logit space. Our analysis reveals that negative-gradient updates cause residuals to expand rapidly along high-curvature directions, which underlies the squeezing effect, whereas Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) can suppress this behavior through its curvature-regularization effect. Building on this insight, we investigate logits-SAM, a computationally efficient variant that perturbs only the output layer with negligible overhead. Extensive experiments on Pythia-2.8B, Mistral-7B, and Gemma-2B-IT across multiple datasets and benchmarks demonstrate that logits-SAM consistently improves the effectiveness of DPO and integrates seamlessly with other DPO variants. Code is available at https://github.com/RitianLuo/logits-sam-dpo.

</details>


### [37] [Enactor: From Traffic Simulators to Surrogate World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18266)
*Yash Ranjan,Rahul Sengupta,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer架构的交通参与者生成模型，通过World Model范式捕捉参与者交互和路口几何特征，生成物理一致的长时程轨迹，在仿真环境中验证了性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通微观仿真器的行为模型过于简化，无法捕捉真实的参与者交互；现有深度学习方法虽能学习交互但无法生成长期物理一致的轨迹，且未专门处理交通路口这一关键场景的复杂动态

Method: 采用World Model范式，构建基于Transformer的参与者中心生成模型，同时捕捉参与者-参与者交互和理解交通路口几何特征；在"仿真在环"设置中测试，使用SUMO生成初始条件，模型控制参与者动态

Result: 模型能有效捕捉复杂参与者交互，生成长时程物理一致的轨迹，所需训练样本显著少于传统方法；在交通相关和聚合指标上优于基线，KL散度指标超过基线10倍以上

Conclusion: 提出的基于Transformer的World Model框架能够生成物理一致的长时程交通轨迹，有效解决传统仿真器行为模型简化和现有深度学习方法长期一致性不足的问题

Abstract: Traffic microsimulators are widely used to evaluate road network performance under various ``what-if" conditions. However, the behavior models controlling the actions of the actors are overly simplistic and fails to capture realistic actor-actor interactions. Deep learning-based methods have been applied to model vehicles and pedestrians as ``agents" responding to their surrounding ``environment" (including lanes, signals, and neighboring agents). Although effective in learning actor-actor interaction, these approaches fail to generate physically consistent trajectories over long time periods, and they do not explicitly address the complex dynamics that arise at traffic intersections which is a critical location in urban networks. Inspired by the World Model paradigm, we have developed an actor centric generative model using transformer-based architecture that is able to capture the actor-actor interaction, at the same time understanding the geometry to the traffic intersection to generate physically grounded trajectories that are based on learned behavior. Moreover, we test the model in a live ``simulation-in-the-loop" setting, where we generate the initial conditions of the actors using SUMO and then let the model control the dynamics of the actors. We let the simulation run for 40000 timesteps (4000 seconds), testing the performance of the model on long timerange and evaluating the trajectories on traffic engineering related metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively captures complex actor-actor interactions and generates long-horizon, physically consistent trajectories, while requiring significantly fewer training samples than traditional agent-centric generative approaches. Our model is able to outperform the baseline in traffic related as well as aggregate metrics where our model beats the baseline by more than 10x on the KL-Divergence.

</details>


### [38] [Detection Is Cheap, Routing Is Learned: Why Refusal-Based Alignment Evaluation Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18280)
*Gregory N. Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出对齐评估应关注概念检测到行为策略的路由机制，而非仅检测概念或拒绝行为。通过研究中文语言模型的政治审查，发现探测准确率不可靠，手术式消融揭示实验室特定路由，拒绝机制不再是主要审查方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估主要关注模型是否编码危险概念和是否拒绝有害请求，但忽略了对齐操作的核心层面：从概念检测到行为策略的路由机制。论文旨在研究这一被忽视的中间层，以政治审查作为自然实验来揭示对齐的实际运作方式。

Method: 使用探测、手术式消融和行为测试三种方法，分析来自五个实验室的九个开源权重模型。通过政治敏感性探测、零控制、排列基线等方法评估概念检测；通过手术式消融特定方向研究路由机制；通过行为测试评估审查表现。

Result: 1) 探测准确率单独不可靠：政治探测、零控制和排列基线都能达到100%，只有保留类别泛化测试有效；2) 手术式消融揭示实验室特定路由：移除政治敏感性方向能消除审查并恢复准确事实输出，但一个模型因架构纠缠而产生幻觉；3) 拒绝机制不再是主导审查方式：在一个模型家族中，硬拒绝降至零而叙事引导升至最大，使审查对仅测试拒绝的基准不可见。

Conclusion: 提出三阶段描述框架：检测、路由、生成。模型通常保留相关知识，对齐改变知识的表达方式。仅审计检测或拒绝的评估会错失最直接影响行为的路由机制，需要更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Current alignment evaluation mostly measures whether models encode dangerous concepts and whether they refuse harmful requests. Both miss the layer where alignment often operates: routing from concept detection to behavioral policy. We study political censorship in Chinese-origin language models as a natural experiment, using probes, surgical ablations, and behavioral tests across nine open-weight models from five labs. Three findings follow. First, probe accuracy alone is non-diagnostic: political probes, null controls, and permutation baselines can all reach 100%, so held-out category generalization is the informative test. Second, surgical ablation reveals lab-specific routing. Removing the political-sensitivity direction eliminates censorship and restores accurate factual output in most models tested, while one model confabulates because its architecture entangles factual knowledge with the censorship mechanism. Cross-model transfer fails, indicating that routing geometry is model- and lab-specific. Third, refusal is no longer the dominant censorship mechanism. Within one model family, hard refusal falls to zero while narrative steering rises to the maximum, making censorship invisible to refusal-only benchmarks. These results support a three-stage descriptive framework: detect, route, generate. Models often retain the relevant knowledge; alignment changes how that knowledge is expressed. Evaluations that audit only detection or refusal therefore miss the routing mechanism that most directly determines behavior.

</details>


### [39] [On Additive Gaussian Processes for Wind Farm Power Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18281)
*Simon M. Brealy,Lawrence A. Bull,Daniel S. Brennan,Pauline Beltrando,Anders Sommer,Nikolaos Dervilis,Keith Worden*

TL;DR: 该论文探索使用加性高斯过程在风电场数据集上揭示涡轮机特定和风电场级功率模型的变异，以支持基于群体的结构健康监测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于群体的结构健康监测旨在在相似机器或结构之间共享信息。本文从群体层面视角出发，探索如何利用加性高斯过程揭示风电场中涡轮机特定和风电场级功率模型的变化，以更好地理解风电场发电模式

Method: 使用加性高斯过程对收集的风电场数据集进行分析，建立能够同时捕捉涡轮机特定变异和风电场级整体模式的功率模型

Result: 预测结果显示了风电场发电的模式，这些模式符合直观理解，能够为更明智的控制和决策制定提供支持

Conclusion: 加性高斯过程能够有效揭示风电场功率模型中的涡轮机特定和群体级变异，为基于群体的结构健康监测提供有价值的工具，支持更优化的风电场运营决策

Abstract: Population-based Structural Health Monitoring (PBSHM) aims to share information between similar machines or structures. This paper takes a population-level perspective, exploring the use of additive Gaussian processes to reveal variations in turbine-specific and farm-level power models over a collected wind farm dataset. The predictions illustrate patterns in wind farm power generation, which follow intuition and should enable more informed control and decision-making.

</details>


### [40] [Path-Constrained Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18297)
*Zijin Gu,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Vimal Thilak,Jason Ramapuram,Navdeep Jaitly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathMoE通过跨层共享路由器参数来约束专家路径空间，提高稀疏混合专家模型的统计效率和性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE路由每层独立选择专家，产生N^L条可能路径，远超训练集规模，导致统计效率低下，难以学习路径空间的有意义结构

Method: 提出PathMoE方法，在连续层之间共享路由器参数，约束专家路径空间，消除辅助负载平衡损失的需求

Result: 在0.9B和16B参数模型上，相比独立路由在困惑度和下游任务上取得一致改进；分析显示相同路径的token自然按语言功能聚类，PathMoE产生更集中的分组、更好的跨层一致性和更强的路由扰动鲁棒性

Conclusion: PathMoE通过约束专家路径空间提高了MoE架构的统计效率和性能，为通过专家路径视角理解MoE架构提供了新视角

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient scaling by activating only a subset of parameters for each input. However, conventional MoE routing selects each layer's experts independently, creating N^L possible expert paths -- for N experts across L layers. This far exceeds typical training set sizes, leading to statistical inefficiency as the model may not learn meaningful structure over such a vast path space. To constrain it, we propose \pathmoe, which shares router parameters across consecutive layers. Experiments on 0.9B and 16B parameter models demonstrate consistent improvements on perplexity and downstream tasks over independent routing, while eliminating the need for auxiliary load balancing losses. Analysis reveals that tokens following the same path naturally cluster by linguistic function, with \pathmoe{} producing more concentrated groups, better cross-layer consistency, and greater robustness to routing perturbations. These results offer a new perspective for understanding MoE architectures through the lens of expert paths.

</details>


### [41] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum I: Provable Benefits of Autocurriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18325)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miro Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan J. Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明通过自课程学习（autocurriculum）可以显著降低思维链推理模型的训练成本，在监督微调和强化学习中都实现了指数级效率提升。


<details>
