Abstract: In recent years, machine learning models have been increasingly applied to spectroscopic datasets for chemical and biomedical analysis. For their successful adoption, particularly in clinical and safety-critical settings, professionals and researchers must be able to understand and trust the reasoning behind model predictions. However, the inherently high dimensionality and strong collinearity of spectroscopy data pose a fundamental challenge to model explainability. These properties not only complicate model training but also undermine the stability and consistency of explanations, leading to fluctuations in feature importance across repeated training runs. Feature extraction techniques have been used to reduce the input dimensionality; these new features hinder the connection between the prediction and the original signal. This study proposes SHAPCA, an explainable machine learning pipeline that combines Principal Component Analysis (for dimensionality reduction) and Shapely Additive exPlanations (for post hoc explanation) to provide explanations in the original input space, which a practitioner can interpret and link back to the biological components. The proposed framework enables analysis from both global and local perspectives, revealing the spectral bands that drive overall model behaviour as well as the instance-specific features that influence individual predictions. Numerical analysis demonstrated the interpretability of the results and greater consistency across different runs.

</details>


### [82] [DyMoE: Dynamic Expert Orchestration with Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19172)
*Yuegui Huang,Zhiyuan Fang,Weiqi Luo,Ruoyu Wu,Wuhui Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyMoE是一个动态混合精度量化框架，通过重要性感知优先级、深度自适应调度和前瞻预取技术，显著提升MoE模型在边缘设备上的推理性能，减少内存占用和I/O开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘平台实时推理面临内存占用大和I/O开销高的挑战，现有静态方法在延迟-精度权衡上过于僵化，且观察到专家重要性存在高度偏斜和深度依赖性。

Method: 提出DyMoE框架，包含三个关键技术：1) 重要性感知优先级：运行时动态量化专家；2) 深度自适应调度：保护关键层的语义完整性；3) 前瞻预取：重叠I/O停滞时间。

Result: 在商用边缘硬件上，DyMoE将首词时间(TTFT)减少3.44-22.7倍，每输出词时间(TPOT)最高加速14.58倍，相比最先进的卸载基线方法，实现了实时且保持精度的MoE推理。

Conclusion: DyMoE通过动态混合精度量化有效解决了MoE模型在资源受限边缘设备上的推理挑战，实现了实时、高精度的边缘推理，为边缘AI应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the computational efficiency of MoE models, the excessive memory footprint and I/O overhead inherent in multi-expert architectures pose formidable challenges for real-time inference on resource-constrained edge platforms. While existing static methods struggle with a rigid latency-accuracy trade-off, we observe that expert importance is highly skewed and depth-dependent. Motivated by these insights, we propose DyMoE, a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework designed for high-performance edge inference. Leveraging insights into expert importance skewness and depth-dependent sensitivity, DyMoE introduces: (1) importance-aware prioritization to dynamically quantize experts at runtime; (2) depth-adaptive scheduling to preserve semantic integrity in critical layers; and (3) look-ahead prefetching to overlap I/O stalls. Experimental results on commercial edge hardware show that DyMoE reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by 3.44x-22.7x and up to a 14.58x speedup in Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) compared to state-of-the-art offloading baselines, enabling real-time, accuracy-preserving MoE inference on resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [83] [MIDST Challenge at SaTML 2025: Membership Inference over Diffusion-models-based Synthetic Tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19185)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Xi He,Mahshid Alinoori,John Jewell,Sana Ayromlou,Wei Pang,Veronica Chatrath,Garui Sharma,Deval Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIDST挑战赛评估扩散模型生成的合成表格数据在成员推理攻击下的隐私保护效果，开发了针对扩散模型的黑盒和白盒攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据常被视为数据匿名化和隐私保护数据发布的理想解决方案，但扩散模型生成的表格数据在隐私攻击（特别是成员推理攻击）下的实际保护效果尚未得到充分探索。

Method: MIDST挑战赛对扩散模型生成的合成表格数据进行定量隐私评估，重点关注其对成员推理攻击的抵抗能力。针对表格数据的异质性和复杂性，探索了多种目标模型，包括混合数据类型的单表扩散模型和具有互连约束的多关系表扩散模型。开发了专门针对这些目标扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法。

Result: MIDST挑战赛的主要成果是开发了针对扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法，这些方法能够全面评估扩散模型生成合成表格数据的隐私保护效果。相关代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 虽然扩散模型生成的合成数据在理论上具有隐私保护潜力，但需要通过专门的攻击方法（如MIDST开发的成员推理攻击）进行实证评估，以确定其在实际应用中的隐私保护效果。

Abstract: Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing. Drawn from generative models like diffusion models, synthetic data is expected to preserve the statistical properties of the original dataset while remaining resilient to privacy attacks. Recent developments of diffusion models have been effective on a wide range of data types, but their privacy resilience, particularly for tabular formats, remains largely unexplored. MIDST challenge sought a quantitative evaluation of the privacy gain of synthetic tabular data generated by diffusion models, with a specific focus on its resistance to membership inference attacks (MIAs). Given the heterogeneity and complexity of tabular data, multiple target models were explored for MIAs, including diffusion models for single tables of mixed data types and multi-relational tables with interconnected constraints. MIDST inspired the development of novel black-box and white-box MIAs tailored to these target diffusion models as a key outcome, enabling a comprehensive evaluation of their privacy efficacy. The MIDST GitHub repository is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST

</details>


### [84] [Improving RCT-Based Treatment Effect Estimation Under Covariate Mismatch via Calibrated Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19186)
*Amir Asiaee,Samhita Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM方法通过嵌入对齐解决RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，避免插补，在非线性CATE估计中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验（RCT）是估计异质性治疗效应的金标准，但通常缺乏检测效应异质性的统计功效。大型观察性研究（OS）可以补充RCT进行条件平均治疗效应（CATE）估计，但关键障碍是协变量不匹配：两种来源测量不同且仅部分重叠的协变量。

Method: 提出CALM（协变量不匹配下的校准对齐）方法，通过学习嵌入将每个数据源的特征映射到共同的表示空间。将OS结果模型转移到RCT嵌入空间，并使用试验数据进行校准，同时保持随机化带来的因果识别性。

Result: 有限样本风险界限分解为对齐误差、结果模型复杂度和校准复杂度项，确定了嵌入对齐何时优于插补。在线性变体中提供负迁移保护；神经变体在严重分布偏移下可能脆弱。在稀疏线性模型下，嵌入方法严格泛化了插补。51个设置的模拟证实：（1）基于校准的方法在线性CATE中效果相当；（2）神经嵌入变体在所有22个非线性设置中以较大优势胜出。

Conclusion: CALM通过嵌入对齐有效解决了RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，避免了插补的局限性，在非线性CATE估计中表现突出，为结合两种数据源进行异质性治疗效应估计提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity. Large observational studies (OS) can supplement RCTs for conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation, but a key barrier is covariate mismatch: the two sources measure different, only partially overlapping, covariates. We propose CALM (Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch), which bypasses imputation by learning embeddings that map each source's features into a common representation space. OS outcome models are transferred to the RCT embedding space and calibrated using trial data, preserving causal identification from randomization. Finite-sample risk bounds decompose into alignment error, outcome-model complexity, and calibration complexity terms, identifying when embedding alignment outperforms imputation. Under the calibration-based linear variant, the framework provides protection against negative transfer; the neural variant can be vulnerable under severe distributional shift. Under sparse linear models, the embedding approach strictly generalizes imputation. Simulations across 51 settings confirm that (i) calibration-based methods are equivalent for linear CATEs, and (ii) the neural embedding variant wins all 22 nonlinear-regime settings with large margins.

</details>


### [85] [Robustness, Cost, and Attack-Surface Concentration in Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19204)
*Julian Allagan,Mohamed Elbakary,Zohreh Safari,Weizheng Gao,Gabrielle Morgan,Essence Morgan,Vladimir Deriglazov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究钓鱼网站检测器在对抗性特征操纵下的鲁棒性，发现模型鲁棒性主要受特征经济性而非模型复杂度影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测器在独立同分布评估下能达到接近完美的准确率，但在实际部署中面临攻击者特征操纵的威胁。研究旨在评估检测器在预算约束的特征编辑攻击下的鲁棒性差距

Method: 提出成本感知的规避框架，建模离散、单调的特征编辑和明确的攻击者预算。引入三个诊断指标：最小规避成本(MEC)、规避生存率S(B)和鲁棒性集中指数(RCI)。在UCI钓鱼网站基准数据集上评估逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树和XGBoost等模型

Result: 所有模型在静态评估下AUC≥0.979，但在预算约束的规避攻击下，鲁棒性在不同架构间收敛：完整特征下中位MEC为2，超过80%的最小成本规避集中在三个低成本表面特征。特征限制仅在移除所有主导低成本转换时才能提高鲁棒性

Conclusion: 钓鱼检测的对抗鲁棒性主要由特征经济性而非模型复杂度决定。如果正确检测的钓鱼实例中有正比例可通过最小成本c_min的单个特征转换实现规避，则任何分类器都无法在不修改特征表示或成本模型的情况下提高相应的MEC分位数

Abstract: Phishing detectors built on engineered website features attain near-perfect accuracy under i.i.d.\ evaluation, yet deployment security depends on robustness to post-deployment feature manipulation. We study this gap through a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete, monotone feature edits under explicit attacker budgets. Three diagnostics are introduced: minimal evasion cost (MEC), the evasion survival rate $S(B)$, and the robustness concentration index (RCI).
  On the UCI Phishing Websites benchmark (11\,055 instances, 30 ternary features), Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost all achieve $\mathrm{AUC}\ge 0.979$ under static evaluation. Under budgeted sanitization-style evasion, robustness converges across architectures: the median MEC equals 2 with full features, and over 80\% of successful minimal-cost evasions concentrate on three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction improves robustness only when it removes all dominant low-cost transitions. Under strict cost schedules, infrastructure-leaning feature sets exhibit 17-19\% infeasible mass for ensemble models, while the median MEC among evadable instances remains unchanged. We formalize this convergence: if a positive fraction of correctly detected phishing instances admit evasion through a single feature transition of minimal cost $c_{\min}$, no classifier can raise the corresponding MEC quantile above $c_{\min}$ without modifying the feature representation or cost model. Adversarial robustness in phishing detection is governed by feature economics rather than model complexity.

</details>


### [86] [Online Learning and Equilibrium Computation with Ranking Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19221)
*Mingyang Liu,Yongshan Chen,Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种仅通过排名反馈而非数值效用反馈的在线学习模型，分析了瞬时效用排名和时间平均效用排名两种机制下的遗憾界限，并提出了在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的次线性遗憾算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习算法依赖数值效用反馈，但在人机交互应用和隐私保护场景中，数值反馈可能不可用或受限。本文旨在研究仅通过排名反馈进行在线学习的可行性，这种反馈机制更符合实际应用中的限制条件。

Method: 研究两种排名反馈机制：1）基于当前时间步瞬时效用的排名；2）基于截至当前时间步时间平均效用的排名。在完全信息和赌博机反馈两种设置下，分析了外部遗憾界限。针对时间平均效用排名反馈，提出了在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的新算法。

Result: 1）瞬时效用排名反馈下一般无法实现次线性遗憾；2）在相对确定性的排名模型（如温度足够小的Plackett-Luce模型）下，时间平均效用排名反馈也无法实现次线性遗憾；3）在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下，新算法能够实现次线性遗憾；4）完全信息时间平均效用排名反馈下，无需额外假设即可实现次线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 仅通过排名反馈进行在线学习在特定条件下是可行的。当所有玩家在标准形式博弈中遵循所提算法时，重复博弈会产生近似粗相关均衡。算法在在线大型语言模型路由任务中表现出有效性，为受限反馈环境下的在线学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online learning in arbitrary, and possibly adversarial, environments has been extensively studied in sequential decision-making, and it is closely connected to equilibrium computation in game theory. Most existing online learning algorithms rely on \emph{numeric} utility feedback from the environment, which may be unavailable in human-in-the-loop applications and/or may be restricted by privacy concerns. In this paper, we study an online learning model in which the learner only observes a \emph{ranking} over a set of proposed actions at each timestep. We consider two ranking mechanisms: rankings induced by the \emph{instantaneous} utility at the current timestep, and rankings induced by the \emph{time-average} utility up to the current timestep, under both \emph{full-information} and \emph{bandit} feedback settings. Using the standard external-regret metric, we show that sublinear regret is impossible with instantaneous-utility ranking feedback in general. Moreover, when the ranking model is relatively deterministic, \emph{i.e.}, under the Plackett-Luce model with a temperature that is sufficiently small, sublinear regret is also impossible with time-average utility ranking feedback. We then develop new algorithms that achieve sublinear regret under the additional assumption that the utility sequence has sublinear total variation. Notably, for full-information time-average utility ranking feedback, this additional assumption can be removed. As a consequence, when all players in a normal-form game follow our algorithms, repeated play yields an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms in an online large-language-model routing task.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Can LLMs Reason Like Automated Theorem Provers for Rust Verification? VCoT-Bench: Evaluating via Verification Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18334)
*Zichen Xie,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VCoT-Lift框架将底层求解器推理提升为高层可读验证步骤，创建VCoT-Bench基准评估LLMs在Rust程序验证中的推理能力，发现现有模型存在严重脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将Rust验证视为黑盒，仅通过证明提示的二元通过/失败结果评估模型，无法判断模型是否真正理解验证非平凡Rust代码所需的逻辑推理。

Method: 引入VCoT-Lift框架，将底层求解器推理提升为高层、人类可读的验证步骤，通过显式的验证思维链提供细粒度评估的真实依据。基于此创建VCoT-Bench基准，包含1,988个VCoT完成任务。

Result: 评估十个最先进模型显示严重脆弱性，表明当前LLMs远未达到自动化定理证明器展现的推理能力。基准从三个正交维度衡量性能：对不同程度缺失证明的鲁棒性、跨不同证明类型的能力、对证明位置的敏感性。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够理解完整验证过程的LLMs，当前模型在Rust程序验证中的推理能力存在显著不足，VCoT-Lift和VCoT-Bench为细粒度评估提供了新框架。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly assist secure software development, their ability to meet the rigorous demands of Rust program verification remains unclear. Existing evaluations treat Rust verification as a black box, assessing models only by binary pass or fail outcomes for proof hints. This obscures whether models truly understand the logical deductions required for verifying nontrivial Rust code. To bridge this gap, we introduce VCoT-Lift, a framework that lifts low-level solver reasoning into high-level, human-readable verification steps. By exposing solver-level reasoning as an explicit Verification Chain-of-Thought, VCoT-Lift provides a concrete ground truth for fine-grained evaluation. Leveraging VCoT-Lift, we introduce VCoT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,988 VCoT completion tasks for rigorously evaluating LLMs' understanding of the entire verification process. VCoT-Bench measures performance along three orthogonal dimensions: robustness to varying degrees of missing proofs, competence across different proof types, and sensitivity to the proof locations. Evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals severe fragility, indicating that current LLMs fall well short of the reasoning capabilities exhibited by automated theorem provers.

</details>


### [88] [Where are the Hidden Gems? Applying Transformer Models for Design Discussion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18393)
*Lawrence Arkoh,Daniel Feitosa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Transformer的模型在跨领域检测软件设计讨论中的性能，发现BERT和RoBERTa具有强召回率，ChatGPT-4o-mini表现最佳，而数据增强方法效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件设计讨论广泛存在于各种开发平台，为重构和现代化提供重要信息。传统机器学习方法受限于标注数据稀缺和标注成本高，而基于Transformer的模型在此跨领域检测任务中尚未充分探索。

Method: 对先前跨领域研究进行概念性复制，扩展使用现代Transformer架构（BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M和ChatGPT-4o-mini）。在Stack Overflow上微调模型，在GitHub工件（拉取请求、问题和提交）上评估。同时评估相似词注入的数据增强方法。

Result: BERT和RoBERTa在跨领域表现出强召回率；XLNet获得更高精度但召回率较低；ChatGPT-4o-mini获得最高召回率和有竞争力的整体性能；LaMini-Flan-T5-77M作为轻量级替代方案，精度更强但性能不够平衡。相似词注入的数据增强未带来显著改进。

Conclusion: 现代语言模型在检测设计讨论方面展现出机会和权衡，不同模型在精度和召回率之间存在不同权衡，为实际应用提供了多种选择方案。

Abstract: Design decisions are at the core of software engineering and appear in Q\&A forums, mailing lists, pull requests, issue trackers, and commit messages. Design discussions spanning a project's history provide valuable information for informed decision-making, such as refactoring and software modernization. Machine learning techniques have been used to detect design decisions in natural language discussions; however, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of labeled data and the high cost of annotation. Prior work adopted cross-domain strategies with traditional classifiers, training on one domain and testing on another. Despite their success, transformer-based models, which often outperform traditional methods, remain largely unexplored in this setting. The goal of this work is to investigate the performance of transformer-based models (i.e., BERT, RoBERTa, XLNet, LaMini-Flan-T5-77M, and ChatGPT-4o-mini) for detecting design-related discussions. To this end, we conduct a conceptual replication of prior cross-domain studies while extending them with modern transformer architectures and addressing methodological issues in earlier work. The models were fine-tuned on Stack Overflow and evaluated on GitHub artifacts (i.e., pull requests, issues, and commits). BERT and RoBERTa show strong recall across domains, while XLNet achieves higher precision but lower recall. ChatGPT-4o-mini yields the highest recall and competitive overall performance, whereas LaMini-Flan-T5-77M provides a lightweight alternative with stronger precision but less balanced performance. We also evaluated similar-word injection for data augmentation, but unlike prior findings, it did not yield meaningful improvements. Overall, these results highlight both the opportunities and trade-offs of using modern language models for detecting design discussion.

</details>


### [89] [SQL-Commenter: Aligning Large Language Models for SQL Comment Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18606)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jia Xu,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Jiajia Ma,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SQL-Commenter：基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL注释生成方法，通过持续预训练、监督微调和DPO优化，显著提升复杂SQL查询的注释质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL查询理解面临复杂语法、多样连接类型和深度嵌套等挑战，缺乏充分注释严重影响代码可读性、可维护性和知识传递。现有方法存在两个主要问题：数据集不足以代表复杂真实查询，以及LLM对SQL特定语义理解不足

Method: 1. 构建包含复杂SQL查询和专家验证注释的综合数据集；2. 在大规模SQL语料上进行持续预训练以增强LLM的语法和语义理解；3. 进行监督微调；4. 引入基于人类反馈的直接偏好优化（DPO），使用偏好损失函数优化输出质量

Result: 在Spider和Bird基准测试中，SQL-Commenter显著优于现有最佳基线（Qwen3-14B），在BLEU-4、METEOR和ROUGE-L指标上分别平均提升9.29、4.99和13.23个百分点。人工评估显示生成的注释在正确性、完整性和自然性方面具有优越质量

Conclusion: SQL-Commenter通过综合数据集构建、持续预训练、监督微调和DPO优化，有效解决了SQL注释生成的挑战，显著提升了复杂SQL查询的注释质量，为SQL代码理解和维护提供了有效工具

Abstract: SQL query comprehension is a significant challenge due to complex syntax, diverse join types, and deep nesting. Many queries lack adequate comments, severely hindering code readability, maintainability, and knowledge transfer. Automated SQL comment generation faces two main challenges: limited datasets that inadequately represent complex real-world queries, and Large Language Models' (LLMs) insufficient understanding of SQL-specific semantics. Our empirical analysis shows that even after continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, LLMs struggle with complex SQL semantics, yielding inaccurate comments. To address this, we propose SQL-Commenter, an advanced method based on LLaMA-3.1-8B. We first construct a comprehensive dataset of complex SQL queries with expert-verified comments. Next, we perform continual pre-training on a large SQL corpus to enhance the LLM's syntax and semantic understanding, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Finally, we introduce Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) using human feedback. SQL-Commenter utilizes a preference-based loss function to favor preferred outputs, enhancing fine-grained semantic learning and context-dependent quality assessment. Evaluated on the Spider and Bird benchmarks, SQL-Commenter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On average, it surpasses the strongest baseline (Qwen3-14B) by 9.29, 4.99, and 13.23 percentage points on BLEU-4, METEOR, and ROUGE-L, respectively. Moreover, human evaluation demonstrates the superior quality of comments generated by SQL-Commenter in terms of correctness, completeness, and naturalness.

</details>


### [90] [Green Architectural Tactics in ML-enabled Systems: An LLM-based Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18734)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Silverio Martínez-Fernández,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析205个GitHub开源ML项目，使用基于LLM的新方法识别已知和未记录的可持续实践，发现文献中的绿色策略在实践中被采用但采用率不一，并发现了9个新的可持续实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和人工智能技术的广泛应用，其环境可持续性问题日益受到关注。ML系统的开发和部署（特别是训练和推理阶段）计算密集，能耗巨大。虽然已有研究提出了可持续实践（绿色策略）目录，但对其在实际中的采用程度以及是否存在未记录的额外策略了解有限。

Method: 本研究采用软件仓库挖掘方法，分析了GitHub上的205个开源ML项目。为了支持分析，设计了一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的新机制，能够从代码仓库中识别已知和新的可持续实践。

Result: 研究发现文献中报告的绿色策略在实践中确实被使用，但采用率各不相同。更重要的是，基于LLM的方法揭示了9个先前未记录的可持续实践。每个策略都提供了代码示例以帮助采用和集成。

Conclusion: 研究为寻求减少ML系统环境影响的从业者提供了见解，并为未来自动化检测和采用可持续实践的研究奠定了基础。研究证实了文献中绿色策略的实际应用，同时发现了新的实践，为绿色AI领域提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Context: The increasing adoption of machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies raises growing concerns about their environmental sustainability. Developing and deploying ML-enabled systems is computationally intensive, particularly during training and inference. Green AI has emerged to address these issues by promoting efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. While prior research has proposed catalogs of sustainable practices (i.e., green tactics), there remains limited understanding of their adoption in practice and whether additional, undocumented tactics exist. Objective: This study aims to investigate the extent to which existing sustainable practices are implemented in real-world ML-enabled systems and to identify previously undocumented practices that support environmental sustainability. Method: We conduct a mining software repository study on 205 open-source ML projects on GitHub. To support our analysis, we design a novel mechanism based on large language models (LLMs) capable of identifying both known and new sustainable practices from code repositories. Results: Our findings confirm that green tactics reported in the literature are used in practice, although adoption rates vary. Furthermore, our LLM-based approach reveals nine previously undocumented sustainable practices. Each tactic is supported with code examples to aid adoption and integration. Conclusions: We finally provide insights for practitioners seeking to reduce the environmental impact of ML-enabled systems and offer a foundation for future research in automating the detection and adoption of sustainable practices.

</details>


### [91] [SpaceTime Programming: Live and Omniscient Exploration of Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18735)
*Jean-Baptiste Döderlein,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher,Benoit Combemale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpaceTime Programming统一了探索式编程、实时编程和全知调试，通过跟踪代码变更和执行状态，让开发者能在空间（代码变体）和时间（执行流程）两个维度探索程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编程环境将静态代码与动态执行分离，开发者需要在编写代码和观察执行之间切换，缺乏理解代码变更与运行时行为关系的工具。现有解决方案往往只关注特定方面或单一范式，缺乏集成多种能力的完整环境。

Method: 提出SpaceTime Programming方法，核心是跟踪机制，不仅捕获执行状态，还记录对应的代码变更。作为概念验证，实现了Python库SpaceTimePy，支持两种应用：实时全知调试器和Pygame游戏开发工具。

Result: 通过Flappy Bird类游戏展示了SpaceTimePy的应用。在五个真实Python项目上评估，测试套件的性能开销在35%到150%之间。

Conclusion: SpaceTime Programming统一了多种编程范式，提供了探索代码修改和执行流程的编程模型，通过跟踪机制实现了空间和时间维度的程序探索。

Abstract: Programming environments typically separate the world of static code from the dynamic execution of programs. Developers must switch between writing code and observing its execution, often with limited tools to understand the relationship between code changes and runtime behavior. Several paradigms and approaches exist to bridge this gap, including exploratory programming for comparing code variants, live programming for immediate feedback, and omniscient debugging for exploring execution history. However, existing solutions tend to focus on specific aspects and one specific paradigm rather than providing a fully integrated environment with multiple capabilities. This paper introduces \spacetime Programming, a novel approach that unifies these paradigms to create a programming model for exploring both code modifications and execution flow. At the core of our approach is a trace mechanism that captures not only execution state but also the corresponding code changes, enabling developers to explore programs in both space (code variants) and time (execution flow). As a proof of concept, we implemented a Python library supporting SpaceTime Programming and applied it in two contexts: a live omniscient debugger and a Pygame game development tool, showcased through a Flappy Bird-like game. We further evaluated SpaceTimePy on five real-world Python projects, finding performance overhead ranging from 35% to 150% on test suites.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [A vision for a colorectal digital twin that enables proactive and personalized disease management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18064)
*Sayed Chhattan Shah,Andrea Townsend-Nicholson,Spiros Denaxas,Pablo Lamata,Manish Chand*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出结直肠数字孪生概念框架，用于非侵入性连续监测和个性化疾病管理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌、炎症性肠病和憩室病是全球性重大疾病，现有诊疗模式依赖间歇性临床就诊和实验室检查，限制了早期检测和个性化长期管理

Method: 提出集成多模态生理行为数据流、混合机制-机器学习建模结直肠功能、个性化人工智能引擎的概念框架

Result: 未部署临床系统，但明确了结直肠数字孪生的愿景和结构化方法，识别了未来实施和验证所需的关键技术、建模和转化挑战

Conclusion: 结直肠数字孪生框架为实现主动疾病管理提供了清晰路径，但需要解决技术、建模和临床转化方面的挑战才能实现临床应用

Abstract: Colorectal cancer, inflammatory bowel disease, and diverticular disease are progressive conditions that affect millions of individuals worldwide and impose substantial clinical and economic burdens. Early detection and personalized management are essential for slowing disease progression and improving patient outcomes. Current care pathways rely primarily on episodic clinical encounters, laboratory testing, and reactive interventions, limiting early detection and personalized longitudinal management. This paper introduces a conceptual framework for an integrated colorectal digital twin that supports non-invasive, continuous monitoring and personalized disease management. The framework integrates multimodal physiological and behavioral data streams, hybrid mechanistic-machine learning modeling of colorectal function, and a personalized artificial intelligence engine to support proactive disease management. Rather than presenting a deployed clinical system, this work outlines a clear vision and a structured approach for colorectal digital twins, identifying key technical, modeling, and translational challenges necessary for future implementation and validation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Semantic Chameleon: Corpus-Dependent Poisoning Attacks and Defenses in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18034)
*Scott Thornton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对RAG系统的梯度引导语料库投毒攻击研究：通过优化休眠文档和触发文档，在纯向量检索下达到38%共检索率；混合检索(BM25+向量)能显著缓解攻击，但攻击者可针对混合信号优化载荷实现部分规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过外部知识源扩展LLM能力，但检索管道引入了新的攻击面。攻击者可投毒检索语料库，使恶意文档在推理时被优先检索，从而定向操纵模型输出。本研究旨在探索梯度引导的语料库投毒攻击并评估无需修改底层LLM的检索层防御方法。

Method: 实现双文档投毒攻击：包含休眠文档和触发文档，使用贪婪坐标梯度(GCG)进行优化。在Security Stack Exchange语料库(67,941文档)进行大规模评估，包含50次攻击尝试。评估混合检索防御(BM25+向量相似度)，并在五个LLM家族(GPT-5.3、GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Llama 4、GPT-4o-mini)和FEVER Wikipedia数据集上进行跨模型和跨语料库评估。

Result: 梯度引导投毒在纯向量检索下达到38.0%共检索率。混合检索将攻击成功率从38%降至0%。当攻击者针对稀疏和密集检索信号联合优化载荷时，混合检索可被部分规避(20-44%成功率)。跨LLM评估显示攻击成功率在46.7%至93.3%之间。在FEVER Wikipedia数据集上所有检索配置均获得0%攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 梯度引导投毒对RAG系统构成严重威胁，特别是在纯向量检索场景下。混合检索(BM25+向量)是有效的防御策略，能显著提高攻击难度，但攻击者可通过针对混合信号优化载荷来部分规避。攻击效果具有语料库依赖性，在Security Stack Exchange上有效但在FEVER Wikipedia上无效。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems extend large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge sources but introduce new attack surfaces through the retrieval pipeline. In particular, adversaries can poison retrieval corpora so that malicious documents are preferentially retrieved at inference time, enabling targeted manipulation of model outputs. We study gradient-guided corpus poisoning attacks against modern RAG pipelines and evaluate retrieval-layer defenses that require no modification to the underlying LLM.
  We implement dual-document poisoning attacks consisting of a sleeper document and a trigger document optimized using Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG). In a large-scale evaluation on the Security Stack Exchange corpus (67,941 documents) with 50 attack attempts, gradient-guided poisoning achieves a 38.0 percent co-retrieval rate under pure vector retrieval.
  We show that a simple architectural modification, hybrid retrieval combining BM25 and vector similarity, substantially mitigates this attack. Across all 50 attacks, hybrid retrieval reduces gradient-guided attack success from 38 percent to 0 percent without modifying the model or retraining the retriever. When attackers jointly optimize payloads for both sparse and dense retrieval signals, hybrid retrieval can be partially circumvented, achieving 20-44 percent success, but still significantly raises attack difficulty relative to vector-only retrieval.
  Evaluation across five LLM families (GPT-5.3, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama 4, and GPT-4o-mini) shows attack success ranging from 46.7 percent to 93.3 percent. Cross-corpus evaluation on the FEVER Wikipedia dataset (25 attacks) yields 0 percent attack success across all retrieval configurations.

</details>


### [94] [Guardrails as Infrastructure: Policy-First Control for Tool-Orchestrated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18059)
*Akshey Sigdel,Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Policy-First Tooling框架，通过显式约束、风险感知门控、恢复控制和可审计解释来中介工具调用，解决自动化系统中常见的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用自动化系统（脚本、CI机器人、智能助手）存在重复性故障模式，包括不安全副作用、无效参数、不受控重试和敏感信息泄露。现有缓解措施多为模型中心化和提示依赖型，脆弱且无法泛化到非LLM调用者。

Method: 提出模型无关的权限层框架，包含紧凑的策略DSL、运行时执行架构（具有可操作原理和修复提示），以及基于轨迹重放和可控故障/滥用注入的可复现基准测试。

Result: 在225次控制运行中，严格策略包将违规预防从P0的0.000提升到P4的0.681，任务成功率从0.356下降到0.067。重试放大从3.774降至1.378，在注入秘密输出时泄漏召回率达到0.875。

Conclusion: Policy-First Tooling使安全性与实用性之间的权衡变得明确且可测量，为工具调用提供了模型无关的安全保障层。

Abstract: Tool-using automation systems, from scripts and CI bots to agentic assistants, fail in recurring patterns. Common failures include unsafe side effects, invalid arguments, uncontrolled retries, and leakage of sensitive outputs. Many mitigations are model-centric and prompt-dependent, so they are brittle and do not generalize to non-LLM callers. We present Policy-First Tooling, a model-agnostic permission layer that mediates tool invocation through explicit constraints, risk-aware gating, recovery controls, and auditable explanations. The paper contributes a compact policy DSL, a runtime enforcement architecture with actionable rationale and fix hints, and a reproducible benchmark based on trace replay with controlled fault and misuse injection. In 225 controlled runs across five policy packs and three fault profiles, stricter packs improve violation prevention from 0.000 in P0 to 0.681 in P4, while task success drops from 0.356 to 0.067. Retry amplification decreases from 3.774 in P0 to 1.378 in P4, and leakage recall reaches 0.875 under injected secret outputs. These results make safety to utility trade-offs explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [95] [TLGLock: A New Approach in Logic Locking Using Key-Driven Charge Recycling in Threshold Logic Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17809)
*Abdullah Sahruri,Martin Margala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLGLock是一种利用阈值逻辑门和电荷回收技术的新型逻辑锁定方法，相比传统方案在面积、延迟、功耗和SAT攻击抵抗力方面均有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前逻辑锁定方法在可扩展性和设计开销方面面临挑战，需要更高效、安全的硬件防伪解决方案。

Method: 提出TLGLock设计范式，利用阈值逻辑门的结构表达能力和电荷回收的能量效率，在门级实现密钥依赖功能；通过将密钥嵌入门的加权逻辑并利用动态电荷共享，提供无状态紧凑的锁定方案；实现完整的综合到锁定流程。

Result: 相比锁存器锁定方案，TLGLock实现高达30%面积节省、50%延迟降低和20%功耗节省；相比XOR和SFLL-HD方法，提供高达3倍的SAT攻击抵抗力且开销显著更低；随机密钥权重实验显示错误密钥下可达100%输出损坏。

Conclusion: TLGLock作为可扩展且具有弹性的解决方案，为安全硬件设计提供了新的设计范式，在最小成本下实现可调安全性。

Abstract: Logic locking remains one of the most promising defenses against hardware piracy, yet current approaches often face challenges in scalability and design overhead. In this paper, we present TLGLock, a new design paradigm that leverages the structural expressiveness of Threshold Logic Gates (TLGs) and the energy efficiency of charge recycling to enforce key-dependent functionality at the gate level. By embedding the key into the gate's weighted logic and utilizing dynamic charge sharing, TLGLock provides a stateless and compact alternative to conventional locking techniques. We implement a complete synthesis-to-locking flow and evaluate it using ISCAS, ITC, and MCNC benchmarks. Results show that TLGLock achieves up to 30% area, 50% delay, and 20% power savings compared to latch-based locking schemes. In comparison with XOR and SFLL-HD methods, TLGLock offers up to 3x higher SAT attack resistance with significantly lower overhead. Furthermore, randomized key-weight experiments demonstrate that TLGLock can reach up to 100% output corruption under incorrect keys, enabling tunable security at minimal cost. These results position TLGLock as a scalable and resilient solution for secure hardware design.

</details>


### [96] [PlanTwin: Privacy-Preserving Planning Abstractions for Cloud-Assisted LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18377)
*Guangsheng Yu,Qin Wang,Rui Lang,Shuai Su,Xu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PlanTwin是一个隐私保护架构，通过创建去标识化的数字孪生环境，使云端LLM规划器能在不暴露原始本地上下文的情况下进行规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于云端LLM的智能体系统在规划时需要暴露原始环境状态（源代码、文件、凭证等敏感信息），存在隐私泄露风险。现有解决方案主要关注执行隔离、访问控制或机密推理，但未能控制云端规划器在规划过程中观察到的内容。

Method: 将真实环境投影到规划导向的数字孪生：一个模式约束且去标识化的抽象图，保留规划相关结构但移除可重构细节。云端规划器仅通过有限能力接口操作这个净化后的孪生，本地看门人强制执行安全策略和累积披露预算。形式化隐私-效用权衡为能力粒度问题，使用(k,δ)-匿名性和ε-不可链接性定义隐私目标，通过多轮披露控制缓解组合泄漏。

Result: 在10个领域的60个智能体任务上评估，PlanTwin实现了完全敏感项非披露(SND=1.0)，同时保持接近全上下文系统的规划质量：4个规划器中的3个达到PQS>0.79，完整流水线的效用损失小于2.2%。

Conclusion: PlanTwin提供了一种有效的隐私保护架构，使云端LLM规划器能在不暴露原始本地上下文的情况下进行规划，平衡了隐私保护和规划效用。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large language models (LLMs) have become the de facto planners in agentic systems, coordinating tools and guiding execution over local environments. In many deployments, however, the environment being planned over is private, containing source code, files, credentials, and metadata that cannot be exposed to the cloud. Existing solutions address adjacent concerns, such as execution isolation, access control, or confidential inference, but they do not control what cloud planners observe during planning: within the permitted scope, \textit{raw environment state is still exposed}.
  We introduce PlanTwin, a privacy-preserving architecture for cloud-assisted planning without exposing raw local context. The key idea is to project the real environment into a \textit{planning-oriented digital twin}: a schema-constrained and de-identified abstract graph that preserves planning-relevant structure while removing reconstructable details. The cloud planner operates solely on this sanitized twin through a bounded capability interface, while a local gatekeeper enforces safety policies and cumulative disclosure budgets. We further formalize the privacy-utility trade-off as a capability granularity problem, define architectural privacy goals using $(k,δ)$-anonymity and $ε$-unlinkability, and mitigate compositional leakage through multi-turn disclosure control.
  We implement PlanTwin as middleware between local agents and cloud planners and evaluate it on 60 agentic tasks across ten domains with four cloud planners. PlanTwin achieves full sensitive-item non-disclosure (SND = 1.0) while maintaining planning quality close to full-context systems: three of four planners achieve PQS $> 0.79$, and the full pipeline incurs less than 2.2\% utility loss.

</details>


### [97] [STEP: Detecting Audio Backdoor Attacks via Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18103)
*Kun Wang,Meng Chen,Junhao Wang,Yuli Wu,Li Lu,Chong Zhang,Peng Cheng,Jiaheng Zhang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STEP是一种针对语音模型后门攻击的黑盒检测方法，利用后门触发器的双重异常特性：在语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性，以及在语义保持扰动下的异常标签脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习语音模型在安全关键应用中的广泛部署，后门攻击已成为严重威胁。现有推理时防御方法不适合音频领域，因为它们要么依赖触发器的过度鲁棒性假设（对基于变换和语义的触发器失效），要么依赖图像或文本模态的特定属性。

Method: STEP是一种黑盒、无需重新训练的后门检测器，仅需硬标签访问。核心思想是利用后门触发器的双重异常特性：1）在语义破坏扰动下的异常标签稳定性；2）在语义保持扰动下的异常标签脆弱性。方法包括：为每个测试样本配置两个互补的扰动分支分别针对这两个特性，使用在良性参考上训练的一类异常检测器对生成的稳定性特征进行评分，并通过无监督加权融合两个分数。

Result: 在七种后门攻击上的广泛实验表明，STEP平均AUROC达到97.92%，EER为4.54%，显著优于最先进的基线方法。该方法在模型架构、语音任务、开放集验证场景以及空中物理世界设置中都具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: STEP是一种有效的语音模型后门攻击检测方法，通过利用后门触发器的双重异常特性，在仅需硬标签访问的黑盒设置下实现了高性能检测，填补了音频领域后门防御的空白。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of deep-learning-based speech models in security-critical applications, backdoor attacks have emerged as a serious threat: an adversary who poisons a small fraction of training data can implant a hidden trigger that controls the model's output while preserving normal behavior on clean inputs. Existing inference-time defenses are not well suited to the audio domain, as they either rely on trigger over-robustness assumptions that fail on transformation-based and semantic triggers, or depend on properties specific to image or text modalities. In this paper, we propose STEP (Stability-based Trigger Exposure Profiling), a black-box, retraining-free backdoor detector that operates under hard-label-only access. Its core idea is to exploit a characteristic dual anomaly of backdoor triggers: anomalous label stability under semantic-breaking perturbations, and anomalous label fragility under semantic-preserving perturbations. STEP profiles each test sample with two complementary perturbation branches that target these two properties respectively, scores the resulting stability features with one-class anomaly detectors trained on benign references, and fuses the two scores via unsupervised weighting. Extensive experiments across seven backdoor attacks show that STEP achieves an average AUROC of 97.92% and EER of 4.54%, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, and generalizes across model architectures, speech tasks, an open-set verification scenario, and over-the-air physical-world settings.

</details>


### [98] [Confidential Databases Without Cryptographic Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18836)
*Wenxuan Huang,Zhanbo Wang,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FEDB是一种新型机密数据库设计，通过将密码学操作移出关键路径，使用无密码映射技术，在不可信云环境中实现高性能的安全查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密数据库在不可信云环境中保护敏感数据查询至关重要，但现有方案因频繁的同步密码学操作导致高性能开销，成为广泛部署的主要障碍。

Method: FEDB采用无密码映射技术，在数据库中维护数据无关的标识符，同时在可信域中安全地将这些标识符映射到明文秘密，从而将密码学操作移出关键路径。

Result: 在TPC-C和TPC-H等行业标准基准测试中，FEDB将运行时开销降低了高达78.0倍。

Conclusion: FEDB通过范式转变显著降低了机密数据库的性能开销，为在不可信云环境中安全高效地处理敏感数据提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Confidential databases (CDBs) are essential for enabling secure queries over sensitive data in untrusted cloud environments using confidential computing hardware. While adoption is growing, widespread deployment is hindered by high performance overhead from frequent synchronous cryptographic operations, which causes significant computational and memory bottlenecks. We present FEDB, a novel CDB design that removes cryptographic operations from the critical path. FEDB leverages crypto-free mappings, which maintain data-independent identifiers within the database while securely mapping them to plaintext secrets in a trusted domain. This paradigm shift reduces the runtime overhead by up to 78.0 times on industry-standard benchmarks including TPC-C and TPC-H.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Fuzzy Logic-Based Steganographic Encryption Framework: A Comprehensive Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18105)
*Aadi Joshi,Kavya Bhand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合模糊推理系统与认证加密的自适应隐写框架，通过局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力动态确定1-3位像素嵌入深度，同时使用密码学层保护载荷安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定深度LSB嵌入在平滑和纹理区域均匀修改，导致失真增加和统计可检测性提高。需要一种自适应方法在容量、视觉保真度和统计不可检测性之间实现更好平衡。

Method: 1. 使用Mamdani型模糊推理系统，以局部熵、边缘幅度和载荷压力为语言输入，确定像素级嵌入深度（1-3位）。2. 从低位剥离图像计算相同特征图，使自适应控制机制对LSB修改不变。3. 基于Argon2id和AES-256-GCM的密码学层独立保护载荷机密性和完整性。

Result: 该方法实现了自适应嵌入深度控制，在平滑区域使用较少位嵌入以减少失真，在纹理区域使用较多位嵌入以增加容量，同时保持编码器-解码器同步。密码学层提供独立于隐写隐藏的载荷保护。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应隐写框架通过模糊推理系统实现智能嵌入深度控制，结合现代认证加密技术，在保持LSB方法简单性和高容量的同时，改善了视觉保真度和统计不可检测性。

Abstract: Digital image steganography requires a careful trade-off among payload capacity, visual fidelity, and statistical undetectability. Fixed-depth least significant bit embedding remains attractive because of its simplicity and high capacity, but it modifies smooth and textured regions uniformly, thereby increasing distortion and detectability in statistically sensitive areas. This paper presents an adaptive steganographic framework that combines a Mamdanitype fuzzy inference system with modern authenticated encryption. The proposed method determines a pixel-wise embedding depth from 1 to 3 bits using local entropy, edge magnitude, and payload pressure as linguistic inputs. To preserve encoder-decoder synchronization, the same feature maps are computed from lower-bit-stripped images, making the adaptive control mechanism invariant to the least significant modifications introduced during embedding. A cryptographic layer based on Argon2id and AES-256-GCM protects payload confidentiality and integrity independently of steganographic concealment.

</details>


### [100] [Toward Reliable, Safe, and Secure LLMs for Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18235)
*Saket Sanjeev Chaturvedi,Joshua Bergerson,Tanwi Mallick*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了大型语言模型作为自主"AI科学家"在科学领域应用时的安全挑战，提出了针对科学领域的威胁分类、自动化安全基准生成机制和多层防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM演变为自主的"AI科学家"，它们在科学领域带来了变革性潜力，但也引入了新的安全漏洞，如"生物安全风险"和"危险爆炸"等。现有的通用安全基准存在领域不匹配、威胁覆盖有限和基准过拟合等问题，无法有效评估科学应用中的漏洞，因此需要新的安全范式。

Method: 1) 为科学研究背景下的LLM威胁制定详细分类法；2) 提出利用专用多智能体系统自动生成领域特定对抗性安全基准的机制；3) 设计多层防御框架，结合红队演练、外部边界控制和主动内部安全LLM智能体。

Result: 论文提出了一个综合性的概念框架，包括：科学领域LLM威胁的详细分类法、自动化安全基准生成机制的概念设计，以及结合红队演练、边界控制和内部安全智能体的多层防御策略。这些元素共同为科学领域中可信LLM智能体部署提供了定义、评估和创建全面防御策略的必要结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为科学领域中LLM智能体的安全部署提供了系统性的概念框架，通过威胁分类、自动化基准生成和多层防御策略，填补了现有安全评估方法的空白，为实现可靠、安全和安全的科学AI应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous "AI scientists," they promise transformative advances but introduce novel vulnerabilities, from potential "biosafety risks" to "dangerous explosions." Ensuring trustworthy deployment in science requires a new paradigm centered on reliability (ensuring factual accuracy and reproducibility), safety (preventing unintentional physical or biological harm), and security (preventing malicious misuse). Existing general-purpose safety benchmarks are poorly suited for this purpose, suffering from a fundamental domain mismatch, limited threat coverage of science-specific vectors, and benchmark overfitting, which create a critical gap in vulnerability evaluation for scientific applications. This paper examines the unique security and safety landscape of LLM agents in science. We begin by synthesizing a detailed taxonomy of LLM threats contextualized for scientific research, to better understand the unique risks associated with LLMs in science. Next, we conceptualize a mechanism to address the evaluation gap by utilizing dedicated multi-agent systems for the automated generation of domain-specific adversarial security benchmarks. Based on our analysis, we outline how existing safety methods can be brought together and integrated into a conceptual multilayered defense framework designed to combine a red-teaming exercise and external boundary controls with a proactive internal Safety LLM Agent. Together, these conceptual elements provide a necessary structure for defining, evaluating, and creating comprehensive defense strategies for trustworthy LLM agent deployment in scientific disciplines.

</details>


### [101] [Pushan: Trace-Free Deobfuscation of Virtualization-Obfuscated Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18355)
*Ashwin Sudhir,Zion Leonahenahe Basque,Wil Gibbs,Ati Priya Bajaj,Pulkit Singh Singaria,Mitchell Zakocs,Jie Hu,Moritz Schloegel,Tiffany Bao,Adam Doupe,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Ruoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PUSHAN是一种新型通用技术，用于对虚拟化混淆二进制文件进行去混淆，克服了现有技术的局限性，无需执行跟踪，避免路径约束积累，并能生成高质量的C伪代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟化混淆是当前最强大的保护机制之一，但现有的自动去混淆技术存在三个主要缺陷：1) 仅适用于执行跟踪，无法恢复所有逻辑；2) 依赖动态符号执行，成本高且不可扩展；3) 无法生成"格式良好"的代码，阻碍二进制反编译器生成人类友好的输出。

Method: PUSHAN采用无跟踪方法，通过VPC敏感、无约束的符号模拟来避免路径约束积累，恢复虚拟化函数的完整控制流图(CFG)。这是首个还能将受保护代码反编译为高质量C伪代码的方法，关键创新是规避了对路径可满足性(NP难问题)的依赖。

Result: 在超过1,000个二进制文件上评估PUSHAN，包括学术最先进(Tigress)和商业级混淆器(VMProtect和Themida)保护的目标。PUSHAN成功去混淆这些二进制文件，检索其完整CFG，并将其反编译为C伪代码。进一步通过分析VirusTotal中先前未分析的VMProtect混淆恶意软件样本展示了实用性。

Conclusion: PUSHAN是首个能够对虚拟化混淆二进制文件进行完整去混淆并生成高质量C伪代码的通用技术，克服了现有方法的可扩展性和输出质量问题，为安全分析提供了有效的自动化工具。

Abstract: In the ever-evolving battle against malware, binary obfuscation techniques are a formidable barrier to effective analysis by both human security analysts and automated systems. In particular, virtualization or VM-based obfuscation is one of the strongest protection mechanisms that evade automated analysis. Despite widespread use of virtualization, existing automated deobfuscation techniques suffer from three major drawbacks. First, they only work on execution traces, which prevents them from recovering all logic in an obfuscated binary. Second, they depend on dynamic symbolic execution, which is expensive and does not scale in practice. Third, they cannot generate "well-formed" code, which prevents existing binary decompilers from generating human-friendly output.
  This paper introduces PUSHAN, a novel and generic technique for deobfuscating virtualization-obfuscated binaries while overcoming the limitations of existing techniques. PUSHAN is trace-free and avoids path-constraint accumulation by using VPC-sensitive, constraint-free symbolic emulation to recover a complete CFG of the virtualized function. It is the first approach that also decompiles the protected code into high-quality C pseudocode to enable effective analysis. Crucially, PUSHAN circumvents reliance on path satisfiability, a known NP-hard problem that hampers scalability. We evaluate PUSHAN on more than 1,000 binaries, including targets protected by academic state of the art (Tigress) and commercial-strength obfuscators VMProtect and Themida. PUSHAN successfully deobfuscates these binaries, retrieves their complete CFGs, and decompiles them to C pseudocode. We further demonstrate applicability by analyzing a previously unanalyzed VMProtect-obfuscated malware sample from VirusTotal, where our decompiled output enables LLM-assisted code simplification, reuse, and program understanding.

</details>


### [102] [Prompt Control-Flow Integrity: A Priority-Aware Runtime Defense Against Prompt Injection in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18433)
*Md Takrim Ul Alam,Akif Islam,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PCFI是一种针对LLM API和RAG系统的运行时防御机制，通过建模请求为结构化组件并应用三层中间件管道来防止提示注入攻击，实现零误报和极低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法将提示视为扁平字符串，依赖临时过滤或静态越狱检测，无法有效应对LLM API和RAG系统中的提示注入攻击，这些攻击可能覆盖系统策略、颠覆预期行为并产生不安全输出。

Method: PCFI将每个请求建模为系统、开发者、用户和检索文档段的结构化组合，应用三层中间件管道：词法启发式、角色切换检测和分层策略执行，在请求转发到后端LLM前进行验证。

Result: 在自定义的合成和半现实提示注入工作负载基准测试中，PCFI拦截了所有攻击标记的请求，保持0%误报率，仅引入0.04毫秒的中位处理开销。

Conclusion: 来源和优先级感知的提示执行是部署LLM系统的实用且轻量级防御方案，PCFI证明了结构化建模和运行时验证的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed behind APIs and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) stacks are vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that may override system policies, subvert intended behavior, and induce unsafe outputs. Existing defenses often treat prompts as flat strings and rely on ad hoc filtering or static jailbreak detection. This paper proposes Prompt Control-Flow Integrity (PCFI), a priority-aware runtime defense that models each request as a structured composition of system, developer, user, and retrieved-document segments. PCFI applies a three-stage middleware pipeline, lexical heuristics, role-switch detection, and hierarchical policy enforcement, before forwarding requests to the backend LLM. We implement PCFI as a FastAPI-based gateway for deployed LLM APIs and evaluate it on a custom benchmark of synthetic and semi-realistic prompt-injection workloads. On the evaluated benchmark suite, PCFI intercepts all attack-labeled requests, maintains a 0% False Positive Rate, and introduces a median processing overhead of only 0.04 ms. These results suggest that provenance- and priority-aware prompt enforcement is a practical and lightweight defense for deployed LLM systems.

</details>


### [103] [CNT: Safety-oriented Function Reuse across LLMs via Cross-Model Neuron Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18449)
*Yue Zhao,Yujia Gong,Ruigang Liang,Shenchen Zhu,Kai Chen,Xuejing Yuan,Wangjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CNT是一种后处理方法，通过从开源LLM中转移神经元子集来复用安全功能，实现模块化功能级适配，支持功能添加和删除，在多种安全应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛部署，需要灵活的后处理方法来适应不断发展的安全要求。开源LLM生态系统已经产生了各种具有安全功能性的模型，这促使从零构建转向复用现有功能，避免昂贵的数据收集和训练过程。

Method: 提出跨模型神经元转移（CNT）方法，通过从开源捐赠LLM中转移最小神经元子集到目标LLM，在神经元级别操作，实现模块化功能级适配，支持功能添加和删除。

Result: 在7个流行LLM和3个代表性应用（安全不对齐、对齐增强、偏见消除）上的实验表明，CNT实现了有针对性的安全功能转移，性能下降最小（大多数模型小于1%），持续优于5个基线方法。

Conclusion: CNT展示了其通用性和实际有效性，为LLM安全功能复用提供了一种高效的后处理方法。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of large language models (LLMs) calls for post-hoc methods that can flexibly adapt models to evolving safety requirements. Meanwhile, the rapidly expanding open-source LLM ecosystem has produced a diverse collection of models that already exhibit various safety-related functionalities. This motivates a shift from constructing safety functionality from scratch to reusing existing functionality from external models, thereby avoiding costly data collection and training procedures.
  In this paper, we present Cross-Model Neuron Transfer (CNT), a post-hoc method that reuses safety-oriented functionality by transferring a minimal subset of neurons from an open-source donor LLM to a target LLM. By operating at the neuron level, CNT enables modular function-level adaptation, supporting both function addition andfunction deletion. We evaluate CNT on seven popular LLMs across three representative applications: safety disalignment, alignment enhancement, and bias removal. Experimental results show that CNT achieves targeted safety-oriented functionality transfer with minimal performance degradation (less than 1% for most models), consistently outperforming five baselines, demonstrating its generality and practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [104] [Cross-Ecosystem Vulnerability Analysis for Python Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18693)
*Georgios Alexopoulos,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Thodoris Sotiropoulos,Charalambos Mitropoulos,Zhendong Su,Dimitris Mitropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种溯源感知的漏洞分析方法，通过内容哈希匹配和动态分析识别Python包中vendor的本地库版本，构建跨生态系统调用图进行可达性分析，显著减少误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前漏洞扫描器在分析Python应用中的本地库漏洞时存在严重问题：一方面会漏报vendor库中的漏洞（假阴性），另一方面会忽略操作系统发行版向后移植的安全补丁（假阳性）。需要一种能够跨生态系统分析的方法来准确识别受影响的Python包。

Method: 1) 使用内容哈希查询vendor库与历史操作系统包数据库匹配；2) 对上游源码构建的二进制文件应用库特定的动态分析提取版本信息；3) 通过拼接Python和二进制调用图构建跨生态系统调用图；4) 对易受攻击函数进行可达性分析。

Result: 在10万个Python包和10个已知CVE的评估中，识别出39个直接易受攻击的包（月下载量超过4700万）和312个通过依赖链间接易受攻击的客户端包。相比上游版本匹配方法，实现了高达97%的误报减少。

Conclusion: 提出的溯源感知漏洞分析方法能够准确识别Python包中vendor本地库的漏洞，有效解决了当前扫描器的假阴性和假阳性问题，为跨生态系统安全分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Python applications depend on native libraries that may be vendored within package distributions or installed on the host system. When vulnerabilities are discovered in these libraries, determining which Python packages are affected requires cross-ecosystem analysis spanning Python dependency graphs and OS package versions. Current vulnerability scanners produce false negatives by missing vendored vulnerabilities and false positives by ignoring security patches backported by OS distributions.
  We present a provenance-aware vulnerability analysis approach that resolves vendored libraries to specific OS package versions or upstream releases. Our approach queries vendored libraries against a database of historical OS package artifacts using content-based hashing, and applies library-specific dynamic analyses to extract version information from binaries built from upstream source. We then construct cross-ecosystem call graphs by stitching together Python and binary call graphs across dependency boundaries, enabling reachability analysis of vulnerable functions. Evaluating on 100,000 Python packages and 10 known CVEs associated with third-party native dependencies, we identify 39 directly vulnerable packages (47M+ monthly downloads) and 312 indirectly vulnerable client packages affected through dependency chains. Our analysis achieves up to 97% false positive reduction compared to upstream version matching.

</details>


### [105] [Impact of Differentials in SIMON32 Algorithm for Lightweight Security of Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18455)
*Jonathan Cook,Sabih ur Rehman,M. Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了SIMON32密码的差分特性，通过识别高概率差分提高了密码分析效率，超越了现有技术水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIMON密码适用于物联网设备，但其密码分析效率低下，主要原因是差分分布表规模大且高概率差分稀缺，需要改进分析效率

Method: 引入SIMON32密码的差分特性分析，通过部分差分分布表识别高概率差分，揭示差分特征

Result: 分析增加了目标轮数，识别了SIMON密码中的高概率差分，超越了现有技术水平

Conclusion: 对SIMON32的差分特性分析揭示了有助于未来效率提升的差分特征，为改进SIMON密码分析效率提供了基础

Abstract: SIMON and SPECK were among the first efficient encryption algorithms introduced for resource-constrained applications. SIMON is suitable for Internet of Things (IoT) devices and has rapidly attracted the attention of the research community to understand its structure and analyse its security. To analyse the security of an encryption algorithm, researchers often employ cryptanalysis techniques. However, cryptanalysis is a resource and time-intensive task. To improve cryptanalysis efficiency, state-of-the-art research has proposed implementing heuristic search and sampling methods. Despite recent advances, the cryptanalysis of the SIMON cypher remains inefficient. Contributing factors are the large size of the difference distribution tables utilised in cryptanalysis and the scarcity of differentials with a high transition probability. To address these limitations, we introduce an analysis of differential properties of the SIMON32 cypher, revealing differential characteristics that pave the way for future efficiency enhancements. Our analysis has further increased the number of targeted rounds by identifying high probability differentials within a partial difference distribution table of the SIMON cypher, exceeding existing state-of-the-art benchmarks. The code designed for this work is available at https://github.com/johncook1979/simon32-analysis.

</details>


### [106] [Quantifying Memory Cells Vulnerability for DRAM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18549)
*Zilong Hu,Hongming Fei,Prosanta Gope,Jack Miskelly,Owen Millwood,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一个定量、单元级的电路框架，从物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径直接建模DRAM漏洞，将器件层行为与系统级安全属性联系起来，用于评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM中的单元漏洞（如强制保留失效、延迟改变、行锤击和行压）可导致意外位翻转，这些现象既被用作攻击手段破坏数据完整性和机密性，也被安全系统利用来生成随机密钥和设备指纹。攻击者可能利用对单个单元翻转漏洞的了解来预测数据内容，从而破坏安全系统。

Method: 开发了一个定量、单元级的电路框架，直接从DRAM的物理电荷泄漏和干扰路径建模其漏洞。通过将器件层行为（电荷泄漏、干扰路径）与系统级安全属性（易失性、完整性、机密性）联系起来，构建系统化的评估框架。

Result: 该框架能够系统评估DRAM的易失性（保留）、完整性（干扰诱导修改）和机密性（模式依赖性泄漏）。应用于已知故障模式时，揭示了非均匀和上下文依赖的漏洞模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估DRAM在安全应用中的适用性提供了理论基础和实际评估工具，能够连接器件物理特性与系统安全属性，有助于设计更安全的DRAM系统和评估现有系统的安全性。

Abstract: Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) is pervasive in computer systems. Cell vulnerabilities caused by unintended phenomena (forced retention failure, latency alteration, rowhammer and rowpress) lead to unintended bit flips in memory. These phenomena have been explored as attacks to violate data integrity and confidentiality during normal operation, but also exploited as a benefit in security systems as a method to generate random secret keys and unique device fingerprints (e.g. Physically Unclonable Functions). In both cases, attackers may wish to exploit knowledge of individual cell flip vulnerability to predict the current/future data contents of a set of cells, which can be utilised to break security systems. In this work, we develop a quantitative, cell-level circuit framework that models DRAM vulnerability directly from its physical charge leakage and disturbance pathways. By linking these device-layer behaviours to system-level security properties, our framework enables systematic evaluation of DRAM with respect to volatility (retention), integrity (disturbance-induced modification), and confidentiality (pattern-dependent leakage). We further demonstrate how the framework can be applied to well-known failure modes, revealing non-uniform and context-dependent vulnerability patterns. This work provides both theoretical foundations and practical evaluation tools for evaluating the suitability of DRAM use within security applications.

</details>


### [107] [A Complexity Hierarchy of Shuffles in Card-Based Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18608)
*Tomoki Ono,Suthee Ruangwises*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于卡片的密码学中洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，证明了不同层次间的分离性，并基于此提出了新的协议复杂度度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于卡片的密码协议使用各种类型的洗牌操作，其中一些在实践中易于实现，而另一些则复杂得多。需要对这些操作的实现复杂度进行系统分类，为协议设计和评估提供理论基础。

Method: 将洗牌操作按照实现复杂度分为多个层次，从实践和理论角度论证这种分类的合理性，通过证明某些洗牌操作无法仅使用低层次操作来实现，建立层次间的分离关系。

Result: 建立了洗牌操作的复杂度层次结构，证明了不同层次之间的分离性，即某些洗牌操作确实需要更高层次的实现复杂度。基于此层次结构提出了评估卡片协议的新复杂度度量方法。

Conclusion: 提出的洗牌操作复杂度层次结构为基于卡片的密码协议提供了系统的复杂度分类框架，新的复杂度度量方法有助于更准确地评估协议的实际可行性，对协议设计和分析具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: Card-based cryptography uses physical playing cards to construct protocols for secure multi-party computation. Existing card-based protocols employ various types of shuffles, some of which are easy to implement in practice while others are considerably more complex. In this paper, we classify shuffle operations into several levels according to their implementation complexity. We motivate this hierarchy from both practical and theoretical perspectives, and prove separation results between several levels by showing that certain shuffles cannot be realized using only operations from lower levels. Finally, we propose a new complexity measure for evaluating card-based protocols based on this hierarchy.

</details>


### [108] [MOSAIC: Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18637)
*Yipu Dou,Wang Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个多目标切片感知迭代优化框架，用于在固定监督微调预算下平衡安全对齐、低过度拒绝和指令跟随三个目标，通过将切片级失败分析转化为可执行的数据操作来优化数据混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在固定监督微调预算下同时平衡三个关键目标：多轮对话安全对齐、良性边界查询的低过度拒绝、以及可验证约束下的指令跟随能力。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，基于统一的L1-L3评估接口，将切片级失败分析转化为可执行的数据操作，包括数据集级混合比例、桶级权重和聚焦标准，实现闭环数据混合搜索。

Result: 在固定100万token预算和五轮独立微调下，MOSAIC将内部XGuard评分从2.76提升至4.67，同时保持OrBench在4.41和IFEval在3.65。最终Pareto解在独立攻击、过度拒绝和能力测试上优于随机静态LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 结构化失败诊断可作为预算数据构建的实用控制信号，MOSAIC框架在平衡多目标对齐方面表现出色，并能更好地泛化到独立测试集。

Abstract: We study how to allocate a fixed supervised fine-tuning budget when three objectives must be balanced at once: multi-turn safety alignment, low over-refusal on benign boundary queries, and instruction following under verifiable constraints. We propose MOSAIC (Multi-Objective Slice-Aware Iterative Curation for Alignment), a multi-objective framework for closed-loop data mixture search built on a unified L1-L3 evaluation interface. MOSAIC turns slice-level failure profiles into executable data actions, including dataset-level mixture ratios, bucket-level weights, and focus criteria. Under a fixed 1M-token budget and five rounds of independent fine-tuning from the same base model, MOSAIC improves internal XGuard from 2.76 to 4.67 while keeping OrBench at 4.41 and IFEval at 3.65. The final Pareto solution also generalizes better than a random static LoRA baseline on independent attack, over-refusal, and capability tests, suggesting that structured failure diagnosis can serve as a practical control signal for budgeted data construction. Code is available at https://github.com/douyipu/mosaic.

</details>


### [109] [Secure Wi-Fi Ranging Today: Security and Adoption of IEEE 802.11az/bk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18687)
*Nikola Antonijević,Bernhard Etzlinger,Dave Singelée,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析IEEE 802.11az和802.11bk标准中安全Wi-Fi测距机制的安全性和可部署性，揭示配置敏感性和硬件实现挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着测距和定位在关键应用中的重要性增加，Wi-Fi标准作为广泛部署的技术自然成为提供这些功能的候选方案。IEEE 802.11az引入了"下一代定位"机制来增强现有不安全的Wi-Fi精细时间测量(FTM)测距方案，而802.11bk通过增加带宽旨在接近超宽带(UWB)系统的厘米级精度。论文旨在从安全和可部署性角度评估这些承诺的实现程度。

Method: 结合标准分析、仿真和商用/开发硬件的测量，从逻辑层和物理层分析安全Wi-Fi测距的核心机制。逻辑层分析常见部署选择的影响，物理层研究安全测距波形的可预测性、符号重复的安全影响以及波形设计选择对频谱掩模合规性的影响。

Result: 研究表明：1) 逻辑层上，常见部署选择可能导致未认证测距、降级攻击和简单拒绝服务攻击，难以安全实现高风险用例；2) 物理层上，安全测距波形具有可预测性，符号重复存在安全影响，波形设计选择影响频谱掩模合规性；3) 安全Wi-Fi测距对配置选择高度敏感，在现有硬件上实现非易事，目前商用设备支持有限。

Conclusion: 安全Wi-Fi测距面临显著的安全和实现挑战。论文提供了安全使用FTM的实用指南，并向供应商和标准化机构提出了改进其鲁棒性和可部署性的建议。需要更谨慎的配置和硬件支持才能充分发挥其潜力。

Abstract: Ranging and localisation have become critical for many applications and services. The Wi-Fi (IEEE 802.11) standard is a natural candidate for providing these functions across diverse environments, given its widespread deployment. The IEEE 802.11az amendment, finalised in 2023, introduces "Next Generation Positioning" mechanisms to secure and harden the existing insecure Wi-Fi Fine Timing Measurement (FTM) ranging solution. Moreover, the recent IEEE 802.11bk amendment increases the available bandwidth with the goal of approaching the centimetre-level ranging accuracy of ultra-wideband (UWB) systems. This paper examines to what extent these promises hold from a security and deployability perspective. We analyse the core mechanisms of secure Wi-Fi ranging as defined in IEEE 802.11az and IEEE 802.11bk at both the logical and physical layers, combining standards analysis with simulations and measurements on commercial and development hardware. At the logical layer, we show how common deployment choices can result in unauthenticated ranging, downgrade attacks, and simple denial-of-service attacks, making it difficult to securely realise many high-stakes use cases. At the physical layer, we study the predictability of secure ranging waveforms, the security impact of symbol repetition, and how waveform design choices affect compliance with spectral masks under realistic RF behaviour. Our results show that secure Wi-Fi ranging is highly sensitive to configuration choices and is non-trivial to implement on existing hardware. This is also evidenced by the currently limited support for secure Wi-Fi ranging in commodity devices. This paper provides practical guidelines for using secure FTM safely and recommendations to vendors and standardisation bodies to improve its robustness and deployability.

</details>


### [110] [Functional Subspace Watermarking for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18793)
*Zikang Ding,Junhao Li,Suling Wu,Junchi Yao,Hongbo Liu,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为FSW的功能子空间水印框架，通过将所有权信号锚定在低维功能骨干中来增强大语言模型水印的鲁棒性，以应对模型修改带来的特征失真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型水印方法在应对实际模型修改（如微调、量化、知识蒸馏）时，内部表示特征会经历复杂失真，导致可靠提取极其困难。尽管已有大量研究，现有方法仍缺乏对参数级扰动的足够鲁棒性。

Method: 提出功能子空间水印（FSW）框架：1）通过求解广义特征值问题提取稳定的功能子空间用于水印注入；2）引入自适应谱截断策略实现鲁棒性与模型效用的最优平衡；3）加入向量一致性约束确保水印注入不损害原始语义性能。

Result: 在各种LLM架构和数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在多种模型攻击下实现了优越的检测精度和统计可验证性，其鲁棒性优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FSW框架通过将所有权信号锚定在低维功能骨干中，有效解决了模型水印在参数扰动下的鲁棒性问题，为LLM所有权保护提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Model watermarking utilizes internal representations to protect the ownership of large language models (LLMs). However, these features inevitably undergo complex distortions during realistic model modifications such as fine-tuning, quantization, or knowledge distillation, making reliable extraction extremely challenging. Despite extensive research on model-side watermarking, existing methods still lack sufficient robustness against parameter-level perturbations. To address this gap, we propose \texttt{\textbf{Functional Subspace Watermarking (FSW)}}, a framework that anchors ownership signals into a low-dimensional functional backbone. Specifically, we first solve a generalized eigenvalue problem to extract a stable functional subspace for watermark injection, while introducing an adaptive spectral truncation strategy to achieve an optimal balance between robustness and model utility. Furthermore, a vector consistency constraint is incorporated to ensure that watermark injection does not compromise the original semantic performance. Extensive experiments across various LLM architectures and datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior detection accuracy and statistical verifiability under multiple model attacks, maintaining robustness that outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


### [111] [Agent Control Protocol: Admission Control for Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18829)
*Marcelo Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) 是一个用于B2B机构环境中自主智能体治理的正式技术规范，作为智能体意图和系统状态变更之间的准入控制层，通过密码学验证确保操作合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自主智能体在B2B机构环境中提供明确的治理框架，确保智能体操作在机构控制下进行，同时保持与现有RBAC和零信任架构的兼容性。

Method: 定义密码学身份、基于能力的授权、确定性风险评估、可验证链式委托、传递式撤销和不可变审计等机制，作为RBAC和零信任架构之上的附加层。

Result: v1.13规范包含36个技术文档、5个符合性级别、Go参考实现（22个包）、51个签名符合性测试向量、OpenAPI 3.1.0规范，定义了62+可验证要求、12个禁止行为以及机构间互操作机制。

Conclusion: ACP为自主智能体在机构环境中的安全治理提供了完整的技术规范和实现框架，通过密码学准入控制确保操作合规性和可审计性。

Abstract: Agent Control Protocol (ACP) is a formal technical specification for governance of autonomous agents in B2B institutional environments. ACP is the admission control layer between agent intent and system state mutation: before any agent action reaches execution, it must pass a cryptographic admission check that validates identity, capability scope, delegation chain, and policy compliance simultaneously.
  ACP defines the mechanisms of cryptographic identity, capability-based authorization, deterministic risk evaluation, verifiable chained delegation, transitive revocation, and immutable auditing that a system must implement for autonomous agents to operate under explicit institutional control. ACP operates as an additional layer on top of RBAC and Zero Trust, without replacing them.
  The v1.13 specification comprises 36 technical documents organized into five conformance levels (L1-L5). It includes a Go reference implementation of 22 packages covering all L1-L4 capabilities, 51 signed conformance test vectors (Ed25519 + SHA-256), and an OpenAPI 3.1.0 specification for all HTTP endpoints. It defines more than 62 verifiable requirements, 12 prohibited behaviors, and the mechanisms for interoperability between institutions.
  Specification and implementation: https://github.com/chelof100/acp-framework-en

</details>


### [112] [Controller Datapath Aware Verification of Masked Hardware Generated via High Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18939)
*Nilotpola Sarma,Vaishali Ghanshyam Chaudhuri,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MaskedHLSVerif工具流，用于验证HLS生成掩码硬件RTL的侧信道安全性，避免因资源共享数据通路导致的误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS工具自动从已验证掩码软件生成掩码RTL硬件可减少设计工作量，但HLS优化可能影响侧信道安全性，现有验证工具对HLS生成设计可能产生误报

Method: 提出MaskedHLSVerif工具流，对HLS生成的控制器-数据通路RTL进行状态式形式验证，避免资源共享数据通路导致的误报

Result: 工具正确验证标准密码学基准（级联掩码模块和PRESENT S-box掩码），而现有工具如REBECCA报告误报；能检测HLS优化引入的掩码缺陷

Conclusion: MaskedHLSVerif为HLS生成掩码硬件提供有效验证策略，避免误报并检测HLS优化引入的安全问题

Abstract: Masking is a countermeasure against Power Side Channel Attacks (PSCAs) in both software and hardware implementations of cryptographic algorithms. Compared to software masking, implementing masked hardware is time consuming and error prone. Recent approaches, therefore, rely on High Level Synthesis (HLS) tools to automatically generate masked Register Transfer Level (RTL) hardware from verified masked software, significantly reducing design effort. Since HLS was never developed for security, HLS optimizations may impact PSCA security of the generated RTL. As a result, verifying the PSCA security of HLS generated masked RTL is crucial. Existing hardware masking verification tools can verify masked hardware, but may produce false positives when applied to HLS generated designs with controller datapath architectures obtained due to resource-shared datapath obtained via HLS. This work proposes a hardware masking verification strategy for HLS generated masked hardware. Our toolflow MaskedHLSVerif, performs state-wise formal verification of controller datapath RTL obtained via HLS, thereby avoiding false positives caused by resource-shared datapaths. Our tool flow correctly verifies standard cryptographic benchmarks, consisting of cascaded masked gadgets and the PRESENT S-box masked with gadgets, where existing tools like REBECCA reports false positives. The proposed tool-flow is able to detect masking flaws induced by HLS-optimizations as well.

</details>


### [113] [FedTrident: Resilient Road Condition Classification Against Poisoning Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19101)
*Sheng Liu,Panos Papadimitratos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedTrident是一个针对联邦学习道路状况分类中目标标签翻转攻击的防御框架，通过神经元分析、自适应客户端评级和机器遗忘技术实现有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在智能交通系统道路状况分类中的应用面临目标标签翻转攻击的威胁，现有防御方法在多种攻击场景下无法维持接近无攻击水平的模型性能，主要因为：1)未针对TLFAs定制本地模型检测；2)未基于历史行为排除恶意客户端；3)排除恶意客户端后未修复已损坏的全局模型。

Method: FedTrident包含三个核心组件：1)基于神经元分析的本地模型异常检测（包括攻击目标识别、关键特征提取、GMM聚类和过滤）；2)根据每轮FL检测结果进行自适应客户端评级以排除恶意客户端；3)在排除恶意客户端后使用机器遗忘技术修复已损坏的全局模型。

Result: 在多种FL-RCC模型、任务和配置下的广泛评估表明，FedTrident能有效抵御TLFAs，在无攻击场景下达到可比性能，在两个最关键指标上分别优于八个基线防御方法9.49%和4.47%。此外，FedTrident对多种恶意客户端比例、数据异构性水平、复杂多任务和动态攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedTrident通过整合针对性的本地模型检测、基于历史行为的客户端排除和全局模型修复，填补了联邦学习道路状况分类中目标标签翻转攻击防御的研究空白，为智能交通系统提供了安全可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: FL has emerged as a transformative paradigm for ITS, notably camera-based Road Condition Classification (RCC). However, by enabling collaboration, FL-based RCC exposes the system to adversarial participants launching Targeted Label-Flipping Attacks (TLFAs). Malicious clients (vehicles) can relabel their local training data (e.g., from an actual uneven road to a wrong smooth road), consequently compromising global model predictions and jeopardizing transportation safety. Existing countermeasures against such poisoning attacks fail to maintain resilient model performance near the necessary attack-free levels in various attack scenarios due to: 1) not tailoring poisoned local model detection to TLFAs, 2) not excluding malicious vehicular clients based on historical behavior, and 3) not remedying the already-corrupted global model after exclusion. To close this research gap, we propose FedTrident, which introduces: 1) neuron-wise analysis for local model misbehavior detection (notably including attack goal identification, critical feature extraction, and GMM-based model clustering and filtering); 2) adaptive client rating for client exclusion according to the local model detection results in each FL round; and 3) machine unlearning for corrupted global model remediation once malicious clients are excluded during FL. Extensive evaluation across diverse FL-RCC models, tasks, and configurations demonstrates that FedTrident can effectively thwart TLFAs, achieving performance comparable to that in attack-free scenarios and outperforming eight baseline countermeasures by 9.49% and 4.47% for the two most critical metrics. Moreover, FedTrident is resilient to various malicious client rates, data heterogeneity levels, complicated multi-task, and dynamic attacks.

</details>


### [114] [Performance Testing of ChaCha20-Poly1305 for Internet of Things and Industrial Control System devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19150)
*Kristján Orri Ragnarsson,Jacky Mallett*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在低成本边缘设备上为工业控制系统协议添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密，加密开销仅占GOOSE协议延迟要求的7.1%以内，完全满足实时性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统和物联网设备常使用未加密或未认证的协议通信，这是历史遗留问题。随着设备处理能力提升和高效加密算法出现，为ICS协议添加加密变得可行，但需要验证是否满足严格的延迟和方差要求。

Method: 在Raspberry PI 4和Intel N95 Mini PC等低成本边缘设备上，测量为通信周期添加ChaCha20-Poly1305加密的时间开销，评估其对Modbus、IEC61850(GOOSE)等协议延迟要求的影响。

Result: 在最坏情况下，加密周期仅占GOOSE协议延迟要求的7.1%以内，对IEC-60834-1协议则小于3%，完全满足这些协议的指定延迟要求。这表明在低成本设备上实现加密是可行的。

Conclusion: 为工业控制系统协议添加加密在低成本边缘设备上是实际可行的，加密开销在可接受范围内，不会违反协议的实时性要求，解决了ICS设备长期存在的安全通信问题。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS), and many simple Internet of Things (IoT) devices, commonly communicate using unencrypted or unauthenticated protocols. For ICS this is an historical carryover since the introduction of these systems predated practical lightweight cryptography. As the processing power of small devices has grown exponentially at the same time as new, more efficient encryption algorithms have become available, end device encryption of communication protocols is becoming much more practical, but is still not widely used with ICS protocols such as Modbus and IEC61850 (GOOSE) which have tight requirements for both latency and variance. Newer micro-processors can also present challenges both to measurement and use, since features such as dynamic frequency scaling can significantly impact performance measurements. In this paper, we measured the time cost of adding encryption into the communication cycle of low-cost edge devices using ChaCha20-Poly1305, and show that in the worst case the encryption cycle took less than 7.1 percent of the latency requirements of Goose, and less than 3% for IEC-60834-1 on Raspberry PI 4, and an Intel N95 Mini PC, which is well within the specified latency requirements for these protocols.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Decidability of Quantum Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18368)
*Kenji Tokuo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 使用Harrop引理证明量子模态逻辑的可判定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑系统的可判定性指存在算法能判定任意给定公式是否为定理，本文旨在证明量子模态逻辑的可判定性

Method: 应用Harrop引理来证明量子模态逻辑的可判定性

Result: 成功证明了量子模态逻辑是可判定的

Conclusion: 通过Harrop引理，量子模态逻辑的可判定性得以确立，为该逻辑系统的算法验证提供了理论基础

Abstract: The decidability of a logical system refers to the existence of an algorithm that can determine whether any given formula in that system is a theorem. In this paper, Harrop's lemma is used to prove the decidability of quantum modal logic.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Final Report for the Workshop on Robotics & AI in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18130)
*Juan P Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARE研讨会汇集医疗机器人AI领域专家，讨论建立国家卓越中心以协调研究、填补数据/评估/监管/培训等关键缺口，推动智能机器人系统在临床转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域对机器人AI系统的需求加速增长，但存在数据可用性、标准化评估方法、监管路径和劳动力培训等关键缺口，阻碍智能机器人系统在手术、诊断、康复和辅助等场景的部署。需要协调工程创新与临床优先事项，强调安全性、可靠性和转化准备。

Method: 通过研讨会形式，汇集研究人员、临床医生、行业创新者和联邦利益相关者，通过主题演讲、小组讨论和分组会议，识别关键挑战和机遇，形成建立国家AI与机器人卓越医学中心（CARE）的共识。

Result: 研讨会达成广泛共识，迫切需要建立国家CARE中心，确定了优先研究方向：人机协作、可信自主性、仿真与数字孪生、多模态感知、生成式AI在临床工作流中的伦理整合。同时明确了高质量数据集、共享测试平台、自主手术系统、临床基准和跨学科培训机制的需求。

Conclusion: AI赋能的机器人技术具有改善精准度、减轻医护人员负担、扩大专科护理可及性、提升患者预后的变革潜力，特别是在资源匮乏地区和高风险手术领域。建立国家协调中心对于加速医疗机器人AI的临床转化至关重要。

Abstract: The CARE Workshop on Robotics and AI in Medicine, held on December 1, 2025 in Indianapolis, convened leading researchers, clinicians, industry innovators, and federal stakeholders to shape a national vision for advancing robotics and artificial intelligence in healthcare. The event highlighted the accelerating need for coordinated research efforts that bridge engineering innovation with real clinical priorities, emphasizing safety, reliability, and translational readiness with an emphasis on the use of robotics and AI to achieve this readiness goal.
  Across keynotes, panels, and breakout sessions, participants underscored critical gaps in data availability, standardized evaluation methods, regulatory pathways, and workforce training that hinder the deployment of intelligent robotic systems in surgical, diagnostic, rehabilitative, and assistive contexts. Discussions emphasized the transformative potential of AI enabled robotics to improve precision, reduce provider burden, expand access to specialized care, and enhance patient outcomes particularly in undeserved regions and high risk procedural domains. Special attention was given to austere settings, disaster and relief and military settings.
  The workshop demonstrated broad consensus on the urgency of establishing a national Center for AI and Robotic Excellence in medicine (CARE). Stakeholders identified priority research thrusts including human robot collaboration, trustworthy autonomy, simulation and digital twins, multi modal sensing, and ethical integration of generative AI into clinical workflows. Participants also articulated the need for high quality datasets, shared test beds, autonomous surgical systems, clinically grounded benchmarks, and sustained interdisciplinary training mechanisms.

</details>


### [117] [GoalVLM: VLM-driven Object Goal Navigation for Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18210)
*MoniJesu James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalVLM：基于视觉语言模型的零样本开放词汇多智能体目标导航框架，无需任务特定训练即可实现自由形式语言目标导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标导航方法局限于地面机器人和封闭词汇集，现有多智能体方法依赖预计算概率图且无法泛化到测试时的新目标。需要开发能够理解自由形式语言目标、无需重训练的零样本导航系统。

Method: 集成视觉语言模型到决策循环，使用SAM3进行文本提示检测分割，SpaceOM进行空间推理。每个智能体通过深度投影体素溅射构建BEV语义地图，Goal Projector通过校准深度反投影检测结果进行目标定位。约束引导推理层通过结构化提示链评估前沿区域。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench val_unseen上（360个多子任务场景，1032个顺序目标子任务），2个智能体的GoalVLM达到55.8%子任务成功率和18.3%SPL，与最先进方法竞争且无需任务特定训练。消融研究确认了VLM引导前沿推理和深度投影目标定位的贡献。

Conclusion: GoalVLM展示了将视觉语言模型集成到多智能体导航系统中的可行性，实现了零样本开放词汇目标导航，为无需任务特定训练的可扩展导航系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has traditionally been limited to ground robots with closed-set object vocabularies. Existing multi-agent approaches depend on precomputed probabilistic graphs tied to fixed category sets, precluding generalization to novel goals at test time.
  We present GoalVLM, a cooperative multi-agent framework for zero-shot, open-vocabulary object navigation. GoalVLM integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) directly into the decision loop, SAM3 for text-prompted detection and segmentation, and SpaceOM for spatial reasoning, enabling agents to interpret free-form language goals and score frontiers via zero-shot semantic priors without retraining. Each agent builds a BEV semantic map from depth-projected voxel splatting, while a Goal Projector back-projects detections through calibrated depth into the map for reliable goal localization. A constraint-guided reasoning layer evaluates frontiers through a structured prompt chain (scene captioning, room-type classification, perception gating, multi-frontier ranking), injecting commonsense priors into exploration.
  We evaluate GoalVLM on GOAT-Bench val_unseen (360 multi-subtask episodes, 1032 sequential object-goal subtasks, HM3D scenes), where each episode requires navigating to a chain of 5-7 open-vocabulary targets. GoalVLM with N=2 agents achieves 55.8% subtask SR and 18.3% SPL, competitive with state-of-the-art methods while requiring no task-specific training. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of VLM-guided frontier reasoning and depth-projected goal localization.

</details>


### [118] [ReDAG-RT: Global Rate-Priority Scheduling for Real-Time Multi-DAG Execution in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18238)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan,Rafid Mostafiz,Bikash Kumar Paul,Md. Abir Hossain,Ziaur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDAGRT：一种用于ROS 2的确定性多DAG调度框架，通过用户空间全局调度解决回调争用和优先级反转问题，提高实时性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2默认执行器采用尽力而为调度，缺乏跨DAG优先级强制执行，导致回调争用、结构性优先级反转和截止期限不稳定，限制了在时间关键和安全关键系统中的应用

Method: 提出ReDAGRT框架，引入基于速率的优先级全局就绪队列，按激活率排序回调，强制执行每DAG并发边界，缓解跨图优先级反转，无需修改ROS 2 API、执行器接口或底层操作系统调度器
