Result: 在ROS 2 Humble环境中测试显示：截止期限错过率降低29.7%，第99百分位响应时间减少42.9%，在可比利用率下比MultiThreadedExecutor提升13.7%，非对称每DAG并发边界进一步减少40.8%的干扰

Conclusion: ReDAGRT证明确定性可分析的多DAG调度可以在ROS 2用户空间执行层完全实现，为安全关键系统中的实时机器人中间件提供了实用基础

Abstract: ROS 2 has become a dominant middleware for robotic systems, where perception, estimation, planning, and control pipelines are structured as directed acyclic graphs of callbacks executed under a shared executor. However, default ROS 2 executors use best-effort dispatch without cross-DAG priority enforcement, leading to callback contention, structural priority inversion, and deadline instability under concurrent workloads. These limitations restrict deployment in time-critical and safety-sensitive cyber-physical systems. This paper presents ReDAGRT, a user-space global scheduling framework for deterministic multi-DAG execution in unmodified ROS 2. The framework introduces a Rate-Priority driven global ready queue that orders callbacks by activation rate, enforces per-DAG concurrency bounds, and mitigates cross-graph priority inversion without modifying the ROS 2 API, executor interface, or underlying operating system scheduler. We formalize a multi-DAG task model for ROS 2 callback pipelines and analyze cross-DAG interference under Rate-Priority scheduling. Response-time recurrences and schedulability conditions are derived within classical Rate-Monotonic theory. Experiments in a ROS 2 Humble environment compare ReDAGRT against SingleThreadedExecutor and MultiThreadedExecutor using synthetic multi-DAG workloads. Results show up to 29.7 percent reduction in deadline miss rate, 42.9 percent reduction in 99th percentile response time, and 13.7 percent improvement over MultiThreadedExecutor under comparable utilization. Asymmetric per-DAG concurrency bounds further reduce interference by 40.8 percent. These results demonstrate that deterministic and analyzable multi-DAG scheduling can be achieved entirely in the ROS 2 user-space execution layer, providing a practical foundation for real-time robotic middleware in safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [119] [Rapid Adaptation of Particle Dynamics for Generalized Deformable Object Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18246)
*Bohan Wu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPiD：一种两阶段方法，通过快速运动适应（RMA）扩展来处理未知动力学的可变形物体操作，在真实世界中实现超过80%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可变形物体操作中，物体的动力学参数（包括质量、位置和形状变化）对成功执行任务至关重要。现有RMA方法主要处理刚性物体，无法直接应用于可变形物体，因为后者需要额外考虑形状变化。

Method: RAPiD采用两阶段方法：1）在仿真中学习基于物体动力学嵌入的视觉运动策略，嵌入编码来自特权信息（质量、真实粒子位置）；2）学习使用非特权信息（机器人视觉观测和动作）推断该嵌入，使策略能迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在具有22自由度的移动机械手上，RAPiD在两个基于视觉的可变形物体移动操作任务中，在不同物体动力学、类别和实例下，实现了超过80%的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过利用仿真中的真实粒子位置信息捕捉形状变化，RAPiD成功将RMA扩展到可变形物体操作领域，实现了从仿真到真实世界的有效迁移。

Abstract: We address the challenge of learning to manipulate deformable objects with unknown dynamics. In non-rigid objects, the dynamics parameters define how they react to interactions -- how they stretch, bend, compress, and move -- and they are critical to determining the optimal actions to perform a manipulation task successfully. In other robotic domains, such as legged locomotion and in-hand rigid object manipulation, state-of-the-art approaches can handle unknown dynamics using Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA). Through a supervised procedure in simulation that encodes each rigid object's dynamics, such as mass and position, these approaches learn a policy that conditions actions on a vector of latent dynamic parameters inferred from sequences of state-actions. However, in deformable object manipulation, the object's dynamics not only includes its mass and position, but also how the shape of the object changes. Our key insight is that the recent ground-truth particle positions of a deformable object in simulation capture changes in the object's shape, making it possible to extend RMA to deformable object manipulation. This key insight allows us to develop RAPiD, a two-phase method that learns to perform real-robot deformable object mobile manipulation by: 1) learning a visuomotor policy conditioned on the object's dynamics embedding, which is encoded from the object's privileged information in simulation, such as its mass and ground-truth particle positions, and 2) learning to infer this embedding using non-privileged information instead, such as robot visual observations and actions, so that the learned policy can transfer to the real world. On a mobile manipulator with 22 degrees of freedom, RAPiD enables over 80%+ success rates across two vision-based deformable object mobile manipulation tasks in the real world, under various object dynamics, categories, and instances.

</details>


### [120] [Manufacturing Micro-Patterned Surfaces with Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18260)
*Annalisa T. Taylor,Malachi Landis,Ping Guo,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人系统使用遍历控制算法协调制造微图案表面，通过压缩轨迹历史通信实现复杂覆盖目标，降低金属表面摩擦系数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前制造技术无法大规模生产微图案表面，因为高成本设备和低效的覆盖技术（如光栅扫描）。需要开发能够协调多个机器人的制造系统来实现规模化生产。

Method: 使用多个配备图案化工具的机器人，采用遍历控制算法协调覆盖任务。机器人通过通信压缩的轨迹历史表示来划分复杂的覆盖目标，在仿真和实验中进行验证。

Result: 机器人能够通过压缩轨迹历史通信有效划分复杂覆盖目标，机器人制造的图案化表面能够降低金属表面的摩擦系数。分布式多机器人系统能够协调制造以前无法规模化生产的产品。

Conclusion: 分布式多机器人系统使用遍历控制算法可以协调制造微图案表面，实现规模化生产，为以前无法大规模制造的表面处理技术提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Applying micro-patterns to surfaces has been shown to impart useful physical properties such as drag reduction and hydrophobicity. However, current manufacturing techniques cannot produce micro-patterned surfaces at scale due to high-cost machinery and inefficient coverage techniques such as raster-scanning. In this work, we use multiple robots, each equipped with a patterning tool, to manufacture these surfaces. To allow these robots to coordinate during the patterning task, we use the ergodic control algorithm, which specifies coverage objectives using distributions. We demonstrate that robots can divide complicated coverage objectives by communicating compressed representations of their trajectory history both in simulations and experimental trials. Further, we show that robot-produced patterning can lower the coefficient of friction of metallic surfaces. This work demonstrates that distributed multi-robot systems can coordinate to manufacture products that were previously unrealizable at scale.

</details>


### [121] [HRI-SA: A Multimodal Dataset for Online Assessment of Human Situational Awareness during Remote Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18344)
*Hashini Senaratne,Richard Attfield,Samith Widhanapathirana,David Howard,Cecile Paris,Dana Kulic,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRI-SA数据集：首个支持人机团队中情境感知在线评估的多模态公开数据集，包含眼动、生理信号等数据，用于检测情境感知延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高工作负荷和动态条件下，操作员常出现情境感知（SA）缺口，但现有SA测量方法要么干扰任务流程，要么无法捕捉实时波动，缺乏支持在线SA评估的公开数据集

Method: 创建HRI-SA多模态数据集，包含30名参与者在搜救人机团队任务中的眼动、瞳孔直径、生物信号、用户交互和机器人数据；通过测量援助需求出现到解决的时间获得两种SA延迟（感知和理解）的真实标签；使用标准机器学习模型评估眼动特征和上下文特征检测感知SA延迟的效果

Result: 仅使用眼动特征就能有效分类感知SA延迟（召回率=88.91%，F1=67.63%）；通过上下文数据融合，性能进一步提升（召回率=91.51%，F1=80.38%）

Conclusion: 本文贡献了首个支持人机团队任务中SA系统评估的公开数据集，同时证明了通用眼动特征在远程人机团队中连续检测感知SA延迟的潜力

Abstract: Maintaining situational awareness (SA) is critical in human-robot teams. Yet, under high workload and dynamic conditions, operators often experience SA gaps. Automated detection of SA gaps could provide timely assistance for operators. However, conventional SA measures either disrupt task flow or cannot capture real-time fluctuations, limiting their operational utility. To the best of our knowledge, no publicly available dataset currently supports the systematic evaluation of online human SA assessment in human-robot teaming. To advance the development of online SA assessment tools, we introduce HRI-SA, a multimodal dataset from 30 participants in a realistic search-and-rescue human-robot teaming context, incorporating eye movements, pupil diameter, biosignals, user interactions, and robot data. The experimental protocol included predefined events requiring timely operator assistance, with ground truth SA latency of two types (perceptual and comprehension) systematically obtained by measuring the time between assistance need onset and resolution. We illustrate the utility of this dataset by evaluating standard machine learning models for detecting perceptual SA latencies using generic eye-tracking features and contextual features. Results show that eye-tracking features alone effectively classified perceptual SA latency (recall=88.91%, F1=67.63%) using leave-one-group-out cross-validation, with performance improved through contextual data fusion (recall=91.51%, F1=80.38%). This paper contributes the first public dataset supporting the systematic evaluation of SA throughout a human-robot teaming mission, while also demonstrating the potential of generic eye-tracking features for continuous perceptual SA latency detection in remote human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [122] [SG-CoT: An Ambiguity-Aware Robotic Planning Framework using Scene Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18271)
*Akshat Rana,Peeyush Agarwal,K. P. S. Rana,Amarjit Malhotra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-CoT是一个两阶段框架，通过让LLM迭代查询场景图来检测和澄清机器人规划中的歧义问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歧义性是大型语言模型作为机器人规划器面临的主要挑战，需要提高在模糊情况下的规划可靠性

Method: 1) 从输入观测构建结构化场景图表示；2) LLM配备检索函数查询相关场景图部分；3) 迭代检测歧义并询问澄清问题

Result: SG-CoT持续优于先前方法：问题准确率至少提升10%，单智能体成功率至少提升4%，多智能体环境至少提升15%

Conclusion: SG-CoT通过场景图增强的思维链机制有效提高机器人规划在歧义情况下的可靠性和泛化能力

Abstract: Ambiguity poses a major challenge to large language models (LLMs) used as robotic planners. In this letter, we present Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT), a two-stage framework where LLMs iteratively query a scene graph representation of the environment to detect and clarify ambiguities. First, a structured scene graph representation of the environment is constructed from input observations, capturing objects, their attributes, and relationships with other objects. Second, the LLM is equipped with retrieval functions to query portions of the scene graph that are relevant to the provided instruction. This grounds the reasoning process of the LLM in the observation, increasing the reliability of robotic planners under ambiguous situations. SG-CoT also allows the LLM to identify the source of ambiguity and pose a relevant disambiguation question to the user or another robot. Extensive experimentation demonstrates that SG-CoT consistently outperforms prior methods, with a minimum of 10% improvement in question accuracy and a minimum success rate increase of 4% in single-agent and 15% in multi-agent environments, validating its effectiveness for more generalizable robot planning.

</details>


### [123] [Sparse3DTrack: Monocular 3D Object Tracking Using Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18298)
*Nikhil Gosala,B. Ravi Kiran,Senthil Yogamani,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个稀疏监督的单目3D目标跟踪框架，通过将任务分解为2D查询匹配和3D几何估计两个子问题，利用时空一致性从稀疏标注生成高质量伪标签，显著提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的单目3D目标跟踪方法需要密集的3D标注，这些标注获取成本高且难以扩展。为了解决这一根本限制，本文提出了首个稀疏监督框架。

Method: 将任务分解为两个顺序子问题：2D查询匹配和3D几何估计。两个组件都利用图像序列的时空一致性来增强稀疏标注样本，学习丰富的2D和3D场景表示。基于这些学习到的线索，模型自动生成整个视频的高质量3D伪标签，将稀疏监督转化为密集的3D轨迹标注。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著提高了跟踪性能，在每条轨迹最多使用四个真实标注的情况下，性能提升高达15.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏监督框架通过将稀疏监督转化为密集伪标签，使现有全监督跟踪器能够在极端标签稀疏性下有效运行，解决了单目3D目标跟踪中标注成本高的关键问题。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object tracking aims to estimate temporally consistent 3D object poses across video frames, enabling autonomous agents to reason about scene dynamics. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches are fully supervised and rely on dense 3D annotations over long video sequences, which are expensive to obtain and difficult to scale. In this work, we address this fundamental limitation by proposing the first sparsely supervised framework for monocular 3D object tracking. Our approach decomposes the task into two sequential sub-problems: 2D query matching and 3D geometry estimation. Both components leverage the spatio-temporal consistency of image sequences to augment a sparse set of labeled samples and learn rich 2D and 3D representations of the scene. Leveraging these learned cues, our model automatically generates high-quality 3D pseudolabels across entire videos, effectively transforming sparse supervision into dense 3D track annotations. This enables existing fully-supervised trackers to effectively operate under extreme label sparsity. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and nuScenes datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves tracking performance, achieving an improvement of up to 15.50 p.p. while using at most four ground truth annotations per track.

</details>


### [124] [Proprioceptive-only State Estimation for Legged Robots with Set-Coverage Measurements of Learned Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18308)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于集合覆盖噪声建模的腿式机器人本体感知状态估计框架，无需高斯噪声假设，在有限训练数据下保持估计一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于高斯噪声假设的本体感知状态估计方法在有限训练数据下容易失效，导致估计不一致和漂移问题，需要更鲁棒的噪声建模方法

Method: 使用集合覆盖语句表征测量噪声，不假设任何分布，开发实用且计算成本低的方法将集合覆盖测量与高斯滤波器系统结合

Result: 在仿真和两个真实世界四足机器人数据集上验证，相比高斯基线方法，提出的方法保持一致性且在实际噪声场景下不易漂移

Conclusion: 基于集合覆盖的噪声建模为腿式机器人本体感知状态估计提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，特别适用于训练数据有限的实际应用场景

Abstract: Proprioceptive-only state estimation is attractive for legged robots since it is computationally cheaper and is unaffected by perceptually degraded conditions. The history of joint-level measurements contains rich information that can be used to infer the dynamics of the system and subsequently produce navigational measurements. Recent approaches produce these estimates with learned measurement models and fuse with IMU data, under a Gaussian noise assumption. However, this assumption can easily break down with limited training data and render the estimates inconsistent and potentially divergent. In this work, we propose a proprioceptive-only state estimation framework for legged robots that characterizes the measurement noise using set-coverage statements that do not assume any distribution. We develop a practical and computationally inexpensive method to use these set-coverage measurements with a Gaussian filter in a systematic way. We validate the approach in both simulation and two real-world quadrupedal datasets. Comparison with the Gaussian baselines shows that our proposed method remains consistent and is not prone to drift under real noise scenarios.

</details>


### [125] [Lightweight Model Predictive Control for Spacecraft Rendezvous Attitude Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18921)
*Peter Stadler,Alexander Meinert,Niklas Baldauf,Alen Turnwald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种轻量级模型预测控制方法，用于航天器交会同步期间使用反作用轮进行姿态跟踪，通过新颖的姿态偏差公式实现线性约束，并在资源受限的航天器平台上验证了实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对航天器交会同步任务中姿态控制的资源约束问题，特别是在使用反作用轮时，需要开发计算效率高、内存占用少的控制方法，以适应New Space平台中常见的商业现货设备。

Method: 提出了两种基于新颖姿态偏差公式的MPC方法：单环MPC和双环MPC。双环MPC在内部环路中嵌入了稳定反馈控制器，形成线性时不变系统。使用CasADi进行实现（包括自动代码生成），在多种求解器上评估，并在Basilisk天体动力学仿真框架中验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，两种方法在提高跟踪精度的同时，减少了计算量和内存消耗。嵌入式部署到ARM Cortex-M7处理器（代表New Space平台使用的商业现货设备）证实了这些方法的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级MPC方法特别适用于资源受限的航天器交会任务中的姿态控制，在商业现货设备上实现了实时性能，为New Space平台提供了可行的机载控制解决方案。

Abstract: This work introduces two lightweight model predictive control (MPC) approaches for attitude tracking with reaction wheels during spacecraft rendezvous synchronization. Both approaches are based on a novel attitude deviation formulation, which enables the use of inherently linear constraints on angular velocity. We develop a single-loop and a dual-loop MPC; the latter embeds a stabilizing feedback controller within the inner loop, yielding a linear time-invariant system. Both controllers are implemented with CasADi - including automatic code generation - evaluated across various solvers, and validated within the Basilisk astrodynamics simulation framework. The experimental results demonstrate improved tracking accuracy alongside reductions in computational effort and memory consumption. Finally, embedded delivery to an ARM Cortex-M7 - representative of commercial off-the-shelf devices used in New Space platforms - confirms the real-time feasibility of these approaches and highlights their suitability for onboard attitude control in resource-constrained spacecraft rendezvous missions.

</details>


### [126] [DriveVLM-RL: Neuroscience-Inspired Reinforcement Learning with Vision-Language Models for Safe and Deployable Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18315)
*Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Zhengyang Wan,Yansong Qu,Junwei You,Sicong Jiang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveVLM-RL：一种将视觉语言模型集成到强化学习中的神经科学启发框架，用于安全、可部署的自动驾驶，通过双通路架构实现语义奖励学习，并在部署时移除VLM组件以确保实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法依赖人工设计的奖励或稀疏碰撞信号，无法捕捉安全驾驶所需的丰富上下文理解，且在真实世界中不可避免地需要进行不安全探索。视觉语言模型虽具备语义理解能力，但高推理延迟和幻觉问题阻碍了其在实时车辆控制中的直接应用。

Method: 提出DriveVLM-RL框架，采用双通路架构：静态通路使用CLIP对比语言目标进行连续空间安全评估；动态通路使用轻量级检测器和大型VLM进行注意力门控的多帧语义风险推理。通过分层奖励合成机制融合语义信号与车辆状态，异步训练管道将昂贵的VLM推理与环境交互解耦。所有VLM组件仅在离线训练中使用，部署时移除以确保实时可行性。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器中的实验显示，在碰撞避免、任务成功率和多样化交通场景的泛化能力方面均有显著提升，包括在没有显式碰撞惩罚的设置下表现出强大的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DriveVLM-RL为将基础模型集成到自动驾驶中提供了一种实用范式，既利用了VLM的语义理解能力，又不影响系统的实时可行性，实现了安全决策与部署实用性的平衡。

Abstract: Ensuring safe decision-making in autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge despite rapid advances in end-to-end learning approaches. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods rely on manually engineered rewards or sparse collision signals, which fail to capture the rich contextual understanding required for safe driving and make unsafe exploration unavoidable in real-world settings. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic understanding capabilities; however, their high inference latency and susceptibility to hallucination hinder direct application to real-time vehicle control. To address these limitations, this paper proposes DriveVLM-RL, a neuroscience-inspired framework that integrates VLMs into RL through a dual-pathway architecture for safe and deployable autonomous driving. The framework decomposes semantic reward learning into a Static Pathway for continuous spatial safety assessment using CLIP-based contrasting language goals, and a Dynamic Pathway for attention-gated multi-frame semantic risk reasoning using a lightweight detector and a large VLM. A hierarchical reward synthesis mechanism fuses semantic signals with vehicle states, while an asynchronous training pipeline decouples expensive VLM inference from environment interaction. All VLM components are used only during offline training and are removed at deployment, ensuring real-time feasibility. Experiments in the CARLA simulator show significant improvements in collision avoidance, task success, and generalization across diverse traffic scenarios, including strong robustness under settings without explicit collision penalties. These results demonstrate that DriveVLM-RL provides a practical paradigm for integrating foundation models into autonomous driving without compromising real-time feasibility. Demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/DriveVLM-RL-website/

</details>


### [127] [NavTrust: Benchmarking Trustworthiness for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19229)
*Huaide Jiang,Yash Chaudhary,Yuping Wang,Zehao Wang,Raghav Sharma,Manan Mehta,Yang Zhou,Lichao Sun,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavTrust是首个系统评估视觉语言导航和物体目标导航在现实世界损坏条件下的统一基准，通过模拟RGB、深度和指令的多种损坏来测试导航模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航研究主要在理想条件下评估模型性能，忽视了现实世界中可能出现的各种输入损坏（如传感器噪声、光照变化、指令歧义等），这限制了导航系统在实际应用中的可信度。

Method: 构建NavTrust基准，系统性地对RGB、深度和指令三种输入模态施加现实场景中的多种损坏，包括传感器噪声、光照变化、遮挡、指令模糊等，并在统一框架下评估其对导航性能的影响。

Result: 对7个最先进的导航方法进行广泛评估，发现所有方法在现实损坏下都出现显著性能下降；同时评估了4种缓解策略，基于Uni-NaVid和ETPNav的模型在真实移动机器人上表现出改进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NavTrust揭示了当前导航系统在现实损坏下的严重鲁棒性缺陷，为开发更可信的具身导航系统提供了评估框架和改进方向，强调了在实际部署中考虑输入损坏的重要性。

Abstract: There are two major categories of embodied navigation: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), where agents navigate by following natural language instructions; and Object-Goal Navigation (OGN), where agents navigate to a specified target object. However, existing work primarily evaluates model performance under nominal conditions, overlooking the potential corruptions that arise in real-world settings. To address this gap, we present NavTrust, a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts input modalities, including RGB, depth, and instructions, in realistic scenarios and evaluates their impact on navigation performance. To our best knowledge, NavTrust is the first benchmark that exposes embodied navigation agents to diverse RGB-Depth corruptions and instruction variations in a unified framework. Our extensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art approaches reveals substantial performance degradation under realistic corruptions, which highlights critical robustness gaps and provides a roadmap toward more trustworthy embodied navigation systems. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate four distinct mitigation strategies to enhance robustness against RGB-Depth and instructions corruptions. Our base models include Uni-NaVid and ETPNav. We deployed them on a real mobile robot and observed improved robustness to corruptions. The project website is: https://navtrust.github.io.

</details>


### [128] [ManiDreams: An Open-Source Library for Robust Object Manipulation via Uncertainty-aware Task-specific Intuitive Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18336)
*Gaotian Wang,Kejia Ren,Andrew S. Morgan,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiDreams是一个用于不确定性感知的机器人操作规划框架，通过可组合的抽象实现分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测和声明式约束规范，在不重新训练的情况下增强基础策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学模型主要关注最小化预测误差，但现实世界操作本质上存在不确定性。作者认为鲁棒操作规划是一个集成问题，需要在规划循环中表示、传播和约束不确定性，而不仅仅在训练中抑制。

Method: 提出ManiDreams框架，包含三个核心抽象：分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测、声明式约束规范。采用sample-predict-constrain循环包装任何基础策略，评估候选动作的分布结果。明确处理感知、参数和结构三种不确定性来源。

Result: 在ManiSkill任务上的实验表明，ManiDreams在各种扰动下保持鲁棒性能，而强化学习基线显著退化。在推动、抓取、捕捉等任务以及真实世界部署中展示了跨不同策略、优化器、物理后端和执行器的灵活性。

Conclusion: ManiDreams为不确定性感知的操作规划提供了一个模块化框架，通过集成不确定性表示、传播和约束到规划循环中，增强了机器人操作的鲁棒性，无需重新训练基础策略。

Abstract: Dynamics models, whether simulators or learned world models, have long been central to robotic manipulation, but most focus on minimizing prediction error rather than confronting a more fundamental challenge: real-world manipulation is inherently uncertain. We argue that robust manipulation under uncertainty is fundamentally an integration problem: uncertainties must be represented, propagated, and constrained within the planning loop, not merely suppressed during training. We present and open-source ManiDreams, a modular framework for uncertainty-aware manipulation planning over intuitive physics models. It realizes this integration through composable abstractions for distributional state representation, backend-agnostic dynamics prediction, and declarative constraint specification for action optimization. The framework explicitly addresses three sources of uncertainty: perceptual, parametric, and structural. It wraps any base policy with a sample-predict-constrain loop that evaluates candidate actions against distributional outcomes, adding robustness without retraining. Experiments on ManiSkill tasks show that ManiDreams maintains robust performance under various perturbations where the RL baseline degrades significantly. Runnable examples on pushing, picking, catching, and real-world deployment demonstrate flexibility across different policies, optimizers, physics backends, and executors. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/Rice-RobotPI-Lab/ManiDreams

</details>


### [129] [Shifting Uncertainty to Critical Moments: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification for VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18342)
*Yanchuan Tang,Taowen Wang,Yuefei Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Qiang Guan,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于VLA机器人策略的改进不确定性量化方法，通过最大滑动窗口池化、运动感知稳定性加权和自由度自适应校准来提升故障预测准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏可靠的内省能力，传统基于令牌级不确定性均值的方法会稀释安全关键的不确定性尖峰，导致故障预测不准确

Method: 1) 使用基于最大值的滑动窗口池化保留瞬态风险信号；2) 应用运动感知稳定性加权强调与不稳定行为相关的高频动作振荡；3) 通过贝叶斯优化进行自由度自适应校准，优先考虑运动学关键轴

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法显著提高了故障预测准确性，并为故障检测提供了更可靠的信号，可支持下游人机交互干预

Conclusion: 提出的统一不确定性量化方法能有效捕捉VLA策略中的瞬态风险信号，为机器人安全监控和人机交互提供了更可靠的不确定性估计框架

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic policies by mapping visual observations and language instructions to low-level actions, but they often lack reliable introspection. A common practice is to compute a token-level uncertainty signal and take its mean over a rollout. However, mean aggregation can dilute short-lived but safety-critical uncertainty spikes in continuous control. In particular, successful rollouts may contain localized high-entropy segments due to benign noise or non-critical micro-adjustments, while failure rollouts can appear low-entropy for most timesteps and only exhibit brief spikes near the onset of failure. We propose a unified uncertainty quantification approach for predicting rollout success versus failure that (1) uses max-based sliding window pooling to preserve transient risk signals, (2) applies motion-aware stability weighting to emphasize high-frequency action oscillations associated with unstable behaviors, and (3) performs DoF-adaptive calibration via Bayesian Optimization to prioritize kinematically critical axes. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method substantially improves failure prediction accuracy and yields more reliable signals for failure detection, which can support downstream human-in-the-loop interventions.

</details>


### [130] [Multi-material Direct Ink Writing and Embroidery for Stretchable Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18354)
*Lukas Cha,Ryman Hashem,Ria Prakash,Tanguy Declety,Wenze Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将多材料直接墨水书写与自动刺绣相结合的纺织兼容制造工作流，用于在服装中直接嵌入可拉伸应变传感器，实现运动监测应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动表现追踪、康复和损伤预防的可穿戴传感系统需求增长，需要结合舒适性、耐用性和精确运动检测的智能服装。

Method: 采用多材料直接墨水书写与自动刺绣结合的混合制造工艺：顺序打印硅胶-碳脂-硅胶三层结构，通过自动刺绣提供机械固定和电气接口一体化。

Result: 传感器拉伸性达120%应变，60%应变内近似线性响应(R²=0.99)，应变系数31.4，滞后22.9%；80次循环测试显示基线漂移0.135%/周期；硅胶-织物界面在大变形下保持完整；集成到肘膝套中成功监测关节角度。

Conclusion: 刺绣集成方法同时解决机械固定和电气接口问题，为将印刷可拉伸电子集成到纺织系统中提供了可重复、可扩展的途径，适用于运动捕捉和软机器人应用。

Abstract: The development of wearable sensing systems for sports performance tracking, rehabilitation, and injury prevention has driven growing demand for smart garments that combine comfort, durability, and accurate motion detection. This paper presents a textile-compatible fabrication workflow that integrates multi-material direct ink writing with automated embroidery to create stretchable strain sensors directly embedded into garments. The process combines sequential multi-material printing of a silicone-carbon grease-silicone stack with automated embroidery that provides both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing in a single step. The resulting hybrid sensor demonstrates stretchability up to 120% strain while maintaining electrical continuity, with approximately linear behaviour up to 60% strain (R^2 = 0.99), a gauge factor of 31.4, and hysteresis of 22.9%. Repeated loading-unloading tests over 80 cycles show baseline and peak drift of 0.135% and 0.236% per cycle, respectively, indicating moderate cycle-to-cycle stability. Mechanical testing further confirms that the silicone-fabric interface remains intact under large deformation, with failure occurring in the textile rather than at the stitched boundary. As a preliminary proof of concept, the sensor was integrated into wearable elbow and knee sleeves for joint angle monitoring, showing a clear correlation between normalised resistance change and bending angle. By addressing both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing through embroidery-based integration, this approach provides a reproducible and scalable pathway for incorporating printed stretchable electronics into textile systems for motion capture and soft robotic applications.

</details>


### [131] [Contact Status Recognition and Slip Detection with a Bio-inspired Tactile Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18370)
*Chengxiao He,Wenhui Yang,Hongliang Zhao,Jiacheng Lv,Yuzhe Shao,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于五指仿生手的多模态触觉反馈，通过接触状态识别和分箱技术实现滑移检测，避免传统阈值方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中稳定可靠的抓取至关重要，特别是对于易碎和光滑物体。传统方法假设物体已被牢固抓取，但在非结构化环境中需要滑移检测和及时预防。人类手部触觉感知启发了分布式触觉传感的设计。

Method: 1) 在五指仿生手的软皮肤中分布式嵌入触觉传感元件，形成24个触觉通道；2) 将滑移检测问题转化为接触状态识别，结合分箱技术；3) 对24通道触觉信号进行离散小波变换，从不同时间和频带提取17个特征；4) 使用最优的120个特征进行状态识别；5) 基于训练好的接触状态识别模型进行滑移检测。

Result: 1) 在三种不同滑动速度和六种材料下，测试准确率达到96.39%；2) 应用于四种未见新材料时，仍保持91.95%的高准确率，验证了方法的泛化能力；3) 基于训练好的接触状态识别模型，滑移检测性能得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出的基于多模态触觉反馈和接触状态识别的方法有效解决了机器人抓取中的滑移检测问题，具有高准确率和良好的泛化能力，优于传统的阈值方法。

Abstract: Stable and reliable grasp is critical to robotic manipulations especially for fragile and glazed objects, where the grasp force requires precise control as too large force possibly damages the objects while small force leads to slip and fall-off. Although it is assumed the objects to manipulate is grasped firmly in advance, slip detection and timely prevention are necessary for a robot in unstructured and universal environments. In this work, we addressed this issue by utilizing multimodal tactile feedback from a five-fingered bio-inspired hand. Motivated by human hands, the tactile sensing elements were distributed and embedded into the soft skin of robotic hand, forming 24 tactile channels in total. Different from the threshold method that was widely employed in most existing works, we converted the slip detection problem to contact status recognition in combination with binning technique first and then detected the slip onset time according to the recognition results. After the 24-channel tactile signals passed through discrete wavelet transform, 17 features were extracted from different time and frequency bands. With the optimal 120 features employed for status recognition, the test accuracy reached 96.39% across three different sliding speeds and six kinds of materials. When applied to four new unseen materials, a high accuracy of 91.95% was still achieved, which further validated the generalization of our proposed method. Finally, the performance of slip detection is verified based on the trained model of contact status recognition.

</details>


### [132] [Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control for Reactive Multi-agent Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18400)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Jeremy Lu,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoC-MPC：一种用于多智能体任务与运动规划的图约束模型预测控制框架，支持部分有序任务、动态智能体协调和扰动恢复，仅通过视觉观测实现鲁棒操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理几何约束序列的方法难以应对部分有序任务和动态智能体分配，通常假设静态分配且无法适应扰动导致的任务分配变化

Method: 提出图约束模型预测控制（GoC-MPC），将广义约束序列框架与MPC集成，通过跟踪3D关键点定义约束，支持部分有序任务和动态协调

Result: 实验表明GoC-MPC相比基线方法获得更高成功率、显著更快的TAMP计算速度和更短整体路径，仅通过视觉观测即可鲁棒解决多智能体操作任务

Conclusion: GoC-MPC为多智能体操作提供了一种高效鲁棒的解决方案，能够处理现实世界扰动，无需训练数据或环境模型

Abstract: Sequences of interdependent geometric constraints are central to many multi-agent Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) problems. However, existing methods for handling such constraint sequences struggle with partially ordered tasks and dynamic agent assignments. They typically assume static assignments and cannot adapt when disturbances alter task allocations. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control (GoC-MPC), a generalized sequence-of-constraints framework integrated with MPC. GoC-MPC naturally supports partially ordered tasks, dynamic agent coordination, and disturbance recovery. By defining constraints over tracked 3D keypoints, our method robustly solves diverse multi-agent manipulation tasks-coordinating agents and adapting online from visual observations alone, without relying on training data or environment models. Experiments demonstrate that GoC-MPC achieves higher success rates, significantly faster TAMP computation, and shorter overall paths compared to recent baselines, establishing it as an efficient and robust solution for multi-agent manipulation under real-world disturbances. Our supplementary video and code can be found at https://sites.google.com/view/goc-mpc/home .

</details>


### [133] [Efficient and Versatile Quadrupedal Skating: Optimal Co-design via Reinforcement Learning and Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18408)
*Hanwen Wang,Zhenlong Fang,Josiah Hanna,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 硬件-控制协同设计方法，通过双层优化框架（上层贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计，下层强化学习训练控制策略）实现四足机器人被动轮滑冰的高效多功能运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮滑冰能减少腿部惯性和提高能量效率，但缺乏直接轮驱动使机械设计与控制紧密耦合，需要系统级方法来充分发挥这种运动模式的潜力

Method: 提出双层优化框架：上层使用贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计空间，下层对每个候选设计使用强化学习训练电机控制策略，实现设计-策略对的协同优化

Result: 优化得到的设计-策略对超越了人工设计的基线，展现出多功能行为如曲棍球式急停（侧转最大化摩擦快速制动）和自对齐运动（自动重新定向以提高行进方向能量效率）

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地研究了四足机器人的动态滑冰运动，通过硬件-控制协同设计方法实现了高效多功能的被动轮滑冰能力

Abstract: In this paper, we present a hardware-control co-design approach that enables efficient and versatile roller skating on quadrupedal robots equipped with passive wheels. Passive-wheel skating reduces leg inertia and improves energy efficiency, particularly at high speeds. However, the absence of direct wheel actuation tightly couples mechanical design and control. To unlock the full potential of this modality, we formulate a bilevel optimization framework: an upper-level Bayesian Optimization searches the mechanical design space, while a lower-level Reinforcement Learning trains a motor control policy for each candidate design. The resulting design-policy pairs not only outperform human-engineered baselines, but also exhibit versatile behaviors such as hockey stop (rapid braking by turning sideways to maximize friction) and self-aligning motion (automatic reorientation to improve energy efficiency in the direction of travel), offering the first system-level study of dynamic skating motion on quadrupedal robots.

</details>


### [134] [MemoAct: Atkinson-Shiffrin-Inspired Memory-Augmented Visuomotor Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18494)
*Liufan Tan,Jiale Li,Gangshan Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoAct：基于Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型的分层记忆策略，通过无损短期记忆实现精确任务状态跟踪，压缩长期记忆实现鲁棒长时程保留，在MemoryRTBench基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强机器人策略通常依赖简单的观察窗口扩展，难以同时实现精确的任务状态跟踪和鲁棒的长时程记忆保留，需要更有效的记忆机制来解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 受Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型启发，提出MemoAct分层记忆策略：1）无损短期记忆确保精确任务状态跟踪；2）压缩长期记忆实现鲁棒长时程保留；3）基于RoboTwin 2.0构建MemoryRTBench基准，专门评估策略在任务状态跟踪和长时程保留方面的能力。

Result: 在模拟和真实场景的广泛实验中，MemoAct相比现有马尔可夫基线和历史感知策略均表现出优越性能，验证了分层记忆架构的有效性。

Conclusion: MemoAct通过分层记忆架构成功解决了记忆增强机器人策略中任务状态跟踪精度与长时程记忆保留之间的权衡问题，为处理记忆依赖任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Memory-augmented robotic policies are essential in handling memory-dependent tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on simple observation window extensions, struggling to simultaneously achieve precise task state tracking and robust long-horizon retention. To overcome these challenges, inspired by the Atkinson-Shiffrin memory model, we propose MemoAct, a hierarchical memory-based policy that leverages distinct memory tiers to tackle specific bottlenecks. Specifically, lossless short-term memory ensures precise task state tracking, while compressed long-term memory enables robust long-horizon retention. To enrich the evaluation landscape, we construct MemoryRTBench based on RoboTwin 2.0, specifically tailored to assess policy capabilities in task state tracking and long-horizon retention. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world scenarios demonstrate that MemoAct achieves superior performance compared to both existing Markovian baselines and history-aware policies. The project page is \href{https://tlf-tlf.github.io/MemoActPage/}{available}.

</details>


### [135] [Robotic Agentic Platform for Intelligent Electric Vehicle Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18520)
*Zachary Allen,Max Conway,Lyle Antieau,Allen Ponraj,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID平台通过集成工业机械臂、RGB-D感知和自动化工具，实现电动汽车电池包的智能拆解，重点研究感知驱动操作、柔性自动化和AI辅助机器人编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车电池回收需求迫切，但电池包拆解仍主要依赖人工，主要挑战在于设计多样性和高可变性，需要开发可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Method: 系统集成龙门式工业机械臂、RGB-D感知和自动螺母拧紧工具；开发开放词汇目标检测管道（mAP50达0.9757）；评估三种一次性紧固件拆卸策略；引入基于LLM的智能体规范，通过结构化工具接口和ROS服务实现高级指令到机器人动作的转换。

Result: 紧固件拆卸成功率：示教位姿97%（24分钟），一次性视觉执行57%（29分钟），视觉伺服83%（36分钟）；基于工具的LLM接口实现100%任务完成率，自动ROS服务发现失败率43.3%；开放词汇检测在螺丝、螺母、母线排等组件上表现可靠。

Conclusion: RAPID平台为系统研究人机协作、智能体机器人编程和自主拆解工作流程提供了实践基础，强调结构化机器人API对可靠LLM驱动控制的重要性，推动可扩展机器人电池回收研究。

Abstract: Electric vehicles (EV) create an urgent need for scalable battery recycling, yet disassembly of EV battery packs remains largely manual due to high design variability. We present our Robotic Agentic Platform for Intelligent Disassembly (RAPID), designed to investigate perception-driven manipulation, flexible automation, and AI-assisted robot programming in realistic recycling scenarios. The system integrates a gantry-mounted industrial manipulator, RGB-D perception, and an automated nut-running tool for fastener removal on a full-scale EV battery pack. An open-vocabulary object detection pipeline achieves 0.9757 mAP50, enabling reliable identification of screws, nuts, busbars, and other components. We experimentally evaluate (n=204) three one-shot fastener removal strategies: taught-in poses (97% success rate, 24 min duration), one-shot vision execution (57%, 29 min), and visual servoing (83%, 36 min), comparing success rate and disassembly time for the battery's top cover fasteners. To support flexible interaction, we introduce agentic AI specifications for robotic disassembly tasks, allowing LLM agents to translate high-level instructions into robot actions through structured tool interfaces and ROS services. We evaluate SmolAgents with GPT-4o-mini and Qwen 3.5 9B/4B on edge hardware. Tool-based interfaces achieve 100% task completion, while automatic ROS service discovery shows 43.3% failure rates, highlighting the need for structured robot APIs for reliable LLM-driven control. This open-source platform enables systematic investigation of human-robot collaboration, agentic robot programming, and increasingly autonomous disassembly workflows, providing a practical foundation for research toward scalable robotic battery recycling.

</details>


### [136] [Scaling Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Robot VLAs with Generative 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18532)
*Andrew Choi,Xinjie Wang,Zhizhong Su,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，生成多样化交互场景进行VLA模型的RL微调，在保持泛化能力的同时显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在矛盾：真实世界RL微调会限制VLA模型的泛化能力，导致过拟合；仿真训练虽能提供多样性但场景设计成本高。需要一种既能保持模型泛化性又能降低人工成本的方法。

Method: 结合3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，自动生成数百个包含独特物体和背景的多样化交互场景，实现可扩展的并行策略学习。使用预训练模仿基线，通过生成的高质量数字孪生和域随机化实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 仿真成功率从9.7%提升至79.8%，任务完成时间加速1.25倍；真实世界成功率从21.7%提升至75%，任务完成时间加速1.13倍。消融研究表明增加场景多样性直接改善零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用3D世界生成模型可以高效生成多样化训练场景，在保持VLA模型泛化能力的同时显著提升RL微调效果，成功实现sim-to-real迁移，为解决机器人VLA模型微调中的泛化与成本矛盾提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The strong performance of large vision-language models (VLMs) trained with reinforcement learning (RL) has motivated similar approaches for fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) models in robotics. Many recent works fine-tune VLAs directly in the real world to avoid addressing the sim-to-real gap. While real-world RL circumvents sim-to-real issues, it inherently limits the generality of the resulting VLA, as scaling scene and object diversity in the physical world is prohibitively difficult. This leads to the paradoxical outcome of transforming a broadly pretrained model into an overfitted, scene-specific policy. Training in simulation can instead provide access to diverse scenes, but designing those scenes is also costly. In this work, we show that VLAs can be RL fine-tuned without sacrificing generality and with reduced labor by leveraging 3D world generative models. Using these models together with a language-driven scene designer, we generate hundreds of diverse interactive scenes containing unique objects and backgrounds, enabling scalable and highly parallel policy learning. Starting from a pretrained imitation baseline, our approach increases simulation success from 9.7% to 79.8% while achieving a 1.25$\times$ speedup in task completion time. We further demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer enabled by the quality of the generated digital twins together with domain randomization, improving real-world success from 21.7% to 75% and achieving a 1.13$\times$ speedup. Finally, we further highlight the benefits of leveraging the effectively unlimited data from 3D world generative models through an ablation study showing that increasing scene diversity directly improves zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [137] [Inductance-Based Force Self-Sensing in Fiber-Reinforced Pneumatic Twisted-and-Coiled Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18555)
*Yunsong Zhang,Tianlin Li,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成导电镍线的自感知纤维增强气动扭卷致动器，通过电感反馈实现固有力量估计和间接位移推断，解决了传统致动器强滞后性和缺乏固有本体感知的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强气动扭卷致动器具有高功率密度和柔顺性，但其强滞后性和缺乏固有本体感知限制了有效的闭环控制。需要开发能够同时提供力量估计和位移推断的自感知致动器。

Method: 集成导电镍线到FR-PTCA中，利用电感反馈实现自感知。开发参数化自感知模型和非线性混合观测器，结合扩展卡尔曼滤波和约束优化，解决电感-力量映射的模糊性并估计致动器状态。

Result: 实验表征显示，在恒定压力下，致动器的电感表现出确定性的低滞后电感-力量关系，与强滞后的电感-长度行为形成对比。所提方法的力量估计精度与外部负载相当，在不同负载条件下保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 集成导电镍线的自感知FR-PTCA通过电感反馈实现了有效的固有力量估计和间接位移推断，解决了传统致动器的滞后性问题，为柔顺机器人的闭环控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fiber-reinforced pneumatic twisted-and-coiled actuators (FR-PTCAs) offer high power density and compliance but their strong hysteresis and lack of intrinsic proprioception limit effective closed-loop control. This paper presents a self-sensing FR-PTCA integrated with a conductive nickel wire that enables intrinsic force estimation and indirect displacement inference via inductance feedback. Experimental characterization reveals that the inductance of the actuator exhibits a deterministic, low-hysteresis inductance-force relationship at constant pressures, in contrast to the strongly hysteretic inductance-length behavior. Leveraging this property, this paper develops a parametric self-sensing model and a nonlinear hybrid observer that integrates an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) with constrained optimization to resolve the ambiguity in the inductance-force mapping and estimate actuator states. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves force estimation accuracy comparable to that of external load cells and maintains robust performance under varying load conditions.

</details>


### [138] [TiBCLaG: A Trigger-induced Bistable Compliant Laparoscopic Grasper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18559)
*Joel J Nellikkunnel,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种单件式、全柔性、双稳态腹腔镜抓取器，通过柔性触发器和柔性夹爪设计，减少零件数量，实现无需持续用户输入的稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腹腔镜抓取器采用多连杆刚性机构，制造公差要求高，导致生产和组装成本昂贵。需要开发更简单、成本更低的替代方案。

Method: 使用双单元梁约束模型作为设计框架，控制V型梁类元件的变形和刚度，实现弹性储能并防止突跳失稳。末端执行器设计为柔性夹爪，通过弹性变形实现自适应抓取。采用非线性有限元分析验证性能，并通过熔融沉积3D打印制造原型。

Result: 制造的原型展示了可靠的双稳态驱动，证实了这种柔性腹腔镜抓取器架构的可行性。设备集成了柔性触发器和柔性夹爪末端执行器，通过控制推杆耦合，实现无需持续用户输入的稳定抓取。

Conclusion: 提出的单件式全柔性双稳态腹腔镜抓取器设计成功消除了多个刚性连杆的需求，减少了零件数量，通过3D打印制造验证了可行性，为低成本腹腔镜器械提供了新方案。

Abstract: Industrial laparoscopic graspers use multi-link rigid mechanisms manufactured to tight tolerances, resulting in high manufacturing and assembly costs. This work presents the design and proof-of-concept validation of a monolithic, fully compliant, bistable, laparoscopic grasper that eliminates the need for multiple rigid links, thereby reducing part count. The device integrates a compliant trigger and a compliant gripper end-effector, coupled via a control push-rod, to achieve stable grasping without continuous user input. The trigger mechanism is synthesized using a Two-Element Beam Constraint Model as a design framework to control the deformation and stiffness of V-beam-like elements. This technique enables elastic energy storage while preventing snap-through instability. The end-effector is designed as a compliant gripper to achieve adaptive grasping through elastic deformation. Jaws' opening-and-closing performance is demonstrated using nonlinear finite element analysis. The laparoscopic design presented here is fabricated using fused deposition 3D printing. The fabricated prototype demonstrates reliable bistable actuation, confirming the feasibility of such compliant laparoscopic grasper architectures.

</details>


### [139] [Benchmarking Visual Feature Representations for LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry Under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18589)
*Eunseon Choi,Junwoo Hong,Daehan Lee,Sanghyun Park,Hyunyoung Jo,Sunyoung Kim,Changho Kang,Seongsam Kim,Yonghan Jung,Jungwook Park,Seul Koo,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了FAST-LIVO2框架，提出了一种结合直接光度法和基于描述符特征匹配的混合方法，用于提升自动驾驶在视觉挑战环境下的定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉里程计方法在低光照、过曝、光照变化和高视差等视觉挑战环境下性能显著下降，影响机器人导航的鲁棒性。虽然已有LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计(LIVO)框架整合多传感器来解决这些问题，但仍需改进视觉状态估计的可靠性。

Method: 扩展FAST-LIVO2框架，提出混合方法：结合直接光度法和基于描述符的特征匹配。评估了四种描述符匹配配置：ORB+汉明距离、SuperPoint+SuperGlue、SuperPoint+LightGlue、XFeat+互最近邻。从精度、计算成本和特征跟踪稳定性三个维度进行基准测试。

Result: 混合方法优于传统的稀疏直接法。稀疏直接法在光照变化导致光度不一致的区域经常无法收敛，而混合方法在相同条件下仍保持鲁棒性能。基于学习描述符的混合方法能够在挑战性环境中实现鲁棒可靠的视觉状态估计。

Conclusion: 提出的混合方法通过整合直接光度法和描述符特征匹配，显著提升了视觉里程计在视觉挑战环境下的性能，为自动驾驶定位提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。学习型描述符在复杂环境中表现出色，为实际应用提供了可靠的技术基础。

Abstract: Accurate localization in autonomous driving is critical for successful missions including environmental mapping and survivor searches. In visually challenging environments, including low-light conditions, overexposure, illumination changes, and high parallax, the performance of conventional visual odometry methods significantly degrade undermining robust robotic navigation. Researchers have recently proposed LiDAR-inertial-visual odometry (LIVO) frameworks, that integrate LiDAR, IMU, and camera sensors, to address these challenges. This paper extends the FAST-LIVO2-based framework by introducing a hybrid approach that integrates direct photometric methods with descriptor-based feature matching. For the descriptor-based feature matching, this work proposes pairs of ORB with the Hamming distance, SuperPoint with SuperGlue, SuperPoint with LightGlue, and XFeat with the mutual nearest neighbor. The proposed configurations are benchmarked by accuracy, computational cost, and feature tracking stability, enabling a quantitative comparison of the adaptability and applicability of visual descriptors. The experimental results reveal that the proposed hybrid approach outperforms the conventional sparse-direct method. Although the sparse-direct method often fails to converge in regions where photometric inconsistency arises due to illumination changes, the proposed approach still maintains robust performance under the same conditions. Furthermore, the hybrid approach with learning-based descriptors enables robust and reliable visual state estimation across challenging environments.

</details>


### [140] [Introducing M: A Modular, Modifiable Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19134)
*Victor Nikhil Antony,Zhili Gong,Yoonjae Kim,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M是一个开源、低成本的社会机器人平台，旨在通过使机器人更易于复制、修改和在实际环境中部署来减少社会机器人研究中的平台摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人研究中存在平台摩擦问题，包括机器人难以复制、修改和在实际环境中部署，这阻碍了研究的进展和可重复性。

Method: 采用模块化机械设计、多模态感知和表达性但机械简单的驱动架构，结合ROS2原生软件包，清晰分离感知、表达控制和数据管理。平台包含与硬件接口等效的仿真环境，支持交互行为的快速从仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 展示了通过额外感知/驱动模块的可扩展性，提供了讲故事和双向对话辅导的交互模板示例。报告了在参与式设计和为期一周的家庭部署中的实际应用。

Conclusion: M可以作为纵向、可重复社会机器人研究的实用基础平台，通过降低平台摩擦来加速研究进展。

Abstract: We present M, an open-source, low-cost social robot platform designed to reduce platform friction that slows social robotics research by making robots easier to reproduce, modify, and deploy in real-world settings. M combines a modular mechanical design, multimodal sensing, and expressive yet mechanically simple actuation architecture with a ROS2-native software package that cleanly separates perception, expression control, and data management. The platform includes a simulation environment with interface equivalence to hardware to support rapid sim-to-real transfer of interaction behaviors. We demonstrate extensibility through additional sensing/actuation modules and provide example interaction templates for storytelling and two-way conversational coaching. Finally, we report real-world use in participatory design and week-long in-home deployments, showing how M can serve as a practical foundation for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.

</details>


### [141] [REST: Receding Horizon Explorative Steiner Tree for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18624)
*Shuqi Xiao,Maani Ghaffari,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REST提出了一种基于路径树的零样本目标导航框架，通过构建探索性斯坦纳树作为选项空间，利用LLM进行粗到细的推理，在保持高成功率的同时实现了优秀的路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航的层次化方法主要关注场景理解和高级决策，但忽视了选项（子目标候选）的设计。传统方法将选项简化为孤立的路径点独立评分，这隐藏了沿途的信息增益价值，且无结构的候选集合模糊了候选之间的关系。

Method: REST框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从在线RGB-D流构建显式的开放词汇3D地图；(2) 通过基于采样的规划构建以智能体为中心的、安全和信息丰富的路径树作为选项空间；(3) 将每个分支文本化为空间叙事，通过思维链LLM推理选择最佳路径。

Result: 在Gibson、HM3D和HSSD基准测试中，REST在成功率方面始终排名前列，同时实现了最佳或次佳的路径效率，展示了良好的效率-成功率平衡。

Conclusion: 将选项空间设计为路径树而非孤立路径点，能够暴露沿途信息增益并支持粗到细的LLM推理，从而在零样本目标导航任务中实现更高效和成功的性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) requires navigating unknown environments to find a target object without task-specific training. Prior hierarchical training-free solutions invest in scene understanding (\textit{belief}) and high-level decision-making (\textit{policy}), yet overlook the design of \textit{option}, i.e., a subgoal candidate proposed from evolving belief and presented to policy for selection. In practice, options are reduced to isolated waypoints scored independently: single destinations hide the value gathered along the journey; an unstructured collection obscures the relationships among candidates. Our insight is that the option space should be a \textit{tree of paths}. Full paths expose en-route information gain that destination-only scoring systematically neglects; a tree of shared segments enables coarse-to-fine LLM reasoning that dismisses or pursues entire branches before examining individual leaves, compressing the combinatorial path space into an efficient hierarchy. We instantiate this insight in \textbf{REST} (Receding Horizon Explorative Steiner Tree), a training-free framework that (1) builds an explicit open-vocabulary 3D map from online RGB-D streams; (2) grows an agent-centric tree of safe and informative paths as the option space via sampling-based planning; and (3) textualizes each branch into a spatial narrative and selects the next-best path through chain-of-thought LLM reasoning. Across the Gibson, HM3D, and HSSD benchmarks, REST consistently ranks among the top methods in success rate while achieving the best or second-best path efficiency, demonstrating a favorable efficiency-success balance.

</details>


### [142] [CSSDF-Net: Safe Motion Planning Based on Neural Implicit Representations of Configuration Space Distance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18669)
*Haohua Chen,Yixuan Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CSSDF-Net，学习配置空间连续符号距离场，为高维机械臂在非结构化环境中提供联合空间距离和梯度查询，实现零样本泛化的安全运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何碰撞检测器通常不可微分，而基于工作空间的隐式距离模型受限于高度非线性映射且收敛性差，同时自碰撞和环境碰撞常被分开处理，需要统一的几何安全概念

Method: 提出CSSDF-Net学习配置空间连续符号距离场，引入基于空间哈希的数据生成管道编码机器人中心几何先验，支持任意障碍点集的风险配置高效检索，集成到安全约束轨迹优化和滚动时域MPC中

Result: 在平面臂和7自由度机械臂上实验显示稳定梯度，在静态和动态场景中有效避碰，大规模点云查询具有实用推理延迟，支持在未见环境中部署

Conclusion: CSSDF-Net通过配置空间距离场提供统一安全几何概念，实现零样本泛化，为高维机械臂在非结构化环境中的安全运动生成提供有效解决方案

Abstract: High-dimensional manipulator operation in unstructured environments requires a differentiable, scene-agnostic distance query mechanism to guide safe motion generation. Existing geometric collision checkers are typically non-differentiable, while workspace-based implicit distance models are hindered by the highly nonlinear workspace--configuration mapping and often suffer from poor convergence; moreover, self-collision and environment collision are commonly handled as separate constraints. We propose Configuration-Space Signed Distance Field-Net (CSSDF-Net), which learns a continuous signed distance field directly in configuration space to provide joint-space distance and gradient queries under a unified geometric notion of safety. To enable zero-shot generalization without environment-specific retraining, we introduce a spatial-hashing-based data generation pipeline that encodes robot-centric geometric priors and supports efficient retrieval of risk configurations for arbitrary obstacle point sets. The learned distance field is integrated into safety-constrained trajectory optimization and receding-horizon MPC, enabling both offline planning and online reactive avoidance. Experiments on a planar arm and a 7-DoF manipulator demonstrate stable gradients, effective collision avoidance in static and dynamic scenes, and practical inference latency for large-scale point-cloud queries, supporting deployment in previously unseen environments.

</details>


### [143] [Empathetic Motion Generation for Humanoid Educational Robots via Reasoning-Guided Vision--Language--Motion Diffusion Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18771)
*Fuze Sun,Lingyu Li,Lekan Dai,Xinyu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RG-VLMD：一个推理引导的视觉-语言-运动扩散框架，用于生成教育场景中人形机器人的指令感知协同语音手势，通过多模态情感估计和教学推理实现自适应、语义一致的机器人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在教育人机交互中，需要生成与教学内容和情感状态一致的协同语音手势，以增强机器人的教学表达力和适应性。现有方法缺乏对教学意图和情感状态的深入推理，导致手势生成不够结构化且缺乏教学相关性。

Method: 1. 多模态情感估计：使用门控专家混合模型从文本、视觉和声学特征预测效价/唤醒度；2. 教学推理：通过情感驱动策略将情感信号映射到离散的教学行为类别；3. 运动生成：使用扩散模型，通过剪辑级意图和帧级教学调度进行条件化，采用加性潜在限制和辅助动作组监督。

Result: 相比基线扩散模型，RG-VLMD生成更具结构化和区分性的运动模式，经运动统计和成对距离分析验证。生成的运动序列保持物理合理性，可重定向到NAO机器人进行实时执行。推理引导的教学条件化提高了手势可控性和教学表达力。

Conclusion: RG-VLMD框架通过整合多模态情感估计、教学推理和教学行为条件化运动合成，显著提升了教育人机交互中机器人手势生成的适应性、语义一致性和教学表达力，为教育机器人提供了更自然有效的交互能力。

Abstract: This article suggests a reasoning-guided vision-language-motion diffusion framework (RG-VLMD) for generating instruction-aware co-speech gestures for humanoid robots in educational scenarios. The system integrates multi-modal affective estimation, pedagogical reasoning, and teaching-act-conditioned motion synthesis to enable adaptive and semantically consistent robot behavior. A gated mixture-of-experts model predicts Valence/Arousal from input text, visual, and acoustic features, which then mapped to discrete teaching-act categories through an affect-driven policy.These signals condition a diffusion-based motion generator using clip-level intent and frame-level instructional schedules via additive latent restriction with auxiliary action-group supervision. Compared to a baseline diffusion model, our proposed method produces more structured and distinctive motion patterns, as verified by motion statics and pairwise distance analysis. Generated motion sequences remain physically plausible and can be retargeted to a NAO robot for real-time execution. The results reveal that reasoning-guided instructional conditioning improves gesture controllability and pedagogical expressiveness in educational human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [144] [ViTac-Tracing: Visual-Tactile Imitation Learning of Deformable Object Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18784)
*Yongqiang Zhao,Haining Luo,Yupeng Wang,Emmanouil Spyrakos Papastavridis,Yiannis Demiris,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉-触觉模仿学习的可变形物体追踪方法，使用统一模型处理一维和二维可变形物体，通过局部加权损失和全局任务损失提升性能，在低成本遥操作系统中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体追踪方法存在局限性：要么缺乏跨不同类别物体的泛化能力，要么难以在真实世界中可靠完成任务。这主要是由于需要特定物体建模或仿真到现实的迁移困难。

Method: 提出视觉-触觉模仿学习方法，从局部和全局两个角度设计：局部引入加权损失强调在触觉图像中心附近保持接触的动作；全局提出追踪任务损失帮助策略调节任务进度。硬件上，在低成本遥操作系统中集成触觉感知以补偿视觉信息提取的有限特征。

Result: 在多样的一维和二维可变形物体上进行广泛消融和对比实验，平均成功率在已见物体上达到80%，在未见物体上达到65%，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过视觉-触觉融合和精心设计的损失函数，实现了对可变形物体的有效追踪，具有较好的泛化能力，能够处理不同类别的可变形物体。

Abstract: Deformable objects often appear in unstructured configurations. Tracing deformable objects helps bringing them into extended states and facilitating the downstream manipulation tasks. Due to the requirements for object-specific modeling or sim-to-real transfer, existing tracing methods either lack generalizability across different categories of deformable objects or struggle to complete tasks reliably in the real world. To address this, we propose a novel visual-tactile imitation learning method to achieve one-dimensional (1D) and two-dimensional (2D) deformable object tracing with a unified model. Our method is designed from both local and global perspectives based on visual and tactile sensing. Locally, we introduce a weighted loss that emphasizes actions maintaining contact near the center of the tactile image, improving fine-grained adjustment. Globally, we propose a tracing task loss that helps the policy to regulate task progression. On the hardware side, to compensate for the limited features extracted from visual information, we integrate tactile sensing into a low-cost teleoperation system considering both the teleoperator and the robot. Extensive ablation and comparative experiments on diverse 1D and 2D deformable objects demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving an average success rate of 80% on seen objects and 65% on unseen objects.

</details>


### [145] ["You've got a friend in me": Co-Designing a Peer Social Robot for Young Newcomers' Language and Cultural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18804)
*Neil Fernandes,Cheng Tang,Tehniyat Shahbaz,Alex Hauschildt,Emily Davies-Robinson,Yue Hu,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员与加拿大United for Literacy的导师合作设计了Maple——一款桌面式、同伴型社交辅助机器人，用于在导师指导的会话中作为新移民儿童的英语和文化学习练习伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大社区扫盲项目面临人员有限和一对一辅导时间稀缺的问题，这限制了为新移民儿童提供个性化的英语和文化学习支持。需要创新的解决方案来弥补这些资源限制。

Method: 采用协同设计研究方法：1) 通过影子观察和协同设计访谈了解需求；2) 设计集成原型Maple，包含短篇故事活动、多模态支架（语音、面部反馈、手势）和嵌入式测验；3) 系统支持注意力维持并产生导师可操作的形成性信号。

Result: 开发了Maple原型系统，满足新移民儿童特定需求，支持语言社会化。提出了导师在环社交辅助机器人在社区环境中的系统设计启示，并规划了在真实项目中以儿童为中心的评价方向。

Conclusion: Maple作为导师指导下的练习伙伴，通过故事活动、多模态支架和嵌入式测验，为社区扫盲项目提供了可行的补充支持方案，为类似情境下的社交辅助机器人设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Community literacy programs supporting young newcomer children in Canada face limited staffing and scarce one-to-one time, which constrains personalized English and cultural learning support. This paper reports on a co-design study with United for Literacy tutors that informed Maple, a table-top, peer-like Socially Assistive Robot (SAR) designed as a practice partner within tutor-mediated sessions. From shadowing and co-design interviews, we derived newcomer-specific requirements and added them in an integrated prototype that uses short story-based activities, multi-modal scaffolding (speech, facial feedback, gesture), and embedded quizzes that support attention while producing tutor-actionable formative signals. We contribute system design implications for tutor-in-the-loop SARs supporting language socialization in community settings and outline directions for child-centered evaluation in authentic programs.

</details>


### [146] [V-Dreamer: Automating Robotic Simulation and Trajectory Synthesis via Video Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18811)
*Songjia He,Zixuan Chen,Hongyu Ding,Dian Shao,Jieqi Shi,Chenxu Li,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V-Dreamer：一个从自然语言指令自动生成开放词汇、仿真就绪的机器人操作环境和专家轨迹的框架，无需人工干预


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练通用机器人需要大规模、多样化的操作数据，但现实世界数据收集成本高昂，现有仿真器受限于固定资产库和手动启发式方法

Method: 使用大语言模型和3D生成模型构建物理基础的3D场景，通过几何约束验证确保稳定无碰撞布局；利用视频生成模型作为运动先验，通过Sim-to-Gen视觉-运动学对齐模块将视觉预测映射为可执行机器人轨迹

Result: 在桌面操作任务上的广泛评估表明，基于合成轨迹训练的模仿学习策略能稳健地泛化到仿真中的未见物体，并实现有效的仿真到现实迁移，成功操作真实世界的新物体

Conclusion: V-Dreamer提供了一个完全自动化的框架，能够生成具有高视觉多样性和物理保真度的机器人操作数据，支持开放词汇指令，无需手动干预，为机器人学习提供了可扩展的数据生成解决方案

Abstract: Training generalist robots demands large-scale, diverse manipulation data, yet real-world collection is prohibitively expensive, and existing simulators are often constrained by fixed asset libraries and manual heuristics. To bridge this gap, we present V-Dreamer, a fully automated framework that generates open-vocabulary, simulation-ready manipulation environments and executable expert trajectories directly from natural language instructions. V-Dreamer employs a novel generative pipeline that constructs physically grounded 3D scenes using large language models and 3D generative models, validated by geometric constraints to ensure stable, collision-free layouts. Crucially, for behavior synthesis, we leverage video generation models as rich motion priors. These visual predictions are then mapped into executable robot trajectories via a robust Sim-to-Gen visual-kinematic alignment module utilizing CoTracker3 and VGGT. This pipeline supports high visual diversity and physical fidelity without manual intervention. To evaluate the generated data, we train imitation learning policies on synthesized trajectories encompassing diverse object and environment variations. Extensive evaluations on tabletop manipulation tasks using the Piper robotic arm demonstrate that our policies robustly generalize to unseen objects in simulation and achieve effective sim-to-real transfer, successfully manipulating novel real-world objects.

</details>


### [147] [PRIOR: Perceptive Learning for Humanoid Locomotion with Reference Gait Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18979)
*Chenxi Han,Shilu He,Yi Cheng,Linqi Ye,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIOR是一个高效可复现的人形机器人运动框架，通过参数化步态生成器、基于GRU的地形估计器和地形自适应脚步奖励，实现了在复杂地形上的稳健自然步态行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练感知型人形机器人运动策略面临挑战，通常需要多阶段训练流程、对抗性目标或大量真实世界校准。需要一种更简单高效的方法来实现复杂地形上的自然步态行走。

Method: 1. 参数化步态生成器：提供源自运动捕捉的稳定参考轨迹，无需对抗训练；2. GRU状态估计器：通过自监督高度图重建从自我中心深度图像推断地形几何；3. 地形自适应脚步奖励：引导脚步放置到可穿越区域；4. 系统分析深度图像分辨率权衡，在实时约束下最大化地形保真度。

Result: 在包括楼梯、箱子和间隙等不同难度地形上的综合实验表明，每个组件都带来互补且必要的性能提升，完整框架实现了100%的穿越成功率。深度图像分辨率优化显著减少了感知开销而不降低穿越性能。

Conclusion: PRIOR提供了一个简单有效的框架，通过结合参数化步态生成、地形感知和自适应脚步奖励，实现了稳健的人形机器人地形穿越。该框架将开源，为Isaac Lab上的人形机器人运动研究提供可复现的基础。

Abstract: Training perceptive humanoid locomotion policies that traverse complex terrains with natural gaits remains an open challenge, typically demanding multi-stage training pipelines, adversarial objectives, or extensive real-world calibration. We present PRIOR, an efficient and reproducible framework built on Isaac Lab that achieves robust terrain traversal with human-like gaits through a simple yet effective design: (i) a parametric gait generator that supplies stable reference trajectories derived from motion capture without adversarial training, (ii) a GRU-based state estimator that infers terrain geometry directly from egocentric depth images via self-supervised heightmap reconstruction, and (iii) terrain-adaptive footstep rewards that guide foot placement toward traversable regions. Through systematic analysis of depth image resolution trade-offs, we identify configurations that maximize terrain fidelity under real-time constraints, substantially reducing perceptual overhead without degrading traversal performance. Comprehensive experiments across terrains of varying difficulty-including stairs, boxes, and gaps-demonstrate that each component yields complementary and essential performance gains, with the full framework achieving a 100% traversal success rate. We will open-source the complete PRIOR framework, including the training pipeline, parametric gait generator, and evaluation benchmarks, to serve as a reproducible foundation for humanoid locomotion research on Isaac Lab.

</details>


### [148] [MERGE: Guided Vision-Language Models for Multi-Actor Event Reasoning and Grounding in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18988)
*Joerg Deigmoeller,Nakul Agarwal,Stephan Hasler,Daniel Tanneberg,Anna Belardinelli,Reza Ghoddoosian,Chao Wang,Felix Ocker,Fan Zhang,Behzad Dariush,Michael Gienger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MERGE系统通过视觉语言模型与感知管道的集成，实现动态人机群体交互中参与者、对象和事件的情境化定位，提高效率并减少延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人机群体交互需要一致的情境感知，但现有方法缺乏多参与者协作的合适基准，且逐帧视觉语言模型处理成本高、延迟大、输出碎片化。

Method: MERGE系统整合视觉语言模型与感知管道：轻量级流式模块持续处理视觉输入检测变化，仅在必要时调用VLM；通过唯一识别物理实例并构建参与者-动作-对象关系，确保跨交互的时间一致性。

Result: 在GROUND数据集上，MERGE将平均定位分数提高2倍（相比GPT-4o、GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash等VLM基线），同时运行时减少4倍。

Conclusion: MERGE通过解耦设计在保持VLM推理能力和零样本泛化性的同时显著提升效率，为解决多参与者协作的情境定位问题提供了有效方案，并贡献了GROUND基准数据集。

Abstract: We introduce MERGE, a system for situational grounding of actors, objects, and events in dynamic human-robot group interactions. Effective collaboration in such settings requires consistent situational awareness, built on persistent representations of people and objects and an episodic abstraction of events. MERGE achieves this by uniquely identifying physical instances of actors (humans or robots) and objects and structuring them into actor-action-object relations, ensuring temporal consistency across interactions. Central to MERGE is the integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) guided with a perception pipeline: a lightweight streaming module continuously processes visual input to detect changes and selectively invokes the VLM only when necessary. This decoupled design preserves the reasoning power and zero-shot generalization of VLMs while improving efficiency, avoiding both the high monetary cost and the latency of frame-by-frame captioning that leads to fragmented and delayed outputs. To address the absence of suitable benchmarks for multi-actor collaboration, we introduce the GROUND dataset, which offers fine-grained situational annotations of multi-person and human-robot interactions. On this dataset, our approach improves the average grounding score by a factor of 2 compared to the performance of VLM-only baselines - including GPT-4o, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash - while also reducing run-time by a factor of 4. The code and data are available at www.github.com/HRI-EU/merge.

</details>


### [149] [ATG-MoE: Autoregressive trajectory generation with mixture-of-experts for assembly skill learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19029)
*Weihang Huang,Chaoran Zhang,Xiaoxin Deng,Hao Zhou,Zhaobo Xu,Shubo Cui,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATG-MoE：一种用于装配技能学习的端到端自回归轨迹生成方法，通过混合专家架构实现多模态输入到操作轨迹的闭环映射，支持多技能统一学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程劳动密集且不灵活，现有基于学习的装配方法存在位置泛化能力弱、多阶段设计复杂、多技能集成能力有限等问题，需要更灵活、泛化能力强的装配技能学习方法。

Method: 提出ATG-MoE方法，建立从RGB-D观测、自然语言指令和机器人本体感知等多模态输入到操作轨迹的闭环映射。采用多模态特征融合进行场景和任务理解，自回归序列建模生成时序一致的轨迹，混合专家架构实现统一的多技能学习。

Result: 在减压阀装配任务的八个代表性装配技能上评估，仿真中平均抓取成功率96.3%，平均总体成功率91.8%，表现出强大的泛化能力和有效的多技能集成。真实世界实验进一步验证了其多技能工业装配的实用性。

Conclusion: ATG-MoE通过端到端自回归轨迹生成和混合专家架构，成功解决了传统装配方法的局限性，实现了强大的位置泛化、时序一致性和多技能集成，为灵活制造提供了有效的机器人装配解决方案。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires robot systems that can adapt to constantly changing tasks, objects, and environments. However, traditional robot programming is labor-intensive and inflexible, while existing learning-based assembly methods often suffer from weak positional generalization, complex multi-stage designs, and limited multi-skill integration capability. To address these issues, this paper proposes ATG-MoE, an end-to-end autoregressive trajectory generation method with mixture of experts for assembly skill learning from demonstration. The proposed method establishes a closed-loop mapping from multi-modal inputs, including RGB-D observations, natural language instructions, and robot proprioception to manipulation trajectories. It integrates multi-modal feature fusion for scene and task understanding, autoregressive sequence modeling for temporally coherent trajectory generation, and a mixture-of-experts architecture for unified multi-skill learning. In contrast to conventional methods that separate visual perception and control or train different skills independently, ATG-MoE directly incorporates visual information into trajectory generation and supports efficient multi-skill integration within a single model. We train and evaluate the proposed method on eight representative assembly skills from a pressure-reducing valve assembly task. Experimental results show that ATG-MoE achieves strong overall performance in simulation, with an average grasp success rate of 96.3% and an average overall success rate of 91.8%, while also demonstrating strong generalization and effective multi-skill integration. Real-world experiments further verify its practicality for multi-skill industrial assembly. The project page can be found at https://hwh23.github.io/ATG-MoE

</details>


### [150] [Fire as a Service: Augmenting Robot Simulators with Thermally and Visually Accurate Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19063)
*Anton R. Wagner,Madhan Balaji Rao,Helge Wrede,Sören Pirk,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FaaS是一个异步协同仿真框架，为现有机器人模拟器添加高保真火灾模拟，解决传统模拟器忽视热学和光学复杂现象的问题，支持消防机器人能力评估和训练数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模拟器主要关注刚体动力学和逼真渲染，但忽视了真实火灾环境中的热学和光学复杂现象。这限制了消防机器人的可靠能力评估和部署前代表性训练数据的生成。

Method: 提出Fire as a Service (FaaS)异步协同仿真框架，将高保真且计算高效的火灾模拟集成到现有机器人模拟器中。该框架支持准确的多组分热力学热传递和视觉一致的体积烟雾，同时不干扰高频刚体控制循环。

Result: FaaS能够与多种机器人模拟器集成，生成物理准确的火灾行为，评估机器人平台面临的热危害，并收集真实的多模态感知数据。其实时性能支持人在环遥操作，通过行为克隆成功训练反应性多模态策略。

Conclusion: 通过为机器人模拟添加火灾动力学，FaaS为在火灾场景中更安全、更可靠的机器人部署提供了可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Most existing robot simulators prioritize rigid-body dynamics and photorealistic rendering, but largely neglect the thermally and optically complex phenomena that characterize real-world fire environments. For robots envisioned as future firefighters, this limitation hinders both reliable capability evaluation and the generation of representative training data prior to deployment in hazardous scenarios. To address these challenges, we introduce Fire as a Service (FaaS), a novel, asynchronous co-simulation framework that augments existing robot simulators with high-fidelity and computationally efficient fire simulations. Our pipeline enables robots to experience accurate, multi-species thermodynamic heat transfer and visually consistent volumetric smoke without disrupting high-frequency rigid-body control loops. We demonstrate that our framework can be integrated with diverse robot simulators to generate physically accurate fire behavior, benchmark thermal hazards encountered by robotic platforms, and collect realistic multimodal perceptual data. Crucially, its real-time performance supports human-in-the-loop teleoperation, enabling the successful training of reactive, multimodal policies via Behavioral Cloning. By adding fire dynamics to robot simulations, FaaS provides a scalable pathway toward safer, more reliable deployment of robots in fire scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [CAMO: A Conditional Neural Solver for the Multi-objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19074)
*Fengxiaoxiao Li,Xiao Mao,Mingfeng Fan,Yifeng Zhang,Yi Li,Tanishq Duhan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAMO是一个用于多目标多旅行商问题的条件神经求解器，通过条件编码器融合偏好向量，协作解码器协调多智能体，能够泛化到不同目标数、智能体数和偏好向量，生成高质量的帕累托前沿近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法主要关注单智能体TSP或多目标TSP变体，很少同时处理多智能体协调和多目标权衡这两个复杂挑战。多目标多旅行商问题需要团队机器人集体访问多个目标并优化竞争性目标（如总旅行成本和完工时间），现有方法难以应对这种双重复杂性。

Method: CAMO包含条件编码器将偏好向量融入实例表示，实现多目标权衡的显式控制；协作解码器通过交替智能体选择和节点选择自回归构建多智能体路径。使用基于REINFORCE的目标在混合问题规模分布上进行训练以提高泛化能力。

Result: CAMO在广泛实验中优于神经和传统启发式方法，实现了更接近帕累托前沿的近似解。消融实验验证了CAMO关键组件的贡献，移动机器人平台上的实际测试证明了其实际应用性。

Conclusion: CAMO成功解决了多目标多旅行商问题的双重复杂性挑战，通过条件神经架构实现了多智能体协调和多目标权衡的有效处理，具有良好的泛化能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robotic systems often require a team of robots to collectively visit multiple targets while optimizing competing objectives, such as total travel cost and makespan. This setting can be formulated as the Multi-Objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem (MOMTSP). Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance on the single-agent TSP and multi-objective TSP variants, they rarely address the combined challenges of multi-agent coordination and multi-objective trade-offs, which introduce dual sources of complexity. To bridge this gap, we propose CAMO, a conditional neural solver for MOMTSP that generalizes across varying numbers of targets, agents, and preference vectors, and yields high-quality approximations to the Pareto front (PF). Specifically, CAMO consists of a conditional encoder to fuse preferences into instance representations, enabling explicit control over multi-objective trade-offs, and a collaborative decoder that coordinates all agents by alternating agent selection and node selection to construct multi-agent tours autoregressively. To further improve generalization, we train CAMO with a REINFORCE-based objective over a mixed distribution of problem sizes. Extensive experiments show that CAMO outperforms both neural and conventional heuristics, achieving a closer approximation of PFs. In addition, ablation results validate the contributions of CAMO's key components, and real-world tests on a mobile robot platform demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [152] [Tendon-Actuated Robots with a Tapered, Flexible Polymer Backbone: Design, Fabrication, and Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19124)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Nandita Gallacher,Nicholas B. Andrews,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种3D打印、肌腱驱动的锥形连续体机器人设计，具有低成本、可定制、快速组装的特点，并建立了基于Cosserat杆理论的锥形背板运动静力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人多为单用途、高成本设计，缺乏可定制性和快速部署能力。本研究旨在开发一种低成本、可定制、快速组装的锥形连续体机器人，用于广泛的柔性检查与操作任务。

Method: 采用TPU材料3D打印制造柔性锥形背板，集成电子基座实现肌腱张力控制与传感。基于Cosserat杆理论建立牛顿方法的广义运动静力学模型，扩展现有肌腱驱动Cosserat杆公式以显式考虑空间变化的背板截面几何。通过参数化iLogic/CAD脚本实现快速几何生成与缩放。

Result: 模型验证显示厘米级形状预测精度（经杨氏模量校准后）。分析了锥角对机器人配置空间和可操作性的影响。演示了通过6自由度机械臂搭载内窥镜夹持器的遥操作抓取功能。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了从参数化设计到受控肌腱驱动的简单、快速、可重复路径，为熔融沉积成型3D打印制造的锥形肌腱驱动连续体机器人提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, modeling, and fabrication of 3D-printed, tendon-actuated continuum robots featuring a flexible, tapered backbone constructed from thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). Our scalable design incorporates an integrated electronics base housing that enables direct tendon tension control and sensing via actuators and compression load cells. Unlike many continuum robots that are single-purpose and costly, the proposed design prioritizes customizability, rapid assembly, and low cost while enabling high curvature and enhanced distal compliance through geometric tapering, thereby supporting a broad range of compliant robotic inspection and manipulation tasks. We develop a generalized forward kinetostatic model of the tapered backbone based on Cosserat rod theory using a Newtonian approach, extending existing tendon-actuated Cosserat rod formulations to explicitly account for spatially varying backbone cross-sectional geometry. The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.

</details>


### [153] [Meanings and Measurements: Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19166)
*Swagat Padhan,Lakshya Jain,Bhavya Minesh Shah,Omkar Patil,Thao Nguyen,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAPG框架解决机器人理解包含度量约束的自然语言指令问题，通过多智能体概率分解与组合实现3D空间中的语义-度量联合推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在语义理解方面表现良好，但缺乏对物理空间中度量约束（如距离、位置）的显式推理能力，导致处理复杂语义-度量混合查询时效果不佳

Method: 提出MAPG（多智能体概率接地）框架：将语言查询分解为结构化子组件，使用VLM对每个组件进行接地，然后概率性地组合这些接地结果以生成3D空间中度量一致的可执行决策

Result: 在HM-EQA基准测试中表现优于现有基线；提出新基准MAPG-Bench专门评估语义-度量目标接地；真实机器人演示显示MAPG可迁移到实际场景

Conclusion: MAPG框架有效解决了复杂语义-度量语言查询的接地问题，填补了现有语言接地评估的空白，为机器人理解包含度量约束的自然语言指令提供了可行方案

Abstract: Robots collaborating with humans must convert natural language goals into actionable, physically grounded decisions. For example, executing a command such as "go two meters to the right of the fridge" requires grounding semantic references, spatial relations, and metric constraints within a 3D scene. While recent vision language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong semantic grounding capabilities, they are not explicitly designed to reason about metric constraints in physically defined spaces. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLM-based grounding approaches struggle with complex metric-semantic language queries. To address this limitation, we propose MAPG (Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding), an agentic framework that decomposes language queries into structured subcomponents and queries a VLM to ground each component. MAPG then probabilistically composes these grounded outputs to produce metrically consistent, actionable decisions in 3D space. We evaluate MAPG on the HM-EQA benchmark and show consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark, MAPG-Bench, specifically designed to evaluate metric-semantic goal grounding, addressing a gap in existing language grounding evaluations. We also present a real-world robot demonstration showing that MAPG transfers beyond simulation when a structured scene representation is available.

</details>


### [154] [ADMM-Based Distributed MPC with Control Barrier Functions for Safe Multi-Robot Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19170)
*Yicheng Zeng,Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Basit Muhammad Imran,Jeeseop Kim,Vittorio Pastore,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多机器人腿式系统安全关键轨迹规划的完全分散式模型预测控制框架，结合控制屏障函数约束，通过ADMM分布式优化实现并行计算，在四足机器人上验证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人腿式系统需要安全关键的轨迹规划，但集中式MPC计算负担重且难以扩展。控制屏障函数约束引入了显式的智能体间耦合，使得最优控制问题无法直接分解，需要开发能够保持安全性和动态可行性的完全分散式解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出完全分散式MPC框架，结合CBF约束确保安全性；2) 采用基于ADMM的结构化分布式优化框架，通过新颖的节点-边分割公式和共识约束，将全局问题分解为独立的节点局部和边局部二次规划；3) 集成到四足机器人分层控制架构：高层分布式轨迹规划、中层非线性MPC（单刚体动力学）、低层全身控制（全阶机器人动力学）。

Result: 1) 在两个Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行硬件实验，在粗糙地形和外部干扰的不确定环境中验证；2) 数值模拟涉及最多四个机器人；3) 分布式公式性能与集中式MPC相当，在四智能体情况下每周期规划时间平均减少51%，实现高效实时分散实施。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式安全关键MPC框架成功实现了多机器人腿式系统的完全分散式轨迹规划，通过ADMM分解保持了安全性和动态可行性，显著降低了计算时间，使实时分散实施成为可能，为复杂环境中多机器人协作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully decentralized model predictive control (MPC) framework with control barrier function (CBF) constraints for safety-critical trajectory planning in multi-robot legged systems. The incorporation of CBF constraints introduces explicit inter-agent coupling, which prevents direct decomposition of the resulting optimal control problems. To address this challenge, we reformulate the centralized safety-critical MPC problem using a structured distributed optimization framework based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM).
