By introducing a novel node-edge splitting formulation with consensus constraints, the proposed approach decomposes the global problem into independent node-local and edge-local quadratic programs that can be solved in parallel using only neighbor-to-neighbor communication. This enables fully decentralized trajectory optimization with symmetric computational load across agents while preserving safety and dynamic feasibility. The proposed framework is integrated into a hierarchical locomotion control architecture for quadrupedal robots, combining high-level distributed trajectory planning, mid-level nonlinear MPC enforcing single rigid body dynamics, and low-level whole-body control enforcing full-order robot dynamics. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 quadrupedal robots and numerical simulations involving up to four robots navigating uncertain environments with rough terrain and external disturbances. The results show that the proposed distributed formulation achieves performance comparable to centralized MPC while reducing the average per-cycle planning time by up to 51% in the four-agent case, enabling efficient real-time decentralized implementation.

</details>


### [155] [Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Interpretable and Steerable Features in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19183)
*Aiden Swann,Lachlain McGranahan,Hugo Buurmeijer,Monroe Kennedy,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究应用机制可解释性技术分析VLA模型，发现大多数特征对应训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性，揭示了VLA泛化能力的机制基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中表现出不一致的泛化能力：在某些场景表现优异，但在新物体、新场景和新指令上微调版本经常失败。需要理解VLA模型的内部工作机制，探究其泛化能力的机制基础。

Method: 在VLA模型的隐藏层激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs），学习稀疏字典特征作为模型计算的可解释基础。提出特征分类指标区分通用可迁移基元与特定演示记忆。在LIBERO基准上进行操控实验验证。

Result: 发现大多数SAE特征对应特定训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性。操控通用特征能诱导与其语义一致的行为，且可跨任务和场景应用。监督微调小数据集会过度放大记忆，而使用更大更多样数据集或知识隔离能促进更通用特征。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供了VLA模型能跨任务和场景学习通用特征的机制证据。稀疏自编码器特征分析揭示了VLA泛化能力的内部机制，为理解模型泛化不一致性提供了新视角，并指出了促进通用特征学习的训练策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for general-purpose robot manipulation. However, their generalization is inconsistent: while these models can perform impressively in some settings, fine-tuned variants often fail on novel objects, scenes, and instructions. We apply mechanistic interpretability techniques to better understand the inner workings of VLA models. To probe internal representations, we train Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on hidden layer activations of the VLA. SAEs learn a sparse dictionary whose features act as a compact, interpretable basis for the model's computation. We find that the large majority of extracted SAE features correspond to memorized sequences from specific training demonstrations. However, some features correspond to interpretable, general, and steerable motion primitives and semantic properties, offering a promising glimpse toward VLA generalizability. We propose a metric to categorize features according to whether they represent generalizable transferable primitives or episode-specific memorization. We validate these findings through steering experiments on the LIBERO benchmark. We show that individual SAE features causally influence robot behavior. Steering general features induces behaviors consistent with their semantic meaning and can be applied across tasks and scenes. This work provides the first mechanistic evidence that VLAs can learn generalizable features across tasks and scenes. We observe that supervised fine-tuning on small robotics datasets disproportionately amplifies memorization. In contrast, training on larger, more diverse datasets (e.g., DROID) or using knowledge insulation promotes more general features. We provide an open-source codebase and user-friendly interface for activation collection, SAE training, and feature steering. Our project page is located at http://drvla.github.io

</details>


### [156] [FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19199)
*Yuxiang Lu,Zhe Liu,Xianzhe Fan,Zhenya Yang,Jinghua Hou,Junyi Li,Kaixin Ding,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FASTER通过引入感知时域的调度策略，在流式VLA模型中优先处理近期动作，将即时反应的去噪压缩到单步完成，大幅降低反应延迟，实现前所未有的实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，但忽视了响应环境变化的关键延迟。标准流式VLA中应用恒定调度策略效率低下，要求系统完成所有采样步骤后才能开始任何动作，形成反应延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出FASTER方法，引入感知时域调度策略，在流采样过程中自适应地优先处理近期动作，将即时反应的去噪压缩十倍至单步完成，同时保持长时域轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务器流水线部署。

Result: FASTER显著降低了真实机器人上的有效反应延迟，特别是在消费级GPU上部署时。真实世界实验（包括高度动态的乒乓球任务）证明FASTER为通用策略解锁了前所未有的实时响应能力，能够快速生成准确平滑的轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过重新思考动作分块策略中的反应概念，FASTER解决了流式VLA模型中的反应延迟瓶颈，为在物理世界中部署VLA模型提供了关键的实时执行能力，特别是在动态环境中表现出色。

Abstract: Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.

</details>


### [157] [OmniVTA: Visuo-Tactile World Modeling for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19201)
*Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yujie Zang,Shuai Tian,Xiang Li,Ruihai Wu,Ce Hao,Chen Gao,Si Liu,Haoran Li,Yilun Chen,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniViTac是一个大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务和100+物体，组织为六种物理基础交互模式。基于此，OmniVTA框架通过自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型、接触感知融合策略和60Hz反射控制器，实现了接触丰富操作的预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务（如擦拭和装配）需要准确感知接触力、摩擦力变化和状态转换，这些无法仅从视觉可靠推断。现有视觉-触觉操作研究面临两个主要限制：数据集规模小、任务覆盖窄；现有方法将触觉信号视为被动观测而非用于建模接触动态或实现显式闭环控制。

Method: 提出OmniViTac大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务、100+物体，组织为六种物理基础交互模式。基于此构建OmniVTA框架，包含四个紧密耦合模块：自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型（预测短期接触演化）、接触感知融合策略（动作生成）、60Hz反射控制器（闭环校正预测与观测触觉信号的偏差）。

Result: 真实机器人实验在所有六种交互类别中显示，OmniVTA优于现有方法，并在未见过的物体和几何配置上表现出良好的泛化能力。这证实了将预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈相结合对于接触丰富操作的价值。

Conclusion: OmniViTac数据集和OmniVTA框架通过大规模数据收集和预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈的闭环集成，显著推进了视觉-触觉操作研究。该方法在接触丰富任务中表现出优越性能和良好泛化能力，为复杂操作任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation tasks, such as wiping and assembly, require accurate perception of contact forces, friction changes, and state transitions that cannot be reliably inferred from vision alone. Despite growing interest in visuo-tactile manipulation, progress is constrained by two persistent limitations: existing datasets are small in scale and narrow in task coverage, and current methods treat tactile signals as passive observations rather than using them to model contact dynamics or enable closed-loop control explicitly. In this paper, we present \textbf{OmniViTac}, a large-scale visuo-tactile-action dataset comprising $21{,}000+$ trajectories across $86$ tasks and $100+$ objects, organized into six physics-grounded interaction patterns. Building on this dataset, we propose \textbf{OmniVTA}, a world-model-based visuo-tactile manipulation framework that integrates four tightly coupled modules: a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model for predicting short-horizon contact evolution, a contact-aware fusion policy for action generation, and a 60Hz reflexive controller that corrects deviations between predicted and observed tactile signals in a closed loop. Real-robot experiments across all six interaction categories show that OmniVTA outperforms existing methods and generalizes well to unseen objects and geometric configurations, confirming the value of combining predictive contact modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback for contact-rich manipulation. All data, models, and code will be made publicly available on the project website at https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA.

</details>


### [158] [Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19233)
*Bryce Grant,Xijia Zhao,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活注入、稀疏自编码器和线性探针等方法，系统分析了6个80M-7B参数的VLA模型在4个基准测试上的行为机制，揭示了视觉通路在动作生成中的主导作用、语言敏感性的任务依赖性，以及专家通路与VLM通路的功能分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型将感知、语言和运动控制整合到单一架构中，但这些模型如何将多模态输入转化为动作的机制仍不清楚。研究者旨在深入理解VLA模型内部的工作机制，特别是视觉、语言和动作生成之间的交互关系。

Method: 研究采用激活注入、稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针等方法，对6个参数规模从80M到7B的VLA模型进行了系统分析。实验基于4个基准测试，覆盖了超过394,000个rollout episodes。通过注入基线激活到null-prompt episodes、跨任务激活注入、子空间注入等技术，探究了模型内部表示与行为的关系。

Result: 1. 视觉通路在所有架构中主导动作生成：将基线激活注入null-prompt episodes能恢复几乎相同的行为；跨任务注入使机器人趋向源任务位置（X-VLA中99.8%的episodes与源轨迹对齐）。2. 语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计：当视觉上下文唯一指定任务时，语言被忽略；当多个目标共享场景时，语言变得关键。3. 在多通路架构中，专家通路编码运动程序，而VLM通路编码目标语义。4. 稀疏自编码器处理对大多数架构的动作保真度至关重要。5. 对比识别恢复了82+个操作概念，因果消融显示敏感性范围在28-92%的零效应率。

Conclusion: VLA模型的动作生成主要由视觉通路驱动，表现出与场景坐标绑定的空间运动程序。语言的作用高度依赖于任务结构，而非模型架构本身。多通路架构中专家通路和VLM通路存在功能分离，分别负责运动程序和目标语义。研究提供了对VLA模型内部工作机制的系统性理解，并发布了Action Atlas平台供交互式探索。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine perception, language, and motor control in a single architecture, yet how they translate multimodal inputs into actions remains poorly understood. We apply activation injection, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and linear probes to six models spanning 80M--7B parameters across 394,000+ rollout episodes on four benchmarks. The visual pathway dominates action generation across all architectures: injecting baseline activations into null-prompt episodes recovers near-identical behavior, while cross-task injection steers robots toward source-task positions (99.8\% of X-VLA episodes align with the source trajectory), exposing spatially bound motor programs tied to scene coordinates rather than abstract task representations. Language sensitivity depends on task structure, not model design: when visual context uniquely specifies the task, language is ignored; when multiple goals share a scene, language becomes essential (X-VLA \texttt{libero\_goal}: 94\%$\to$10\% under wrong prompts vs.\ \texttt{libero\_object}: 60--100\% regardless). In all three multi-pathway architectures (\pizhalf{}, SmolVLA, GR00T), expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics ($2\times$ greater behavioral displacement from expert injection), and subspace injection confirms these occupy separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is essential for action fidelity on most architectures, though mean-pooling improves fidelity on X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation reveals sensitivity spanning 28--92\% zero-effect rates independent of representation width. We release \textbf{Action Atlas} (https://action-atlas.com) for interactive exploration of VLA representations across all six models.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [QuaQue: Design and SQL Implementation of Condensed Algebra for Concurrent Versioning of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18654)
*Jey Puget Gil,Emmanuel Coquery,John Samuel,Gilles Gesquière*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuaQue是ConVer-G系统的关键组件，通过将SPARQL查询转换为SQL，使用压缩代数在关系模型中高效查询多版本知识图谱


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本化知识图谱管理面临重大挑战，特别是在跨多个版本高效查询数据方面

Method: 引入QuaQue组件，采用新颖的压缩代数操作关系模型，其中版本信息使用位串紧凑存储，将SPARQL代数转换为SQL

Result: 在标准关系数据库系统中实现了对知识图谱并发版本的高效查询，通过基准测试证明了相对于原生RDF三元组存储的可行性和性能优势

Conclusion: QuaQue方法为版本化知识图谱管理提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过关系数据库系统实现了高效的跨版本查询

Abstract: The management of versioned knowledge graphs presents significant challenges, particularly in querying data across multiple versions efficiently. This paper introduces QuaQue, a key component of the ConVer-G system, which addresses this challenge by translating SPARQL (SPARQL Protocol and RDF Query Language) queries into SQL (Structured Query Language). QuaQue leverages a novel condensed algebra to operate on a relational model where versioning information is compactly stored using bitstrings. This approach allows for efficient querying of concurrent versions of knowledge graphs within a standard relational database system. We present the key concepts of our condensed algebra, detail the translation process from SPARQL algebra to SQL, and provide a comparative benchmark against a native RDF (Resource Description Framework) triple store, demonstrating the viability and performance benefits of our approach.

</details>


### [160] [Let's Play Tag: Linear Time Evaluation of Conjunctive Queries under TGD Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18709)
*Nofar Carmeli,Carsten Lutz,Marcin Przybyłko*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 研究在元组生成依赖约束下合取查询的线性时间评估极限，涵盖多种评估模式，提出方法将无约束设置下的二分性结果推广到特定约束类


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在数据库约束（元组生成依赖）下合取查询的线性时间评估复杂性，探索不同评估模式（单测试、全测试、计数、字典序直接访问、枚举）的极限，旨在将无约束设置下的已知二分性结果推广到有约束环境

Method: 提出一种方法，对于特定评估模式和TGD类，能够将无约束设置下的已知二分性结果"提升"到有约束环境。方法特别适用于非递归TGD集（头部最多使用二元关系符号或最多两个边界变量）和边界保护全TGD

Result: 方法成功应用于除枚举外的所有评估模式，当约束属于两类TGD时：1)非递归TGD集（每个TGD头部最多使用二元关系符号或最多两个边界变量）；2)边界保护全TGD。同时提供了展示枚举和限制较少TGD类挑战的示例集合

Conclusion: 虽然完全分类似乎遥不可及，但提出的方法能够将无约束设置下的二分性结果推广到特定约束类和评估模式，为理解约束下查询评估复杂性提供了重要进展，同时揭示了枚举和更一般TGD类带来的挑战

Abstract: We study the limits of linear time evaluation of conjunctive queries under constraints expressed as tuple-generating dependencies (TGDs), across several modes of query evaluation: single-testing, all-testing, counting, lexicographic direct access, and enumeration. While full classifications seem far beyond reach, we propose an approach that, for some evaluation modes and classes of TGDs, makes it possible to lift known dichotomies from the unconstrained setting. In particular, our approach applies to all mentioned evaluation modes except enumeration, when the constraints fall into one of two classes: non-recursive sets of TGDs in which every TGD uses at most binary relation symbols in the head or has at most two frontier variables; and frontier-guarded full TGDs. We further provide a collection of examples showcasing the challenges that arise for enumeration and for less restrictive classes of TGDs.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [A Trace-Based Assurance Framework for Agentic AI Orchestration: Contracts, Testing, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18096)
*Ciprian Paduraru,Petru-Liviu Bouruc,Alin Stefanescu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一个用于Agentic AI系统的保障框架，通过消息-动作追踪、契约验证、压力测试和运行时治理来确保多智能体LLM系统的可靠性和安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统中LLM在协调层使用时面临多种故障类型（非终止、角色漂移、错误传播等），需要系统化的保障机制来确保可靠性、安全性和可验证性

Method: 1) 将执行过程仪器化为消息-动作追踪(MAT)，包含显式的步骤和追踪契约；2) 压力测试作为有界扰动下的预算化反例搜索；3) 在服务、检索和内存边界进行结构化故障注入；4) 将治理作为运行时组件，实施能力限制和动作调解

Result: 定义了基于追踪的度量标准（任务成功率、终止可靠性、契约合规性、事实性指标、遏制率、治理结果分布），支持跨随机种子、模型和编排配置的比较评估

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体LLM系统提供了一个通用的抽象层，支持测试评估和跨编排设计与配置的可重复比较，有助于提高Agentic AI系统的可靠性和安全性

Abstract: In Agentic AI, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in the orchestration layer to coordinate multiple agents and to interact with external services, retrieval components, and shared memory. In this setting, failures are not limited to incorrect final outputs. They also arise from long-horizon interaction, stochastic decisions, and external side effects (such as API calls, database writes, and message sends). Common failures include non-termination, role drift, propagation of unsupported claims, and attacks via untrusted context or external channels.
  This paper presents an assurance framework for such Agentic AI systems. Executions are instrumented as Message-Action Traces (MAT) with explicit step and trace contracts. Contracts provide machine-checkable verdicts, localize the first violating step, and support deterministic replay. The framework includes stress testing, formulated as a budgeted counterexample search over bounded perturbations. It also supports structured fault injection at service, retrieval, and memory boundaries to assess containment under realistic operational faults and degraded conditions. Finally, governance is treated as a runtime component, enforcing per-agent capability limits and action mediation (allow, rewrite, block) at the language-to-action boundary.
  To support comparative evaluations across stochastic seeds, models, and orchestration configurations, the paper defines trace-based metrics for task success, termination reliability, contract compliance, factuality indicators, containment rate, and governance outcome distributions. More broadly, the framework is intended as a common abstraction to support testing and evaluation of multi-agent LLM systems, and to facilitate reproducible comparison across orchestration designs and configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Conditional Execution of Transpiler Passes Based on Per-Script Feature Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18049)
*Rishipal Singh Bhatia*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于特征集动态跟踪的JavaScript编译器优化方法，通过条件执行转译过程来减少不必要的AST遍历，显著提升编译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ECMAScript规范不断演进，工业级JavaScript编译器面临支持现代语法同时保持向后兼容的挑战。传统编译器采用基于目标语言级别的固定转译流水线，即使输入代码不包含某些语言特性，也会执行昂贵的AST遍历，造成显著的计算浪费。

Method: 提出在编译单元级别动态维护特征集的方法，通过准确跟踪并动态维护编译过程中JavaScript脚本实际包含的语言特性集合，条件性地执行转译过程。在Google Closure Compiler中实现，包括战略性的过程排序和转译代码特征正确性的动态验证等架构保障措施。

Result: 在大规模生产级monorepo上的评估显示，该改进显著减少了编译时间，并节省了计算和内存使用量。

Conclusion: 通过动态跟踪语言特征集并条件执行转译过程，可以有效优化JavaScript编译器的性能，减少不必要的计算开销，为工业级编译器提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: As the ECMAScript specification evolves, industrial-scale JavaScript compilers face the challenge of supporting modern language syntax while maintaining compatibility for diverse execution environments. Traditionally, compilers solve this by running transpilation passes in a monolithic pipeline, where the transpilation passes are chosen to execute strictly based on a target language level. This results in significant computational waste, as compilers perform expensive Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) traversals to lower features that may not exist in the actual input source code. We present a compiler improvement that conditionally executes transpiler passes based on accurately tracking and dynamically maintaining the exact set of language features present in the compilation unit throughout the transpilation process. It is implemented in the production Google Closure Compiler. By populating and maintaining a FeatureSet at every JavaScript script-level, it dynamically skips running unnecessary lowering passes. We detail the architectural safeguards -- including strategic pass ordering and dynamic validation of the transpiled code for feature-correctness. Evaluation of this improvement on large-scale production monorepos produced a considerable reduction in compilation time and saved compute and memory usage.

</details>


### [163] [TENSURE: Fuzzing Sparse Tensor Compilers (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18372)
*Kabilan Mahathevan,Yining Zhang,Muhammad Ali Gulzar,Kirshanthan Sundararajah*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: TENSURE：首个专门针对稀疏张量编译器（STC）的可扩展黑盒模糊测试框架，使用爱因斯坦求和表示法生成复杂张量收缩运算，通过约束生成算法保证100%语义有效性，在TACO和Finch编译器中发现广泛缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏张量编译器需要从高级声明式规范合成复杂、不规则的控制流，容易产生微妙正确性缺陷。现有测试框架无法充分测试这些编译器的任意循环合成能力，而通用语法模糊测试器难以生成有效输入。

Method: 提出TENSURE框架：1）使用爱因斯坦求和表示法作为通用输入抽象，生成复杂非常规张量收缩运算；2）设计新颖约束生成算法保证100%语义有效性；3）引入语义保持变异算子，利用代数交换性和存储格式异质性进行蜕变测试。

Result: 在TACO和Finch两个最先进系统上的评估显示广泛脆弱性：TENSURE在TACO中暴露了大多数生成测试用例的崩溃或静默错误编译，约束生成算法有效性显著优于基线语法模糊测试器的~3.3%成功率。

Conclusion: 稀疏编译生态系统迫切需要专门测试工具，TENSURE作为首个针对STC的模糊测试框架，成功暴露了现有编译器的严重缺陷，证明了其有效性和必要性。

Abstract: Sparse Tensor Compilers (STCs) have emerged as critical infrastructure for optimizing high-dimensional data analytics and machine learning workloads. The STCs must synthesize complex, irregular control flow for various compressed storage formats directly from high-level declarative specifications, thereby making them highly susceptible to subtle correctness defects. Existing testing frameworks, which rely on mutating computation graphs restricted to a standard vocabulary of operators, fail to exercise the arbitrary loop synthesis capabilities of these compilers. Furthermore, generic grammar-based fuzzers struggle to generate valid inputs due to the strict rules governing how indices are reused across multiple tensors.
  In this paper, we present TENSURE, the first extensible black-box fuzzing framework specifically designed for the testing of STCs. TENSURE leverages Einstein Summation (Einsum) notation as a general input abstraction, enabling the generation of complex, unconventional tensor contractions that expose corner cases in the code-generation phases of STCs. We propose a novel constraint-based generation algorithm that guarantees 100% semantic validity of synthesized kernels, significantly outperforming the ~3.3% validity rate of baseline grammar fuzzers. To enable metamorphic testing without a trusted reference, we introduce a set of semantic-preserving mutation operators that exploit algebraic commutativity and heterogeneity in storage formats. Our evaluation on two state-of-the-art systems, TACO and Finch, reveals widespread fragility, particularly in TACO, where TENSURE exposed crashes or silent miscompilations in a majority of generated test cases. These findings underscore the critical need for specialized testing tools in the sparse compilation ecosystem.

</details>


### [164] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Generalizing Peephole Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18477)
*Chunhao Liao,Hongxu Xu,Xintong Zhou,Zhenyang Xu,Chengnian Sun*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: LPG是一个使用大语言模型（LLM）来自动化窥孔优化泛化的框架，通过LLM驱动的符号常量泛化、结构泛化、约束松弛和位宽/精度泛化，结合形式化验证，显著提升了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窥孔优化是现代优化编译器的核心组件，但将具体优化实例泛化为更通用的重写规则需要大量人工努力和领域专业知识。现有自动化方法（如Hydra）存在搜索空间爆炸、泛化不足和对不同指令域支持有限等问题。

Method: LPG采用闭环工作流，整合LLM驱动的符号常量泛化、结构泛化、约束松弛和位宽/精度泛化，并通过语法验证、语义验证和收益性检查提供反馈，确保生成的规则既正确又有效。

Result: 在LLVM生态系统的真实窥孔优化问题上，LPG成功泛化了102个优化中的90个。在可与Hydra直接比较的整数优化子集上，LPG泛化了81个中的74个，而Hydra仅泛化了35个。

Conclusion: LPG框架通过结合LLM的语义抽象和探索性推理能力与形式化分析，显著提升了窥孔优化的自动化泛化能力，在真实场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Peephole optimizations are a core component of modern optimizing compilers. It rewrites specific instruction into semantically equivalent but more efficient forms. In practice, creating a new peephole optimization often starts from a concrete optimization instance and requires lifting it into a more general rewrite rule that matches a wider range of instruction patterns. This generalization step is critical to optimization effectiveness, but it is also difficult: producing rules that are both correct and sufficiently general typically demands substantial manual effort and domain expertise. Existing approaches such as Hydra attempt to automate this task with program synthesis, but their generalization capability is often limited by search-space explosion, under-generalization, and restricted support for diverse instruction domains.
  We present LPG, large language model aided peephole optimization generalization, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to generalize peephole optimizations. The design of LPG is motivated by the observation that LLMs are effective at semantic abstraction and exploratory reasoning, while formal analyses are necessary to ensure that generated rules are sound and profitable. Based on this observation, LPG adopts a closed-loop workflow that integrates LLM-driven symbolic constant generalization, structural generalization, constraint relaxation, and bitwidth/precision generalization with feedback from syntactic validation, semantic verification, and profitability checking.
  We evaluate LPG on real-world peephole optimization issues drawn from the LLVM ecosystem. Overall, LPG successfully generalizes 90 out of 102 optimizations. On the integer-focused subset that is directly comparable to Hydra, LPG generalizes 74 out of 81 optimizations, whereas Hydra generalizes 35.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [A Synthesizable RTL Implementation of Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18066)
*Timothy Oh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种直接实现预测编码学习动力学的数字硬件架构，通过本地预测误差动态实现完全分布式的在线学习，避免了反向传播的全局依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播在现代深度学习中虽然有效，但难以实现为完全分布式的在线硬件学习系统，因为它存在全局误差传播、阶段分离和对集中式内存的严重依赖等问题。预测编码提供了一种替代方案，通过相邻层之间的本地预测误差动态实现推理和学习。

Method: 设计了一个数字架构，直接在硬件中实现离散时间预测编码更新。每个神经核心维护自己的活动、预测误差和突触权重，仅通过硬连线连接与相邻层通信。通过统一的每神经元钳制原语支持监督学习和推理，该原语强制边界条件同时保持内部更新计划不变。设计基于顺序MAC数据路径和固定有限状态计划的确定性可综合RTL基板。

Result: 提出了一个完整的可综合数字基板，直接执行预测编码学习动力学，而不是在硬件中实现新的学习规则。系统在固定的本地更新规则下演化，通过连接性、参数和边界条件施加任务结构。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献不是新的学习规则，而是一个完整的可综合数字基板，直接执行预测编码学习动力学，为完全分布式在线硬件学习系统提供了可行的实现方案，克服了反向传播在硬件实现中的局限性。

Abstract: Backpropagation has enabled modern deep learning but is difficult to realize as an online, fully distributed hardware learning system due to global error propagation, phase separation, and heavy reliance on centralized memory. Predictive coding offers an alternative in which inference and learning arise from local prediction-error dynamics between adjacent layers. This paper presents a digital architecture that implements a discrete-time predictive coding update directly in hardware. Each neural core maintains its own activity, prediction error, and synaptic weights, and communicates only with adjacent layers through hardwired connections. Supervised learning and inference are supported via a uniform per-neuron clamping primitive that enforces boundary conditions while leaving the internal update schedule unchanged. The design is a deterministic, synthesizable RTL substrate built around a sequential MAC datapath and a fixed finite-state schedule. Rather than executing a task-specific instruction sequence inside the learning substrate, the system evolves under fixed local update rules, with task structure imposed through connectivity, parameters, and boundary conditions. The contribution of this work is not a new learning rule, but a complete synthesizable digital substrate that executes predictive-coding learning dynamics directly in hardware.

</details>


### [166] [Constrained Hybrid Metaheuristic: A Universal Framework for Continuous Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18295)
*Piotr A. Kowalski,Szymon Kucharczyk,Jacek Mańdziuk*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: cHM算法是一个通用的连续优化框架，通过模块化结构和两阶段操作，能够适应多种复杂目标函数，在基准测试和特征选择应用中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元启发式算法通常针对特定函数类别或问题领域设计，缺乏通用性。需要开发一个能够处理未知、异构或复杂属性（如非凸性、非可分性和变化平滑度）的通用优化框架。

Method: 提出约束混合元启发式（cHM）算法，采用模块化结构和两阶段操作。关键特点是能够利用候选解和组件元启发式策略之间的协同作用，在优化过程的不同阶段应用最合适的搜索行为。

Result: 在28个基准函数上的实验评估表明，cHM在解质量和收敛速度方面一致匹配或优于传统元启发式算法。在数据分类的特征选择问题中的实际应用也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: cHM是一个通用且有效的黑盒优化器，适用于理论研究和实际应用，具有动态适应问题特性的能力，能够处理广泛的复杂优化问题。

Abstract: This paper presents the constrained Hybrid Metaheuristic (cHM) algorithm as a general framework for continuous optimisation. Unlike many existing metaheuristics that are tailored to specific function classes or problem domains, cHM is designed to operate across a broad spectrum of objective functions, including those with unknown, heterogeneous, or complex properties such as non-convexity, non-separability, and varying smoothness. We provide a formal description of the algorithm, highlighting its modular structure and two-phase operation, which facilitates dynamic adaptation to the problem's characteristics. A key feature of cHM is its ability to harness synergy between both candidate solutions and component metaheuristic strategies. This property allows the algorithm to apply the most appropriate search behaviour at each stage of the optimisation process, thereby improving convergence and robustness. Our extensive experimental evaluation on 28 benchmark functions demonstrates that cHM consistently matches or outperforms traditional metaheuristics in terms of solution quality and convergence speed. In addition, a practical application of the algorithm is demonstrated for a feature selection problem in the context of data classification. The results underscore its potential as a versatile and effective black-box optimiser suitable for both theoretical research and practical applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [AutORAN: LLM-driven Natural Language Programming for Agile xApp Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18604)
*Xin Li,Shiming Yu,Leming Shen,Jianing Zhang,Yuanqing Zheng,Yaxiong Xie*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: AutORAN是一个基于大语言模型的自然语言编程框架，能够将高级用户意图在几分钟内转化为可快速部署的xApps，无需手动编码或测试，显著加速O-RAN应用开发周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAN系统封闭且单一，阻碍创新。O-RAN虽然通过xApps实现了开放性和可编程性，但xApp开发仍然耗时且繁琐，通常需要数月的手动编码和集成，这阻碍了新功能在实际中的快速部署。

Method: AutORAN构建了一个完全自动化的xApp生成流水线，集成了多个功能模块：从用户需求获取、AI/ML功能设计与验证，到xApp合成和部署。这是一个LLM驱动的自然语言编程框架，将高级用户意图转化为可部署的xApps。

Result: 在代表性xApp任务上的评估显示，AutORAN生成的xApps能够达到甚至优于已知最佳手工编写基线的性能。AutORAN大幅加速了xApp开发周期（从用户意图获取到部署）。

Conclusion: AutORAN是首个LLM驱动的自然语言编程框架，能够将高级用户意图在几分钟内转化为可部署的xApps，无需手动编码或测试，显著降低了xApp开发门槛，促进了O-RAN创新。

Abstract: Traditional RAN systems are closed and monolithic, stifling innovation. The openness and programmability enabled by Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) are envisioned to revolutionize cellular networks with control-plane applications--xApps. The development of xApps (typically by third-party developers), however, remains time-consuming and cumbersome, often requiring months of manual coding and integration, which hinders the roll-out of new functionalities in practice. To lower the barrier of xApp development for both developers and network operators, we present AutORAN, the first LLM-driven natural language programming framework for agile xApps that automates the entire xApp development pipeline. In a nutshell, AutORAN turns high-level user intents into swiftly deployable xApps within minutes, eliminating the need for manual coding or testing. To this end, AutORAN builds a fully automated xApp generation pipeline, which integrates multiple functional modules (from user requirement elicitation, AI/ML function design and validation, to xApp synthesis and deployment). We design, implement, and comprehensively evaluate AutORAN on representative xApp tasks. Results show AutORAN-generated xApps can achieve similar or even better performance than the best known hand-crafted baselines. AutORAN drastically accelerates the xApp development cycle (from user intent elicitation to roll-out), streamlining O-RAN innovation.

</details>


### [168] [Masking Intent, Sustaining Equilibrium: Risk-Aware Potential Game-empowered Two-Stage Mobile Crowdsensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18670)
*Houyi Qi,Minghui Liwang,Kaiwen Tan,Wenyong Wang,Sai Zou,Yiguang Hong,Xianbin Wang,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: iParts是一个意图保护的风险可控两阶段移动群智感知服务框架，通过本地差分隐私保护工人意图，使用博弈论方法进行离线预规划和在线动态修复，平衡隐私、效用和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动群智感知在复杂应用中面临多重挑战：1) 工人意图/偏好轨迹可能被诚实但好奇的平台利用进行意图推断；2) 工人退出和执行不确定性导致覆盖不稳定和冗余感知；3) 重复全局在线重新优化带来高交互开销并扩大可观测攻击面。

Method: 提出iParts两阶段框架：离线阶段，工人通过个性化本地差分隐私报告扰动意图向量，平台基于扰动意图构建冗余感知质量模型，在预算、个体理性、质量失败风险和意图不匹配风险约束下进行风险感知预规划，建模为精确势博弈。在线阶段，当运行时动态导致质量赤字时，通过空闲/待机工人的临时招募势博弈进行轻量级修复。

Result: 实验表明iParts实现了良好的隐私-效用-效率权衡，相比代表性基线方法提高了社会福利和任务完成率，同时减少了冗余和通信开销。

Conclusion: iParts框架有效解决了动态移动群智感知中的隐私保护、风险控制和效率问题，通过两阶段博弈论方法在保护工人意图隐私的同时实现了可靠的服务提供。

Abstract: Beyond data collection, future mobile crowdsensing (MCS) in complex applications must satisfy diverse requirements, including reliable task completion, budget and quality constraints, and fluctuating worker availability. Besides raw-data and location privacy, workers' intent/preference traces can be exploited by an honest-but-curious platform, enabling intent inference from repeated observations and frequency profiling. Meanwhile, worker dropouts and execution uncertainty may cause coverage instability and redundant sensing, while repeated global online re-optimization incurs high interaction overhead and enlarges the observable attack surface. To address these issues, we propose iParts, an intent-preserving and risk-controllable two-stage service provisioning framework for dynamic MCS. In the offline stage, workers report perturbed intent vectors via personalized local differential privacy with memorization/permanent randomization, suppressing frequency-based inference while preserving decision utility. Using only perturbed intents, the platform builds a redundancy-aware quality model and performs risk-aware pre-planning under budget, individual rationality, quality-failure risk, and intent-mismatch risk constraints. We formulate offline pre-planning as an exact potential game with expected social welfare as the potential function, ensuring a constrained pure-strategy Nash equilibrium and finite-step convergence under asynchronous feasible improvements. In the online stage, when runtime dynamics cause quality deficits, a temporary-recruitment potential game over idle/standby workers enables lightweight remediation with bounded interaction rounds and low observability. Experiments show that iParts achieves a favorable privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off, improving welfare and task completion while reducing redundancy and communication overhead compared with representative baselines.

</details>
